　P19-3 (flip shift law; preferred) \| T3 \| H4 (first-order γ1 shallowing); common threshold 0 \< s\_m \< σ₁ \< σ₂, m\* \> 0, 0 \< ρ\_γ \< 1, no parameter change between the two strengths (same ρ\_γ and s\_m) \| Discrete: k₊(σ) = ⌈ln(σ/s\_m)/ln(1/(1 − ρ\_γ))⌉, k₊(σ₂) − k₊(σ₁) ∈ {⌊L⌋, ⌈L⌉}, L = ln(σ₂/σ₁)/ln(1/(1 − ρ\_γ)); continuous (common exponential shallowing, common τ\_γ1 and s\_m): t\_flip(σ₂) − t\_flip(σ₁) = τ\_γ1·ln(σ₂/σ₁), so doubling σ delays the flip by τ\_γ1·ln 2 \| Manipulation: two training strengths (σ₁, σ₂ estimated from the reduction of the MVP2 response at the end of training); read-out: the MVP2 recovery time course (fitting τ\_γ1, or ρ\_γ and s\_m) and the flip delays of the two groups; the sampling interval must be shorter than the duration corresponding to L, otherwise only “the delay does not decrease” is tested \| Breach: the difference of the delays falls outside {⌊L⌋, ⌈L⌉} (in continuous time, outside τ\_γ1·ln(σ₂/σ₁) ± error); or, when L exceeds the sum of one beat and the measurement error, the two groups still have equal delays. When L is less than one beat or the measurement error, equal delays are consistent with this claim and are not a breach (Example X12: ρ\_γ = 3/4, s\_m = 1/4; σ = 1/2 and 1 both at beat 1) \| C-clk predicts a flip time independent of σ (fixed clock); C-nor has no flip \| \[verified Q9b\] 292 cases; \[verified Q11g, Q11h\] Example X12 and the full grid of the rounding law.

The blockade effect “blocking the cerebellar-nuclei → inferior-olive inhibition abolishes the equilibrium” holds only under a frozen single-feedback bearing; the finite verification covers only the complete return, the blockade version has not been constructed, and neither the equilibrium concept of the original model (joint regulation by background activity and LTD/LTP) nor possible alternative feedbacks have been compared, so the blockade item is not among the signed new predictions (U19-9) \| Only a structural inference, not entered in the *Drosophila* certificate \| — \| \[verified Q7b\] reduced member, CF → 1/4 = η\_p/η.  
10.1  New Phenomena Derived by the Theory  
　(1) Early-test interference: an intermediate test changes long-term memory, with a direction determined by the test time relative to the flip interval; the magnitude in readings passes through the difference propagation Σ\_s W\_s∏\_{j\>s} r\_j (P19-5).  
　(2) Flip shift law: the stronger the training, the later unpaired CS+ flips from strengthening to weakening; the discrete first-arrival difference ∈ {⌊L⌋, ⌈L⌉}, and in continuous time the delay is τ\_γ1·ln(σ₂/σ₁) (P19-3).  
　(3) Cross-constraint of a shared threshold: the dose–response zero point, the bracketing interval of the delay scan and the zero point of the optogenetically driven write are all determined by the same set of parameters (P19-2, P19-15).  
　(4) α3 self-limitation: under a common γ state with both stages off plateau, the deeper the existing long-term memory, the smaller the same-beat strengthening by early re-exposure (P19-7).  
　(5) Criterion for continued writing by disinhibition: positive net component η·DAN(s) \> ρ·s, with strict net deepening further requiring s \< hi (P19-8); γ1pedc does not satisfy it.  
　(6) Nonmonotonicity of long-term memory in training strength (under H6′ and the setting-set antecedents; P19-12): crossing the tag gate can give a jump up and crossing the DAN upper limit a jump down; read with frozen depth pairs spanning a boundary, it discriminates, under the comparability antecedents, the permissive type from the excitatory-return type of the consolidation window.  
　(7) Odor-specific linearity in overlap: under equal depth, equal unit activation and the linear regime, the absolute drop in α3 drive is linear in the number of shared KCs (P19-6).  
10.2  Preferred Experiments (Ranked by Discriminating Power)  
　★1  Joint dose–delay experiment (P19-2, including P19-1): the same threshold m\* must both be the dose–response zero point and fall within the bracketing interval of the delay scan (m\_γ1 taken as the pre-beat value of the re-exposure beat); the experiment simultaneously discriminates graded (line), binary (step), no-return (slope 0) and alternative-route (slope persisting under blockade); the CS+ presentations that read MVP2 and α3 are counted in the common history of the two arms. Highest discriminating power.  
　★2  Early-test interference experiment (P19-5): requires only group assignment and a single test, with memory read at 24 h; the prediction has a direction and a sign-change interval, which must intersect the bracketing interval of ★1; the magnitude follows the difference-propagation formula; no difference is a breach only at times where the lower bound of the predicted effect exceeds the detection and control error; the “testing is non-invasive” reading and the no-return model both predict no difference.  
　★3  Flip shift law (P19-3): two levels of training strength and the MVP2 recovery time course; the shift L must exceed the sum of one beat and the measurement error; discriminates “the γ1 Trace-Response determines the flip” from “a clock determines the flip.”  
　★4  DAN activity of the trained group versus the stimulus-matched sham-trained group in the consolidation window, and in-window blockade (P19-10 + P19-11): with the common-window statistic frozen in advance, independently calibrated g, e, u\_min and the working regions of both stages, discriminate by Theorem 19-D among the permissive type, inhibitory return and excitatory return; in-window blockade tests history control versus clock control under the three antecedents.  
　★5  Odor specificity graded by overlap (P19-6): discriminates synaptic Trace-Responses from uniform-gain intrinsic MBON excitability (C-fir), and adds a synaptic-layer read-out to test H1 directly.

### 10.3  Three-Layer Registration

　Theoretical conditions: Claims T1–T10 and the theorems they rely on (19-2, 19-4, 19-7, 19-7′, 19-7″, 19-10, 19-11, 19-12, 19-H, 19-5, 19-6, Proposition 19-3).  
　Testable predictions: P19-1–P19-16, preferred ★1–★5.  
　Empirical certification: not yet obtained (Column C ⊥); the route to it is the preferred experiments and animal-by-animal raw data.  
11\. Three-Column Master Table  
　χ\_γα3 \| A: Fld = 1 (Fly(full) of \[17\], Φ\_γα3 of 𝒦\_B, members of 𝒩₃F; witnessed by the activity-mediated branch) \| B: this theory claims Fld = 1, with historical dynamics borne by activity-mediated writes across compartments (T10; the sufficient derivation invokes all conditions of Theorem 19-H(2)); predictions P19-1–P19-7, P19-10–P19-15 \| C: ⊥ (empirical certification not yet obtained: ⊥\_Obs, ⊥\_Chain(C2, C3′), ⊥\_Act).  
　χ\_γ \| A: Fld = 1 (direct-read branch; no activity-mediated branch in the insufficient-disinhibition regime, Φ\_γ) \| B: this theory claims that γ1pedc does not continue writing (T4(A)), P19-8 \| C: ⊥\_Obs.  
　χ\_α \| A: Fld = 1 (Type I has a self-limiting activity-mediated branch; Type II has only teaching writes and direct reads) \| B: this theory claims self-limitation and history-controlled consolidation (T4(C), T6), P19-9–P19-12 \| C: ⊥ (empirical certification not yet obtained).  
　χ\_ext \| A: the parallel opposing member of Theorem 19-12 \| B: T7, P19-13 \| C: ⊥.  
　Consolidation-window reading (Discrimination Form) \| A: Theorem 19-D closes on 𝔇₁₉, and the outputs of the three candidates are pairwise separated (m \> 2e) \| B: the preferred claim of this theory is P (T6); reading follows the frozen protocol of P19-10 \| C: ⊥ (experiments and calibrations do not yet exist, U19-11).  
　Cerebellar PF→PC \| A: the reduced member of Cereb has SED\_W but not SED⁺ \| B: the reduced consequence of T9 (P19-16) \| C: not issued.  
　How to read: Column A verdicts judge only the declared models; the claims in Column B can be breached under the premise groups listed in Section 10; ⊥ in Column C means only that empirical certification has not yet been obtained, and it neither downgrades the theoretical statements nor makes them “undetermined.”

12\. Domain of Validity  
　(1) Discrimination Theorem 19-D applies only to the frozen working domain 𝔇₁₉: a single compartment, depression orientation, background and window shared by both arms, candidates P and R\_u with \|u\| ≥ u\_min, and antecedents D1–D4 independently checked. When the in-window DAN passes through multistage returns, multi-compartment convergence or a non-monotone read-out, separate candidates and forward maps must be set up; these do not follow from this theorem.  
　(2) The conditional mechanism theorems apply only to their declared classes: 𝒩₃F has discrete beats, determinism, truncated-linear read-outs and monotone write laws; 𝒦\_B has binary coarse beats; the consolidation state of Consol is a binary law-layer component. Continuous time and stochastic read-outs are outside the domain (U19-4).  
　(3) Real objects: the γ1pedc ∪ α3 chain of aversive memory in the *Drosophila* mushroom body is the main instance; appetitive α1 and the γ1pedc return (F7, F8, F9) are dependence references and are not transplanted as the same window for PPL1-α3. The vertebrate cerebellum and basal ganglia serve only as structural comparisons (4.7.4, U19-9). The nematode cases and mammalian STDP serve only as structural analogies for the Trace-Response–carrier correspondence (4.1.2).  
　(4) All parameters are constructed values; quantitative predictions for the real object hold only under the antecedents and read-out assumptions listed in Section 10, with parameters estimated independently in the same preparation.

13\. Open Items  
　U19-1  Empirical tests of H1–H3: the α3 synaptic layer (P19-6), the PPL1-α3 → α3 write law (P19-15), the same-trial reading relation and the reading budget r₁, ζ, C6 (P19-4).  
　U19-2  Empirical tests of Claim T6: history control versus clock control (P19-11), permissive versus return (P19-10, P19-12); the molecules and location of the consolidation state.  
　U19-3  Timing dependence within a single stimulus slot: members constructed with the residue r of \[18\] and fine beats have not yet been given.  
　U19-4  Duty adjudication under continuous time and stochastic firing (U9 of \[S11\], U13-19 of \[S13\], HC^c of \[17\]): a general upstream problem.  
　U19-5  How to choose, on the real object, the minimal setting subset and the joint setting family of Proposition 19-3 (overlap probe family, positions that can be set back, and their legitimacy).  
　U19-6  The run results of the model accompanying F11 (an external floating-point model, whose error must be declared) and the correspondence of its parameter labels with their meaning in the original; the equation-by-equation correspondence between the accompanying model and the signed members of 𝒩₃F; the results of an animal-by-animal recomputation, under the declared criteria, of the per-figure Source Data of the original and the traces of Zenodo record 10.5281/zenodo.10998457.  
　U19-7  Empirical test of Claim T7: no spontaneous recovery after late weakening (P19-13).  
　U19-8  Evidence for, and representation of, the formation and elimination of new synapses in *Drosophila* long-term memory (the existence variable of D18-12 of \[18\]).  
　U19-9  𝒩₃F membership and transfer conditions for the cerebellum and basal ganglia; the blockade version of P19-16 (frozen single feedback, the equilibrium concept and alternative feedbacks).  
　U19-10  Evidence on χ\_γα3 for the antecedent of the within-boundary self-feedback verdict (no out-of-boundary read returning to T).  
　U19-11  Real-object applicability and experimental calibration (antecedents of the experimental reading): the common-window statistic of P19-10, the unit map a, c, the three working regions (MBON model, DAN model, sensor), g, u\_min, e and its coverage, and the item-by-item mechanism budget of e\_o; the implementation of synchronous reading of m\_γ1 at the re-exposure beat for P19-1 and P19-2, or the dynamic map from the early reading to that beat and its propagation error; the sign calibration of read-out assumption RO1 (behavioral PI and synaptic depth) and the rest law of difference propagation; the absolute lower bounds on late-reading effects given in advance for P19-5 and P19-14, together with detection thresholds and control errors; the three antecedents of P19-11 and the training × blockade bypass budget; for P19-12, the frozen depth pairs spanning the target boundary, ρ₀, ρ₁ and the late-reading margin.

14\. Interfaces  
　To *The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields* v1.0: the clauses used are limited to the definitions and theorems listed in Section 2.1; the antecedent N1′ of Theorem 19-H is an additional restriction of the sufficiency theorem of this paper and does not change upstream Definition 6′.  
　To *The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation* v1.0: the clauses used are limited to the clauses with antecedents listed in its scope of inheritance; Theorem 19-4 is the instance on 𝒩₃F of its Theorem 18-5(3); law-layer consolidation uses its D18-11, Theorem 18-8 and the FLYC model.  
　To *The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics* v1.0: dynamics types are registered by the vocabulary of its Section 5 (4.7.1); the format of the Discrimination Form is shared within the same group and the same form kind; the criteria for its hosts are not transplanted.  
　To *The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification* v1.0: Theorems 19-4, 19-5 and 19-6 give the protection conditions for depression-type Trace-Responses under inhibitory and excitatory return, available as a conditional reading for its U14-16.  
　To *The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization* v1.0: Column C verdicts must be declared by the measurement mode and channels of that paper; the verdicts of this paper are limited to the model layer and the prediction layer.  
　Later experiments: the protocols of Section 10 and the calibration tasks of U19-11 are the test interface for the predictions of this paper; when any prediction is breached, the corresponding premise set is excluded according to Section 8.

## Appendix A  Program Source

Runtime environment: Python 3.10 or later, standard library only, with exact rational arithmetic from fractions. The following 10 files are placed in the same directory: A.1–A.6 are frozen programs from the appendices of the upstream main texts (byte-for-byte identical to the upstream files, with SHA-256 equal to the values listed upstream), A.7–A.9 are the libraries of this paper, and A.10 is the run entry; the run entry reads only the Appendix A files in the same directory (to print and check their SHA-256) and no other files. The programs give finite verification and exact witnesses; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.  
A.1  jhc\_core.py  Upstream frozen program: Appendix A.1 of *The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields* v1.0 (three-way split, depth, the organization hypergraph, Definition 6′ certificates and the two-branch report)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields v1.0: core library.
Python standard library only; all numerical values are fractions.Fraction. Host-model interface: X (positions), lo/hi (clipping bounds), ground (trace-free ground state),
orient (declared orientation), init, grid (gridding), act(w, s) (activity), comps(w, y, s) (list of components (process, position, value)).
  branches          two-branch report: A branch (activity swapped only) and D branch (direct read changed after the activity swap); A + D = total difference (recomputed under the unclamped mechanism).
  branches_rev      telescoping decomposition in the other order (direct read changed first, then activity swapped).
  hc_total          difference reading: some legitimate setting makes the total component difference > ε (not restricted to available positions; for contrast only).
  avail_at          bearing positions already above threshold in the pre-beat state (the numerical part of Definition 6′(B)).
  hc_status         the three values of the effective-difference certificate: effective difference certified / functional support established / effective difference not certified.
  hdyn_cert         Act and effective difference on the same beat and the same process (model mode of Definitions 6′ and 7′).
  sed               activity-mediated positive component (optional strengthening ledger); with plus, SED⁺.
  net_deep          actual net deepening (a separate ledger).
  trace_candidates  screening of Trace-Response candidates (oriented depth > ε and < −ε listed separately; not an element certificate).
  retention         self-retention: observed lower bound and complete verdict kept apart; a complete verdict requires explicit tail evidence (Definition 2′).
  triage            three-way verdict from the lower bound, completeness and the declared τ_act, τ_W; ⊥_Tail only when there is just a lower bound and it falls short of τ_W.
  org_graph_exact   exact enumeration over all declared joint settings (hyperedges included).
  org_graph_sample  bounded sampling: reports only witness edges and coverage gaps.
  basins            basins of attraction of a map on a finite chain (each basin is the set of points whose limit is that fixed point).
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from itertools import combinations
import math
ZERO = Fr(0)
FRACS = (Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2))

def floor_grid(x, G):
    return Fr(math.floor(x * G), G)

class Host:
    name = 'host'
    grid = None
    def step(self, w, s):
        y = self.act(w, s)
        new = dict(w)
        for (_p, x, c) in self.comps(w, y, s):
            new[x] = new[x] + c
        for x in self.X:
            v = min(self.hi[x], max(self.lo[x], new[x]))
            if self.grid is not None:
                v = floor_grid(v, self.grid)
            new[x] = v
        return new

def run(host, w0, hist):
    out, w = [dict(w0)], dict(w0)
    for s in hist:
        w = host.step(w, s)
        out.append(w)
    return out

def ref_hist(host, hist):
    return ['rest' for _ in hist]

def depth(host, w, x):
    """Oriented depth (signed): a positive value is a Trace-Response in the declared orientation; a negative value is the other type, beyond the ground state."""
    return host.orient[x] * (w[x] - host.ground[x])

def trace_candidates(host, hist, eps):
    """Candidate screening (not an element certificate). Returns (trajectory, reference trajectory, positive candidates, reverse candidates, signal-formed positions)."""
    S = run(host, host.init, hist)
    R = run(host, host.init, ref_hist(host, hist))
    w, wr = S[-1], R[-1]
    pos = [x for x in host.X if depth(host, w, x) > eps]
    neg = [x for x in host.X if depth(host, w, x) < -eps]
    sig = [x for x in host.X if abs(w[x] - wr[x]) > eps]
    return S, R, pos, neg, sig

def retention(seq, eps, tail=None):
    """Self-retention (Definition 2′). Returns (lower bound, verdict); lower bound = 1 + the last t in the record with |u| > eps (0 if no point crosses the threshold).
    The verdict is 'complete' if and only if tail evidence is given and checked, and 'lower bound' otherwise, regardless of whether the last record point is above threshold:
    a last point below threshold does not rule out a later crossing during the ensuing rest. tail is one of:
      ('coverage',)                    the record already covers the whole remaining rest domain (declared; the function does not certify it);
      ('invariant set', step, band)    band lists all points of the subthreshold set (|u| ≤ eps), step is the one-step rest map;
                                       requires the last record point to lie in band and step(band) ⊆ band (checked exactly, point by point);
      ('envelope', C, lam)             |u(t_end + s)| ≤ C·lam^s (s ≥ 0) is proved, 0 ≤ lam < 1; requires C ≤ eps."""
    last = 0
    for t, v in enumerate(seq):
        if abs(v) > eps:
            last = t + 1
    if tail is None or not seq:
        return last, 'lower bound'
    kind = tail[0]
    ok = False
    if kind == 'coverage':
        ok = True
    elif kind == 'invariant set':
        step, band = tail[1], set(tail[2])
        ok = (seq[-1] in band and all(abs(u) <= eps for u in band)
              and all(step(u) in band for u in band))
    elif kind == 'envelope':
        C, lam = tail[1], tail[2]
        ok = 0 <= lam < 1 and C <= eps and abs(seq[-1]) <= C
    return last, ('complete' if ok else 'lower bound')

def triage(lb, status, tau_act, tau_W):
    """Three-way verdict for a carrier that can be written (Definition 2′): lower bound ≥ τ_W gives a Trace-Response carrier; activity and activity residue require complete self-retention;
    when there is only a lower bound and it is < τ_W, the verdict is ⊥_Tail (no verdict of activity, residue or non-Trace-Response is issued)."""
    if lb >= tau_W:
        return 'Trace-Response carrier'
    if status != 'complete':
        return '⊥_Tail'
    return 'activity' if lb <= tau_act else 'activity residue'

def setv(host, w, vals):
    v = dict(w)
    for x, val in vals.items():
        v[x] = min(host.hi[x], max(host.lo[x], val))
    return v

def setT(host, w, T, frac=Fr(0)):
    return setv(host, w, {x: host.ground[x] + frac * (w[x] - host.ground[x]) for x in T})

def comp_of(host, w, y, s, proc, x):
    tot = ZERO
    for (p, xx, c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
        if p == proc and xx == x:
            tot += c
    return tot

def total_comp(host, w, s, proc, x):
    return comp_of(host, w, host.act(w, s), s, proc, x)

def branches(host, w, w2, s, proc, x):
    """Two-branch report (the control w2 is the state after a legitimate setting). A = c(direct read of w, y(w2)) − c(direct read of w, y(w));
    D = c(direct read of w2, y(w2)) − c(direct read of w, y(w2)); A + D = total difference = c(w2) − c(w) (recomputed under the unclamped mechanism)."""
    y, y2 = host.act(w, s), host.act(w2, s)
    c0 = comp_of(host, w, y, s, proc, x)
    cA = comp_of(host, w, y2, s, proc, x)
    c2 = comp_of(host, w2, y2, s, proc, x)
    return cA - c0, c2 - cA, c2 - c0

def settings(host, w, T, fracs=FRACS, joint=True):
    """Declared family of legitimate settings: every nonempty subset of T (joint=True), or each single position and the whole of T (joint=False), times the depth multiples."""
    T = list(T)
    subs = []
    if joint:
        for k in range(1, len(T) + 1):
            subs += [list(c) for c in combinations(T, k)]
    else:
        subs = [[x] for x in T] + ([T] if len(T) > 1 else [])
    out = []
    for U in subs:
        for f in fracs:
            w2 = setT(host, w, U, f)
            if w2 != w:
                out.append((tuple(U), f, w2))
    return out

def hc_total(host, w, s, proc, x, T, eps, fracs=FRACS, joint=True):
    """Difference reading of the history condition: some legitimate setting makes the total component difference > eps. Returns (U, f, A, D, total difference) or None."""
    for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, T, fracs, joint):
        A, D, tot = branches(host, w, w2, s, proc, x)
        if abs(tot) > eps:
            return (U, f, A, D, tot)
    return None

def part(host, w, T, probes, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    for s in probes:
        y0 = host.act(w, s)
        for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, T, fracs, joint=False):
            y1 = host.act(w2, s)
            for j in y0:
                if abs(y0[j] - y1[j]) > eps:
                    return (s, j, y0[j], y1[j])
    return None

def hdyn(host, states, sigs, T, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """For contrast: at some beat in the window, the component of process p on x ∈ T is effective, and the total component difference is effective under some legitimate setting of T (not restricted to available positions)."""
    for k, s in enumerate(sigs):
        w = states[k]
        y = host.act(w, s)
        seen = set()
        for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T or (p, x) in seen:
                continue
            seen.add((p, x))
            c = comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if abs(c) <= eps:
                continue
            h = hc_total(host, w, s, p, x, T, eps, fracs)
            if h:
                return (k, s, p, x, c) + h
    return None

def sed(host, reach, T, eps, plus=False, fracs=FRACS):
    """Activity-mediated positive component (optional strengthening ledger): (A) the oriented component is > eps; (B) under the same setting, the total difference is effective and the A branch is effective.
    plus: additionally requires that setting the whole of T shallower by each frac < 1 makes the oriented component strictly smaller (self-reinforcement ledger; not a universal claim over all subsets)."""
    for (w, s) in reach:
        y = host.act(w, s)
        for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T:
                continue
            c = comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if host.orient[x] * c <= eps:
                continue
            ok = False
            for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, T, fracs):
                A, D, tot = branches(host, w, w2, s, p, x)
                if abs(tot) > eps and abs(A) > eps:
                    ok = True
                    break
            if not ok:
                continue
            if not plus:
                return (s, p, x, c)
            good = True
            for f_ in [f_ for f_ in fracs if f_ < 1]:
                w2 = setT(host, w, T, f_)
                if not host.orient[x] * total_comp(host, w2, s, p, x) < host.orient[x] * c:
                    good = False
                    break
            if good:
                return (s, p, x, c)
    return None

def net_deep(host, w, protocol, T, eps):
    """Actual net deepening: applying the protocol increases the oriented depth of some position in T by > eps. Returns [(x, before, after)]."""
    w2 = dict(w)
    for s in protocol:
        w2 = host.step(w2, s)
    return [(x, depth(host, w, x), depth(host, w2, x)) for x in T if depth(host, w2, x) - depth(host, w, x) > eps]

def reach_pairs(host, hists):
    out = []
    for h in hists:
        S = run(host, host.init, h)
        for k, s in enumerate(h):
            out.append((S[k], s))
    return out

class UF:
    def __init__(self, items):
        self.p = {i: i for i in items}
    def f(self, i):
        while self.p[i] != i:
            self.p[i] = self.p[self.p[i]]
            i = self.p[i]
        return i
    def u(self, a, b):
        ra, rb = self.f(a), self.f(b)
        if ra != rb:
            self.p[rb] = ra

def _act_dep(host, w, w2, s, eps):
    y0, y1 = host.act(w, s), host.act(w2, s)
    return [j for j in y0 if abs(y0[j] - y1[j]) > eps]

def org_graph_exact(host, w, E, probes, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """Exact enumeration: finds dependences over all nonempty subsets of E × depth multiples (the declared family of joint settings).
    Co-participation hyperedge: a minimal subset U that changes activity component j (a hyperedge when |U| ≥ 2; when |U| = 1, merged into the single-position dependence group of the same j).
    History-condition hyperedge: a minimal subset U that changes the total component acting on x, together with x. Returns (list of hyperedges, connected components)."""
    E = list(E)
    hyper = set()
    for s in probes:
        y0 = host.act(w, s)
        changed = {}
        for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, E, fracs, joint=True):
            for j in _act_dep(host, w, w2, s, eps):
                changed.setdefault(('act', j), set()).add(U)
            for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y0, s):
                if x not in E:
                    continue
                tot = total_comp(host, w2, s, p, x) - comp_of(host, w, y0, s, p, x)
                if abs(tot) > eps:
                    changed.setdefault(('w', p, x), set()).add(U)
        for key, Us in changed.items():
            mins = [U for U in Us if not any(set(V) < set(U) for V in Us)]
            if key[0] == 'act':
                singles = sorted({U[0] for U in mins if len(U) == 1})
                if len(singles) >= 2:
                    hyper.add(('co-participation', key[1], tuple(singles)))
                for U in mins:
                    if len(U) >= 2:
                        hyper.add(('co-participation', key[1], tuple(sorted(U))))
            else:
                x = key[2]
                for U in mins:
                    nodes = tuple(sorted(set(U) | {x}))
                    if len(nodes) >= 2:
                        hyper.add(('history condition', key[1] + '@' + x, nodes))
    uf = UF(E)
    for (_k, _n, nodes) in hyper:
        for a in nodes[1:]:
            uf.u(nodes[0], a)
    comps = {}
    for i in E:
        comps.setdefault(uf.f(i), []).append(i)
    return sorted(hyper), sorted([sorted(v) for v in comps.values()])

def org_graph_sample(host, w, E, probes, eps, tested, fracs=FRACS):
    """Bounded sampling: settings are made only on tested (a list of subsets). Returns (witness hyperedges, coverage gaps: position pairs not jointly covered by any tested subset; being covered does not mean being identified)."""
    hyper = set()
    for s in probes:
        y0 = host.act(w, s)
        for U in tested:
            for f in fracs:
                w2 = setT(host, w, U, f)
                if w2 == w:
                    continue
                for j in _act_dep(host, w, w2, s, eps):
                    hyper.add(('co-participation', j, tuple(sorted(U))))
                for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y0, s):
                    if x in E and abs(total_comp(host, w2, s, p, x) - comp_of(host, w, y0, s, p, x)) > eps:
                        hyper.add(('history condition', p + '@' + x, tuple(sorted(set(U) | {x}))))
    covered = set()
    for U in tested:
        for a in U:
            for b in U:
                covered.add((a, b))
    unknown = [(a, b) for a, b in combinations(sorted(E), 2) if (a, b) not in covered]
    return sorted(hyper), unknown

def basins(F, chain):
    """F (a dict) is a map on chain, a finite totally ordered chain. Returns {fixed point: [its basin of attraction (the points whose limit is that point)]}."""
    out = {}
    for d in chain:
        x, seen = d, set()
        while F[x] != x:
            if x in seen:
                x = None
                break
            seen.add(x)
            x = F[x]
        out.setdefault(x, []).append(d)
    return out

def order_rel(a1, a2):
    return (a1 > a2) - (a1 < a2)

def order_conflicts(pairs):
    """Returns the point pairs whose order relations under q and e disagree (including equality mismatches in both directions)."""
    bad = []
    for i in range(len(pairs)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(pairs)):
            (q1, e1), (q2, e2) = pairs[i], pairs[j]
            if order_rel(q1, q2) != order_rel(e1, e2):
                bad.append((pairs[i], pairs[j]))
    return bad

def fstr(x):
    if isinstance(x, Fr):
        return str(x.numerator) if x.denominator == 1 else '%d/%d' % (x.numerator, x.denominator)
    return str(x)

def avail_at(host, states, k, T, eps, k_form=None):
    """Available bearing positions at beat k: the positions in T whose oriented depth is > eps in the pre-beat state states[k] of beat k.
    states[k] results from the writes of the earlier beats, so its threshold crossing means the trace was left before beat k; if the crossing dates from states[0] (above threshold at the start),
    its formation needs a separate record (the counterpart of ENT in [S3]), which this function does not certify; here the program verifies only that the trace was numerically “already left”.
    Window-end membership in T is not used for this judgment."""
    return [x for x in T if depth(host, states[k], x) > eps]

def hc_status(host, w, s, proc, x, U_av, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """Three values of the history condition on one beat and one process (over the family of legitimate settings that change only the available positions U_av, joint settings included):
    'effective difference certified': some setting makes the total component difference > eps; 'functional support established': some setting makes the total component difference ≠ 0 but none makes it > eps;
    'effective difference not certified': the total component difference is identically 0 over the tested setting family (this is only a reading within a finite setting family, not a complete negation)."""
    best = None
    nonzero = False
    for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, U_av, fracs, joint=True):
        A, D, tot = branches(host, w, w2, s, proc, x)
        if abs(tot) > eps:
            return 'effective difference certified', (U, f, A, D, tot)
        if tot != 0:
            nonzero = True
            best = (U, f, A, D, tot)
    if nonzero:
        return 'functional support established', best
    return 'effective difference not certified', None

def hdyn_cert(host, states, sigs, T, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """Act and history difference on the same beat and the same process: Act = the total component c of that process on x ∈ T at that beat has |c| > eps;
    the settings change only the available bearing positions of that beat (avail_at). Returns (value, witness); value ∈ {'1', 'functional support established', 'not certified'}.
    This function verifies only the structural dependence and numerical difference of the model (model mode); the actual material route and action certification are left to the declaration."""
    status = 'not certified'
    wit = None
    for k, s in enumerate(sigs):
        w = states[k]
        Uav = avail_at(host, states, k, T, eps)
        if not Uav:
            continue
        y = host.act(w, s)
        seen = set()
        for (p, x, _c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T or (p, x) in seen:
                continue
            seen.add((p, x))
            c = comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if abs(c) <= eps:
                continue
            st, wt = hc_status(host, w, s, p, x, Uav, eps, fracs)
            if st == 'effective difference certified':
                return '1', (k, s, p, x, c, Uav) + wt
            if st == 'functional support established' and status == 'not certified':
                status, wit = 'functional support established', (k, s, p, x, c, Uav) + wt
    return status, wit

def branches_rev(host, w, w2, s, proc, x):
    """Telescoping decomposition in the other declared order: direct read changed first (activity kept at its original value), then activity swapped. Returns (D″, A″, total difference)."""
    y, y2 = host.act(w, s), host.act(w2, s)
    c0 = comp_of(host, w, y, s, proc, x)
    cD = comp_of(host, w2, y, s, proc, x)
    c2 = comp_of(host, w2, y2, s, proc, x)
    return cD - c0, c2 - cD, c2 - c0
~~~

A.2  jhc\_models.py  Upstream frozen program: Appendix A.2 of *The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields* v1.0 (Fly and the other declared models)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields v1.0: declared host models (all are minimal models constructed in this paper; parameters are constructed values).
Representative instances (model layer):
  Ecoli     monotone proxy model of E. coli chemotaxis (joint receptor-cluster activity A; two classes of methylation positions, Tar and Tsr; the CheR and CheB components include a site-availability factor).
  Fly       minimal KC→MBON model of the Drosophila mushroom body (γ1pedc short trace, α3 long trace, PPL1-α3 disinhibited via the γ1 Trace-Responses; three variants).
  Physarum  Wheatstone-type five-tube network as a model of the Physarum polycephalum tube network (Kirchhoff conservation, Tero-type Hill growth and decay).
  Rill      cell model of a beach-surface dent (footprint or rill), with three regimes: scour, deposition and mixed.
Controls (boundary checks):
  SunStone      sun-warmed stone (absorption independent of the current temperature; cooling is a direct read of its own position).
  ContactStone  contact-heated stone (the heat flow is borne by the temperature difference, so it is activity-mediated).
  Scratch       scratch in a constant medium (read out by scanning; no process acts on the Trace-Response).
  Register      odometer counter register (unit increment independent of the current reading; wraps to zero at full scale).
  Gerry         two mutually uncoupled subsystems (construction of a spliced boundary).
Counterexample models: Cancel (X1), NetShallow (X2), Joint (X3), Reverse (X4).
Record models for the cross-record chain: FlyRec2, FlyNoY.
For the periodic write of the digit wheel, see also jhc_bridge.digit_wheel.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from jhc_core import Host
F = Fr

def solve(A, b):
    """Gaussian elimination over the rationals."""
    n = len(A)
    M = [list(A[i]) + [b[i]] for i in range(n)]
    for c in range(n):
        piv = next(r for r in range(c, n) if M[r][c] != 0)
        M[c], M[piv] = M[piv], M[c]
        pv = M[c][c]
        M[c] = [v / pv for v in M[c]]
        for r in range(n):
            if r != c and M[r][c] != 0:
                fct = M[r][c]
                M[r] = [M[r][k] - fct * M[c][k] for k in range(n + 1)]
    return [M[i][n] for i in range(n)]

class Ecoli(Host):
    """Activity proxy A = M²/(M² + K²·Rt·Rs), M = mTar + mTsr, R(L) = ((1+L)/(1+L/50))².
    Components: CheR_i = h·kR·(1 − A)·(4 − m_i)/4 (deepening), CheB_i = −h·kB·A·m_i/4 (shallowing).
    Trace-free ground state m = 2, 2 (A = 1/2; stationary under rest). kR = 0 is the cheR-deletion variant."""
    def __init__(self, kR=F(1), kB=F(1), h=F(1, 4), K=F(4), grid=4096):
        self.name = 'Ecoli' if kR != 0 else 'Ecoli_cheR-'
        self.X = ['mTar', 'mTsr']
        self.lo = {x: F(0) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(4) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.kR, self.kB, self.h, self.K, self.grid = kR, kB, h, K, grid
        if kR != 0:
            g = {'mTar': F(2), 'mTsr': F(2)}
        else:
            g = {'mTar': F(0), 'mTsr': F(0)}
        self.ground = dict(g)
        self.init = dict(g)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'Ltar', 'Ltsr', 'Ltar3']
    @staticmethod
    def R(L):
        return ((1 + L) / (1 + L / 50)) ** 2
    def lig(self, s):
        return {'rest': (F(0), F(0)), 'Ltar': (F(1), F(0)), 'Ltsr': (F(0), F(1)),
                'Ltar3': (F(3), F(0))}[s]
    def act(self, w, s):
        Lt, Ls = self.lig(s)
        M = w['mTar'] + w['mTsr']
        A = M * M / (M * M + self.K ** 2 * self.R(Lt) * self.R(Ls))
        return {'A': A}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        A = y['A']
        out = []
        for x in self.X:
            out.append(('CheR', x, self.h * self.kR * (1 - A) * (4 - w[x]) / 4))
            out.append(('CheB', x, -self.h * self.kB * A * w[x] / 4))
        return out

class Fly(Host):
    """KCs 0..3; K(A) = {0,1}, K(B) = {2,3} (K(B) = {1,2} when overlap=True).
    Depth of the γ1 positions g_i and the α3 positions a_i = depression fraction ∈ [0,1].
    Activity: MBON_g = Σ_{K(s)} (1 − g_i); DAN_g = shock + fb·(|K(s)| − MBON_g) (fb only in variant gfb);
          PPL1_a3 = shock + (−a + b·Σ_{K(s)} g_i) (when an odor is present); MBON_a3 = Σ_{K(s)} (1 − a_i).
    Components: Wg_i = η_g·DAN_g (when the KC is active), Rg_i = −ρ_g·g_i (every beat), Wa_i = η_a·PPL1_a3 (when the KC is active; signed).
    Variants: full (γ1 ∪ α3), gonly (γ1 only, feedforward), gfb (γ1 only, plus MBON-γ1pedc → PPL1-γ1pedc disinhibition)."""
    def __init__(self, variant='full', overlap=False, grid=4096):
        self.name = 'Fly_' + variant + ('_ov' if overlap else '')
        self.variant = variant
        self.KA = [0, 1]
        self.KB = [1, 2] if overlap else [2, 3]
        self.X = ['g0', 'g1', 'g2', 'g3']
        if variant == 'full':
            self.X += ['a0', 'a1', 'a2', 'a3']
        self.lo = {x: F(0) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: F(0) for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
        self.grid = grid
        self.eta_g, self.rho_g, self.eta_a = F(1, 2), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)
        self.a, self.b, self.fb = F(1, 2), F(1), F(1, 2)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'A+', 'B+', 'A', 'B']
    def K(self, s):
        if s.startswith('A'):
            return self.KA
        if s.startswith('B'):
            return self.KB
        return []
    def act(self, w, s):
        K = self.K(s)
        shock = F(1) if s.endswith('+') else F(0)
        odor = len(K) > 0
        mg = sum((1 - w['g%d' % i] for i in K), F(0))
        dang = shock
        if self.variant == 'gfb' and odor:
            dang = shock + self.fb * (len(K) - mg)
        y = {'MBON_g': mg, 'DAN_g': dang}
        if self.variant == 'full':
            p = shock + ((-self.a + self.b * sum((w['g%d' % i] for i in K), F(0))) if odor else F(0))
            y['PPL1_a3'] = p
            y['MBON_a3'] = sum((1 - w['a%d' % i] for i in K), F(0))
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        K = self.K(s)
        out = []
        for i in range(4):
            g = 'g%d' % i
            if i in K:
                out.append(('Wg', g, self.eta_g * y['DAN_g']))
            out.append(('Rg', g, -self.rho_g * w[g]))
            if self.variant == 'full' and i in K:
                out.append(('Wa', 'a%d' % i, self.eta_a * y['PPL1_a3']))
        return out

class Physarum(Host):
    """Nodes 0 (pumped inlet, injection 1), 1, 2, 3 (sink, pressure 0); tubes 01, 02, 13, 23, 12 with lengths 1, 2, 2, 1, 1.
    Flow rate Q_e = (D_e/ℓ_e)(p_u − p_v). Signal N1: node 1 receives an extra injection of 1/2 (nutrient stimulus); the sink absorbs all of it.
    Components: grow_e = h·κ·Q²/(q0² + Q²) (activity-mediated), decay_e = −h·γ·D_e (direct read of own position).
    Trace-free ground state: the gridded state reached after 400 rest beats from D ≡ 1 (computed at run time and checked to be stationary under rest)."""
    E = [('01', 0, 1, F(1)), ('02', 0, 2, F(2)), ('13', 1, 3, F(2)), ('23', 2, 3, F(1)), ('12', 1, 2, F(1))]
    def __init__(self, grid=4096):
        self.name = 'Physarum'
        self.X = [e[0] for e in self.E]
        self.lo = {x: F(0) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(4) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.grid = grid
        self.h, self.kap, self.q0, self.gam = F(1, 8), F(2), F(1, 2), F(1)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'N1']
        w = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        for _ in range(400):
            w = self.step(w, 'rest')
        self.ground = dict(w)
        self.init = dict(w)
    def act(self, w, s):
        inj = [F(1), F(1, 2) if s == 'N1' else F(0), F(0)]
        L = [[F(0)] * 3 for _ in range(3)]
        for (e, u, v, ln) in self.E:
            c = w[e] / ln
            for (a, b_) in ((u, v), (v, u)):
                if a < 3:
                    L[a][a] += c
                    if b_ < 3:
                        L[a][b_] -= c
        try:
            p = solve(L, inj) + [F(0)]
        except StopIteration:
            p = [F(0)] * 4
        y = {}
        for (e, u, v, ln) in self.E:
            y['Q' + e] = w[e] / ln * (p[u] - p[v])
        for k in range(3):
            y['p%d' % k] = p[k]
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for e in self.X:
            q = y['Q' + e]
            out.append(('grow', e, self.h * self.kap * q * q / (self.q0 ** 2 + q * q)))
            out.append(('decay', e, -self.h * self.gam * w[e]))
        return out

class Rill(Host):
    """Depth d of the dent relative to the surrounding beach surface. tide beat: concentrated flow q = q0·(1 + κ·d) (activity);
    components (relative to the surroundings): erode = h·α·(max(0, q − qc) − max(0, q0 − qc)) (activity-mediated deepening),
                      deposit = −h·β·c·(q − q0) (activity-mediated shallowing); step (trampling): foot = sd (independent of d).
    Regimes: dep (qc = 100, c = 1/4); ero (qc = 3/2, c = 0); mix (qc = 3/2, c = 1/4)."""
    REG = {'dep': (F(100), F(1, 4)), 'ero': (F(3, 2), F(0)), 'mix': (F(3, 2), F(1, 4))}
    def __init__(self, regime='mix', grid=4096):
        self.name = 'Rill_' + regime
        self.regime = regime
        self.qc, self.c = self.REG[regime]
        self.X = ['d']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'d': F(0)}, {'d': F(4)}
        self.orient = {'d': 1}
        self.ground = {'d': F(0)}
        self.init = {'d': F(0)}
        self.grid = grid
        self.h, self.q0, self.kap, self.al, self.be, self.sd = F(1, 4), F(1), F(2), F(1), F(1), F(1, 4)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'step', 'tide']
    def act(self, w, s):
        if s == 'tide':
            return {'q': self.q0 * (1 + self.kap * w['d'])}
        return {'q': F(0)}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        if s == 'step':
            return [('foot', 'd', self.sd)]
        if s == 'tide':
            q = y['q']
            er = self.h * self.al * (max(F(0), q - self.qc) - max(F(0), self.q0 - self.qc))
            de = -self.h * self.be * self.c * (q - self.q0)
            return [('erode', 'd', er), ('deposit', 'd', de)]
        return []

class SunStone(Host):
    """Sun-warmed stone: temperature rise T. A sun beat absorbs h·S (independent of T); cooling −h·k·T (direct read of own position); infrared read-out IR = k·T."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'SunStone'
        self.X = ['T']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'T': F(0)}, {'T': F(10)}
        self.orient = {'T': 1}
        self.ground, self.init = {'T': F(0)}, {'T': F(0)}
        self.grid = 4096
        self.h, self.S, self.k = F(1, 4), F(1), F(1, 2)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'sun']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'IR': self.k * w['T']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = [('cool', 'T', -self.h * self.k * w['T'])]
        if s == 'sun':
            out.append(('absorb', 'T', self.h * self.S))
        return out

class ContactStone(Host):
    """Contact-heated stone: on a plate beat the heat flow is J = κ·(Tp − T) (activity), with component conduct = h·J; cooling −h·k·T."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'ContactStone'
        self.X = ['T']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'T': F(0)}, {'T': F(10)}
        self.orient = {'T': 1}
        self.ground, self.init = {'T': F(0)}, {'T': F(0)}
        self.grid = 4096
        self.h, self.kap, self.Tp, self.k = F(1, 4), F(1), F(2), F(1, 2)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'plate']
    def act(self, w, s):
        J = self.kap * (self.Tp - w['T']) if s == 'plate' else F(0)
        return {'J': J, 'IR': self.k * w['T']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return [('conduct', 'T', self.h * y['J']), ('cool', 'T', -self.h * self.k * w['T'])]

class Scratch(Host):
    """Scratch in a constant medium: scratch deepens the cut by 1; scan read-out scan = g; no other process."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'Scratch'
        self.X = ['g']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'g': F(0)}, {'g': F(4)}
        self.orient = {'g': 1}
        self.ground, self.init = {'g': F(0)}, {'g': F(0)}
        self.grid = None
        self.signals = ['rest', 'scratch']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'scan': w['g']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return [('cut', 'g', F(1))] if s == 'scratch' else []

class Register(Host):
    """Odometer counter register N ∈ {0..999}: step adds 1 (independent of N); at N = 999 there is an additional full-scale wrap-to-zero component −1000 (direct read of own position)."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'Register'
        self.X = ['N']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'N': F(0)}, {'N': F(999)}
        self.orient = {'N': 1}
        self.ground, self.init = {'N': F(0)}, {'N': F(0)}
        self.grid = None
        self.signals = ['rest', 'step']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'display': w['N']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        if s != 'step':
            return []
        out = [('inc', 'N', F(1))]
        if w['N'] == 999:
            out.append(('wrap', 'N', F(-1000)))
        return out

class Gerry(Host):
    """Spliced boundary: s is a scratch cut by the signal (read out by scanning; no acting process); b is the Trace-Response left by in-boundary continuation
    (trace-free ground state 0; the starting value 1/2 comes from the in-field pulse of the entry beat, not from a signal); activity y_b = b; component self = h·y_b/2."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = 'Gerry'
        self.X = ['s', 'b']
        self.lo, self.hi = {'s': F(0), 'b': F(0)}, {'s': F(4), 'b': F(4)}
        self.orient = {'s': 1, 'b': 1}
        self.ground = {'s': F(0), 'b': F(0)}
        self.init = {'s': F(0), 'b': F(1, 2)}
        self.grid = 4096
        self.h = F(1, 4)
        self.signals = ['rest', 'scratch']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'scan': w['s'], 'yb': w['b']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = [('self', 'b', self.h * y['yb'] / 2)]
        if s == 'scratch':
            out.append(('cut', 's', F(1)))
        return out

# ---------------- Models for counterexamples X1–X4 ----------------
class Cancel(Host):
    """X1: c = 1 + y − x, y = x. The two branches cancel; the total component is constantly 1."""
    name = 'X1_Cancel'
    X = ['x']
    lo, hi = {'x': F(0)}, {'x': F(4)}
    ground, orient, init = {'x': F(0)}, {'x': 1}, {'x': F(0)}
    signals = ['rest', 'seed', 'use']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {'y': w['x']}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        if s == 'seed':
            return [('seed', 'x', F(1))]
        if s == 'use':
            return [('p', 'x', 1 + y['y'] - w['x'])]
        return []

class NetShallow(Cancel):
    """X2: write y/4 (activity-mediated), shallowing −x/2 (direct read); on an action beat the depth is multiplied by 3/4."""
    name = 'X2_NetShallow'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        if s == 'use':
            return [('grow', 'x', y['y'] / 4), ('decay', 'x', -w['x'] / 2)]
        return super().comps(w, y, s)

class Joint(Host):
    """X3: component (a−1)(b−1) on x, with a = b = x = 1; setting a or b alone gives no response, setting both to zero jointly gives 1."""
    name = 'X3_Joint'
    X = ['a', 'b', 'x']
    lo = {k: F(0) for k in X}
    hi = {k: F(4) for k in X}
    ground = {k: F(0) for k in X}
    orient = {k: 1 for k in X}
    init = {k: F(1) for k in X}
    signals = ['rest', 'use']
    def act(self, w, s):
        return {}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return [('p', 'x', (w['a'] - 1) * (w['b'] - 1))]

class Reverse(Cancel):
    """X4: ground state 1; 1/2 after the write; positive-orientation depth −1/2."""
    name = 'X4_Reverse'
    init = {'x': F(1)}
    ground = {'x': F(1)}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return [('p', 'x', -F(1, 2))] if s == 'use' else []

# ---------------- Record models for the cross-record chain ----------------
class FlyRec2(Fly):
    """Record 2 (graded optogenetic drive of PPL1-α3): PPL1_a3 is set directly by the external drive u; the γ1 Trace-Responses play no part;
    offset is the channel offset ([S13] class Cb: the difference between optical drive and physiological firing; constant, |offset| ≤ ζ).
    co_drive=True is the C2 counterexample: the drive also rewrites another modulatory quantity z (the actual write depends only on z, not on PPL1_a3)."""
    def __init__(self, u, offset=F(0), co_drive=False):
        Fly.__init__(self, 'full')
        self.u, self.offset, self.co_drive = u, offset, co_drive
        self.name = 'FlyRec2'
    def act(self, w, s):
        y = Fly.act(self, w, s)
        if self.K(s):
            y['PPL1_a3'] = self.u + self.offset
            if self.co_drive:
                y['z'] = self.u
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for (p, x, c) in Fly.comps(self, w, y, s):
            if p == 'Wa' and self.co_drive:
                c = self.eta_a * y['z']
            out.append((p, x, c))
        return out

class FlyNoY(Fly):
    """Target instance of the C2 counterexample: the α3 write depends only on the modulatory quantity z (physiologically constant at z₀), not on PPL1-α3."""
    def __init__(self, z0):
        Fly.__init__(self, 'full')
        self.z0 = z0
        self.name = 'FlyNoY'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for (p, x, c) in Fly.comps(self, w, y, s):
            if p == 'Wa':
                c = self.eta_a * self.z0
            out.append((p, x, c))
        return out
~~~

A.3  jhc\_bridge.py  Upstream frozen program: Appendix A.3 of *The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields* v1.0 (cross-record-chain certificates)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields v1.0: dedicated verification of identification and bridging.
  window_geo        robust window of three-way identification (Theorem 17-1): endpoints of the retention classification of geometrically self-decaying modes.
  digit_wheel       carrier with periodic writes (the digit wheel): along the orbit of formation writes it returns to the trace-free ground state (Theorem 17-3).
  probe_orders      conflict of depth orders induced by two probes (Lemma 17-3′).
  cert_continuous   short-beat knockout–setting response of the continuous class and its first-order error bound; cert_short adds the reading budget (Theorem 17-7′).
  exp_bracket       rational lower and upper bounds for e^h − 1 (X5).
  chain_eval        counterexample evaluation for cross-record chain joining; chain_certificate is the chain certificate for the monotone-law class (Theorem 17-8′).
  cycle_map         cycle map; cyclemap_welldef checks that the cycle map is well-defined (Theorem 17-6′).
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
F = Fr

# ---------- Theorem 17-1: robust window of geometric self-decay ----------
def first_below(A, lam, eps):
    """Smallest t ≥ 0 with A·lam^t ≤ eps."""
    t, v = 0, A
    while v > eps:
        t += 1
        v = v * lam
    return t

def last_above(a, lam, eps, cap=100000):
    """Largest t ≥ 0 with a·lam^t > eps (when a > eps); returns -1 if a ≤ eps."""
    if a <= eps:
        return -1
    t, v = 0, a
    while v * lam > eps and t < cap:
        t += 1
        v = v * lam
    return t

def window_geo(lams, a, A, eps):
    """Self-decay rates lams of the modes, write amplitudes ∈ [a, A]. For each mode, find the endpoints that fix, at every window length t, whether it is “surely retained” or “surely not retained”;
    returns a dict mapping each mode k to (t_f(k), t_s(k)): the first t from which k is surely not retained, and the last t at which k is surely retained (−1 if none); the robust window [t_lo, t_hi] is read off as the largest t_f in the fast group and the smallest t_s in the slow group.
    Surely retained: a·lam^t > eps; surely not retained: A·lam^t ≤ eps; all others are “amplitude-dependent”."""
    return {k: (first_below(A, l, eps), last_above(a, l, eps)) for k, l in lams.items()}

# ---------- Theorem 17-3: digit wheel ----------
def digit_wheel(n=10):
    """Digit wheel: carrier states 0..n−1, formation write (advance by one step) w(k) = (k + 1) mod n. Returns the orbit starting from the ground state 0."""
    orb, k = [0], 0
    while True:
        k = (k + 1) % n
        if k == 0:
            break
        orb.append(k)
    return orb

def k3_possible_on_orbit(orb, n):
    """Whether the orbit admits a depth function satisfying K1 (depth 0 at the ground state, > 0 elsewhere) and K3 (formation writes do not decrease depth).
    The write at the last point of the orbit returns to the ground state: K3 requires δ(0) ≥ δ(last point) > 0, contradicting K1. Returns (possible?, point of contradiction)."""
    last = orb[-1]
    if (last + 1) % n == orb[0]:
        return False, (last, orb[0])
    return True, None

# ---------- Lemma 17-3′: order conflict between two probes ----------
def probe_orders(states, e1, e2):
    """Returns a pair of states on which the orders induced by e1 and e2 conflict (None if there is no conflict)."""
    for u in states:
        for v in states:
            if e1(u) < e1(v) and e2(u) > e2(v):
                return (u, v)
    return None

# ---------- Theorem 17-7′: short-beat certificate for the continuous class (polynomial systems, exact integration of the knockout difference) ----------
def pint(coefs, y0, c, h):
    """∫_0^h P(y0 + c s) ds, P a polynomial (coefficients from low to high degree). Exact."""
    # Expand P(y0 + c s) as a polynomial in s
    from math import comb
    deg = len(coefs) - 1
    sc = [F(0)] * (deg + 1)
    for k, a in enumerate(coefs):
        for j in range(k + 1):
            sc[j] += a * comb(k, j) * (y0 ** (k - j)) * (c ** j)
    return sum((sc[j] * h ** (j + 1) / (j + 1) for j in range(deg + 1)), F(0))

def pval(coefs, y):
    return sum((a * y ** k for k, a in enumerate(coefs)), F(0))

def pder(coefs):
    return [k * coefs[k] for k in range(1, len(coefs))]

def sup_abs_quad(coefs, lo, hi):
    """Supremum of |P| on [lo, hi] for a polynomial of degree at most two (endpoints and vertex)."""
    cands = [lo, hi]
    if len(coefs) == 3 and coefs[2] != 0:
        v = -coefs[1] / (2 * coefs[2])
        if lo <= v <= hi:
            cands.append(v)
    return max(abs(pval(coefs, t)) for t in cands)

def cert_continuous(fm, g, c, y0, delta, h):
    """System y' = c, x' = f_m(y) + g(y); f_m and g are polynomials of degree at most two. Process m reads the Trace-Response y and acts on x.
    Knockout difference Δ_m^h(y0) = x_full(h) − x_{−m}(h) = ∫_0^h f_m(y0 + c s) ds (exact).
    Setting response R = Δ_m^h(y0 + δ) − Δ_m^h(y0).
    Error bound B = h²·(L·M + L'·M'): L and M are the Lipschitz constant (max norm) and the supremum of the full-system field on the convex-hull box,
    L' and M' are those of the knockout system. Returns a dict."""
    lo = min(y0, y0 + delta, y0 + c * h, y0 + delta + c * h)
    hi = max(y0, y0 + delta, y0 + c * h, y0 + delta + c * h)
    full = [a + b for a, b in zip(fm + [F(0)] * (3 - len(fm)), g + [F(0)] * (3 - len(g)))]
    gg = g + [F(0)] * (3 - len(g))
    L = sup_abs_quad(pder(full) + [F(0)], lo, hi)
    M = max(abs(c), sup_abs_quad(full, lo, hi))
    L2 = sup_abs_quad(pder(gg) + [F(0)], lo, hi)
    M2 = max(abs(c), sup_abs_quad(gg, lo, hi))
    B = h * h * (L * M + L2 * M2)
    D0 = pint(fm, y0, c, h)
    D1 = pint(fm, y0 + delta, c, h)
    R = D1 - D0
    lin = h * (pval(fm, y0 + delta) - pval(fm, y0))
    return {'R': R, 'lin': lin, 'B': B, 'err': abs(R - lin), 'fires': abs(R) > B,
            'fdiff': pval(fm, y0 + delta) - pval(fm, y0)}

# ---------- Theorem 17-8′: counterexample evaluation for cross-record chain joining ----------
def chain_eval(h_link, g_link, bypass, T1, T2):
    """Write component on the target instance: c(T) = g(h(T)) + bypass(T). Returns (c(T1), c(T2))."""
    return g_link(h_link(T1)) + bypass(T1), g_link(h_link(T2)) + bypass(T2)

# ---------- Theorem 17-6′: cycle map ----------
def cycle_map(host, d, cycle):
    w = {'d': d}
    for s in cycle:
        w = host.step(w, s)
    return w['d']

def cert_short(fm, g, c, y0, delta, h, r_phi=F(0)):
    """Theorem 17-7′: errors reported separately.
    Model error (first-order expansion of the flow): E_mod = h²(LM + L′M′); reading error: each of the four raw flow readings ≤ r_phi,
    each of the two knockout differences ≤ 2 r_phi, the final response ≤ 4 r_phi. Rule for a verdict of 1: |R_obs| > E_mod + 4 r_phi (the finite-difference verdict f(s′) ≠ f(s) is issued first).
    Completeness threshold: K = LM + L′M′, the coefficient of E_mod; when K = 0 the certificate is complete for all h (no division is performed)."""
    r = cert_continuous(fm, g, c, y0, delta, h)
    K = r['B'] / (h * h)
    r['K'] = K
    r['bud_read'] = 4 * r_phi
    r['fires_read'] = abs(r['R']) > r['B'] + 4 * r_phi
    if r['fdiff'] == 0:
        r['h_complete'] = None
    elif K == 0:
        r['h_complete'] = 'all'
    else:
        r['h_complete'] = abs(r['fdiff']) / (2 * K)
    return r

def exp_bracket(h, N=12):
    """Rational lower and upper bounds for e^h − 1 (0 < h ≤ 1/2): the partial sum, with remainder ≤ 2·h^{N+1}/(N+1)!."""
    from math import factorial
    s = sum((h ** k / factorial(k) for k in range(1, N + 1)), F(0))
    return s, s + 2 * h ** (N + 1) / factorial(N + 1)

def cyclemap_welldef(host, states_by_depth, cycle, key):
    """Well-definedness check for the cycle map: whether several reachable states with the same depth key(w) have the same depth after one cycle."""
    outs = {}
    for w in states_by_depth:
        w2 = dict(w)
        for s in cycle:
            w2 = host.step(w2, s)
        outs.setdefault(key(w), set()).add(key(w2))
    return {k: v for k, v in outs.items() if len(v) > 1}

# ---------- Theorem 17-8′: chain nonzeroness, threshold crossing and actual action proven separately ----------
def chain_certificate(y1m, y2m, r1, grid_meas, r2, zeta, eps, full_component=False, bypass=None):
    """Certificate for the monotone-law class (write monotonically nondecreasing in the mediator), with the three items issued separately.
    Nonzero: there exist grid points a < b with y2m + r1 ≤ a and b ≤ y1m − r1 such that (ĝ(b) − r2 − ζ) − (ĝ(a) + r2 + ζ) > 0;
    threshold crossing: the same lower bound > eps (effective difference certified);
    actual action: the certified interval of the target's actual total component c(T1). G(y1) ∈ [ĝ(b) − r2 − ζ, ĝ(b′) + r2 + ζ], where b′ is the smallest grid point ≥ y1m + r1;
      full_component=True declares “G is the complete component” (to be proven separately: no other component at that beat and position, no clipping in effect);
      with bypass = (lower bound, upper bound), the interval of the total component is the G interval plus the bypass interval; when neither is given, actual action gets the verdict ⊥_Act.
      Act is certified when the lower end of the interval is > eps or the upper end is < −eps (a negative sign is effective shallowing, which is likewise action).
    Returns early when the two state intervals of record 1 overlap or there are too few grid points between them, recording ⊥ for both the difference and actual action: this is conservative for a sufficient procedure;
    this function does not exhaust all inputs under which actual action can be certified independently; an independently obtained Act certificate is declared separately."""
    lo1, hi1, hi2 = y1m - r1, y1m + r1, y2m + r1
    out = {'nonzero': False, 'over_eps': False, 'margin': None, 'act': '⊥', 'act_ok': False, 'interval': None}
    if hi2 >= lo1:
        out['v'], out['why'] = '⊥', '⊥_Chain(C3′): the two state intervals of record 1 overlap'
        return out
    pts = sorted(u for u in grid_meas if hi2 <= u <= lo1)
    if len(pts) < 2:
        out['v'], out['why'] = '⊥', '⊥_Chain(C3′): not enough grid points between the two state intervals'
        return out
    a, b = pts[0], pts[-1]
    margin = (grid_meas[b] - r2 - zeta) - (grid_meas[a] + r2 + zeta)
    out.update({'a': a, 'b': b, 'margin': margin})
    if margin > 0:
        out['nonzero'] = True
    if margin > eps:
        out['over_eps'] = True
        out['v'], out['why'] = '1', 'effective difference certified'
    elif margin > 0:
        out['v'], out['why'] = '⊥', 'nonzero proven, threshold crossing unproven'
    else:
        out['v'], out['why'] = '⊥', '⊥_Chain(C3′): insufficient cancellation margin'
    g_lo = grid_meas[b] - r2 - zeta
    ups = sorted(u for u in grid_meas if u >= hi1)
    g_hi = (grid_meas[ups[0]] + r2 + zeta) if ups else None
    if full_component:
        lo_t, hi_t = g_lo, g_hi
    elif bypass is not None:
        lo_t = g_lo + bypass[0]
        hi_t = (g_hi + bypass[1]) if g_hi is not None else None
    else:
        out['act'] = '⊥_Act: bypass base value not declared, and G not proven to be the complete component'
        return out
    out['interval'] = (lo_t, hi_t)
    if lo_t > eps:
        out['act'], out['act_ok'] = 'certified (positive)', True
    elif hi_t is not None and hi_t < -eps:
        out['act'], out['act_ok'] = 'certified (negative, effective shallowing)', True
    else:
        out['act'] = '⊥_Act: certified interval intersects [−ε, ε]'
    return out
~~~

A.4  jxs\_core.py  Upstream frozen program: Appendix A.1 of *The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation* v1.0 (hosts, tendency, Act/Aff, runs and read-back)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Appendix program of The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation v1.0: core library of the run system.
Corresponds to Sections 3–5 of the main text: the complete state σ = (d, r); the read-out law Φ; write components; the residue law P; the two resolution semantics;
tendency, affected set, action set; exact dynamical identity; the permanent-hiding criterion for linear time-invariant closed loops; F-layer compilation and the two readings of the history condition.
Discipline: standard library only; every numerical value is a fractions.Fraction; no random numbers.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product

class Host:
    """Declared host model 𝔐 (D18-1).
    positions: list of Structural Positions with identity labels (X̂; may include latent positions controlled by existence variables).
    read(d, r, s) -> dict: full activity y (D18-7).
    comps(d, y, s) -> dict pos -> list[(process identifier, component c)]: valid process-by-process decomposition of the write law (D18-1).
    post(d_new, d_old) -> dict: declared post-processing (e.g. clipping, generation of existence positions); the identity if none is declared.
    resid(r, y) -> r': residue law; with no residue, r is always None.
    """
    def __init__(self, positions, read, comps, post=None, resid=None):
        self.positions = list(positions)
        self.read = read
        self.comps = comps
        self.post = post or (lambda dn, do: dn)
        self.resid = resid or (lambda r, y: r)

def step(host, d, r, s, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    """One beat of the run (run equation). sem = 'raw': original-mechanism semantics, all components booked; 'booked': booked-as-effective semantics, only |c| > eps booked.
    Returns (y, d', r', record); the record contains the components at each position, Act⁰, Act^ε, Aff⁰ and Aff^ε."""
    y = host.read(d, r, s)
    cs = host.comps(d, y, s)
    dn = dict(d)
    for x, lst in cs.items():
        if sem == "raw":
            dn[x] = d[x] + sum(c for _, c in lst)
        else:
            dn[x] = d[x] + sum(c for _, c in lst if abs(c) > eps)
    dn = host.post(dn, d)
    rn = host.resid(r, y)
    act0 = {x for x, lst in cs.items() if any(c != 0 for _, c in lst)}
    acte = {x for x, lst in cs.items() if any(abs(c) > eps for _, c in lst)}
    aff0 = {x for x in host.positions if dn.get(x, F(0)) != d.get(x, F(0))}
    affe = {x for x in host.positions if abs(dn.get(x, F(0)) - d.get(x, F(0))) > eps}
    return y, dn, rn, {"comps": cs, "Act0": act0, "Acte": acte, "Aff0": aff0, "Affe": affe}

def run(host, d, r, hist, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    ys = []
    for s in hist:
        y, d, r, _ = step(host, d, r, s, eps, sem)
        ys.append(y)
    return ys, d, r

def potential(host, d, r, signals):
    """Tendency Φ_σ (D18-6): the full-activity response over the labeled signal class."""
    return {s: host.read(d, r, s) for s in signals}

def exact_dyn_same(host, s1, s2, alphabet, L, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    """Finite verification of exact dynamical identity (D18-14) over all histories of length ≤ L; returns (whether identical, number of histories).
    eps and sem apply to both the runs and the comparison: the default is original-mechanism semantics (sem = "raw"); booked-as-effective semantics must be passed explicitly as sem = "booked" together with ε."""
    n = 0
    for k in range(L + 1):
        for h in product(alphabet, repeat=k):
            n += 1
            y1, _, _ = run(host, s1[0], s1[1], h, eps, sem)
            y2, _, _ = run(host, s2[0], s2[1], h, eps, sem)
            if y1 != y2:
                return False, n
    return True, n

def booked_sum(lst, eps):
    """Component sum of the purely numerical filtered write W^ε_num: counts only components with |c| > ε (this is the operator of sem = "booked" in step)."""
    return sum(c for _, c in lst if abs(c) > eps)

def booked_sum_F(lst, eps, cert):
    """Component sum of the F-layer certified filtered write W^ε_F: counts only components with |c| > ε and cert_eff = 1 ([S2] 4.2). cert: {process: 1/0/'⊥'}, default 1."""
    return sum(c for rho, c in lst if abs(c) > eps and cert.get(rho, 1) == 1)

def pairing_ok(cs_a, cs_b, pi):
    """Sufficient condition for process-identity pairing (Theorem 18-2(1), booked-as-effective clause): for every position x, the multiset of (process, component) of state a at x
    equals that of state b at π(x) (values preserved component by component; the same threshold ε on both sides). This guarantees that the purely numerical filtered write W^ε_num commutes;
    the F-layer certified filtered write W^ε_F additionally requires equal certification states on both sides of the pairing (cert_pairing_ok)."""
    keys = set(cs_a) | {k for k in pi}
    for x in keys:
        la = sorted(cs_a.get(x, []), key=lambda t: (str(t[0]), t[1]))
        lb = sorted(cs_b.get(pi.get(x, x), []), key=lambda t: (str(t[0]), t[1]))
        if la != lb:
            return False
    return True

def cert_pairing_ok(cs_a, cert_a, cert_b, pi):
    """The action-certification states on both sides of the pairing are equal: cert_eff of process ρ at position x equals cert_eff of the same-named process at position π(x). cert: {(process, position): value}."""
    for x, lst in cs_a.items():
        for rho, _ in lst:
            if cert_a.get((rho, x), 1) != cert_b.get((rho, pi.get(x, x)), 1):
                return False
    return True

def mat_mul(A, B):
    return [[sum(A[i][k] * B[k][j] for k in range(len(B))) for j in range(len(B[0]))] for i in range(len(A))]

def hidden_forever_lti(A, C, delta):
    """Linear time-invariant closed loop Δ' = AΔ with reading CΔ: by Cayley–Hamilton, checking k = 0..n−1 is the universal criterion (Theorem 18-7(4))."""
    v = [[x] for x in delta]
    for _ in range(len(A)):
        if any(row[0] != 0 for row in mat_mul(C, v)):
            return False
        v = mat_mul(A, v)
    return True

# ---- F-layer compilation and the two readings of the history condition (Theorem 18-4) ----
def f_component(law, s):
    """The three classes of numerical law of [S2] D12: ('const', b) / ('lin', b, k, R) / ('bil', b, k, j1, j2)."""
    if law[0] == "const":
        return law[1]
    if law[0] == "lin":
        _, b, k, R = law
        return b + k * sum(s[j] for j in R)
    _, b, k, j1, j2 = law
    return b + k * s[j1] * s[j2]

def f_supp_act(law, s):
    """Functional support of [S2] D15 (mathematically nonzero, independent of ε)."""
    if law[0] == "const":
        return set()
    if law[0] == "lin":
        return set(law[3]) if law[2] != 0 else set()
    _, b, k, j1, j2 = law
    if k == 0:
        return set()
    out = set()
    if s[j2] != 0:
        out.add(j1)
    if s[j1] != 0:
        out.add(j2)
    return out

def histcond_F(law, s, T, eps, formed):
    """HistCond_F of [S2] D17, D18 (path certification taken as 1, cert_read taken as 1): available positions = the already formed positions in Supp_act ∩ T with s > ε."""
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    return 1 if avail else 0

def histcond_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """Numeric-difference reading with single-set contrasts: returns 1 if some valid contrast that changes only one formed position in T with depth > ε makes the total component difference > ε, and 0 otherwise.
    This tests the numerical part of [17] Definition 6′(C) on the single-set subprotocol; it is not a complete certificate. contrasts: table of legitimate values {position: [value…]}."""
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    for i in pos:
        for v in contrasts.get(i, []):
            s2 = dict(s); s2[i] = v
            if abs(f_component(law, s2) - base) > eps:
                return 1
    return 0

# ---- HistCond_F with path and read certification; joint contrasts; two columns of raw judgments; F-layer booking (with cert_eff) ----
def histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read=1):
    """[S2] D18: HistCond_F = [Avail_act ≠ ∅] ∧ cert_read ∧ Path({(i,t) : i ∈ Avail_act}, m).
    path_from(start set) -> 1/0/'⊥': the path status, on the declared evidence-path graph, from that start set to this mechanism's event (D14)."""
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    if not avail:
        return 0
    if cert_read == 0:
        return 0
    p = path_from(frozenset(avail))
    if p == 0:
        return 0
    if cert_read == "⊥" or p == "⊥":
        return "⊥"
    return 1

def histcond_diff_joint(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """Cartesian-closure enumeration (used when the declared family of jointly valid contrasts is explicitly the Cartesian closure of per-position candidate values): legitimate values are taken simultaneously on any nonempty subset of the formed positions in T with depth > ε.
    Returns 'numeric difference crosses threshold' or 'numeric difference does not cross threshold' (only the numerical part of [17] Definition 6′(C); the latter is not a negation either)."""
    from itertools import combinations, product as prod
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    for k in range(1, len(pos) + 1):
        for sub in combinations(pos, k):
            for vals in prod(*[contrasts.get(i, []) for i in sub]):
                s2 = dict(s)
                for i, v in zip(sub, vals):
                    s2[i] = v
                if abs(f_component(law, s2) - base) > eps:
                    return "numeric difference crosses threshold"
    return "numeric difference does not cross threshold"

def readback_hist(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts, path_from, cert_read=1):
    """Two columns of raw judgments: (value of the F-layer functional support, numeric-difference judgment under joint contrasts)."""
    hf = histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read)
    hd = histcond_diff_joint(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts)
    return ("functional support established" if hf == 1 else ("functional support undetermined" if hf == "⊥" else "functional support not established")), hd

def f_layer_update(comps, eps):
    """F-layer booking of [S2] 4.2: counts only the components with Act_F = [|c| > ε] ∧ cert_eff = 1. comps: [(c, cert_eff)]."""
    return sum(c for c, ce in comps if abs(c) > eps and ce == 1)

def raw_update(comps):
    return sum(c for c, _ in comps)

# ---- D18 three-valued conjunction; read-back atoms and three-column summary; F-layer contrast under the actual equation ----
def kleene_and(vals):
    """Three-valued conjunction: 0 if any item is 0; ⊥ if no item is 0 but some item is ⊥; 1 if all items are 1."""
    if any(v == 0 for v in vals):
        return 0
    if any(v == "⊥" for v in vals):
        return "⊥"
    return 1

def max_contrast_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """Maximum difference over the Cartesian closure: use only when the declared family of jointly valid contrasts is exactly the Cartesian closure of per-position candidate values (declared explicitly, never a default)."""
    from itertools import combinations, product as prod
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    best = F(0)
    for k in range(1, len(pos) + 1):
        for sub in combinations(pos, k):
            for vals in prod(*[contrasts.get(i, []) for i in sub]):
                s2 = dict(s)
                for i, v in zip(sub, vals):
                    s2[i] = v
                best = max(best, abs(f_component(law, s2) - base))
    return best

def kleene_or(vals):
    """Three-valued disjunction: 1 if any item is 1; ⊥ if no item is 1 but some item is ⊥; 0 if all items are 0 (or there are none)."""
    if any(v == 1 for v in vals):
        return 1
    if any(v == "⊥" for v in vals):
        return "⊥"
    return 0

def contrast_family(contrasts):
    """Normalized contrast family (preamble of Theorem 18-4).
    contrasts as {position: [value…]}: single-set shorthand; each value at each position forms one single-set contrast, and no joint contrast is generated automatically; the declared coverage is recorded as “complete”.
    contrasts as {"contrast": [...], "coverage": "complete"/"incomplete"}: a frozen set of contrasts, each of the form {"id": name, "set": {position: value}, "evidence": {...}};
      a joint contrast must be listed explicitly as one entry (or generated by a joint-validity predicate and then listed).
    Returns (list of contrasts, coverage)."""
    if "contrast" in contrasts:
        fam = [dict(id=k.get("id", "contrast %d" % n), set=dict(k["set"]), evidence=dict(k.get("evidence", {})))
               for n, k in enumerate(contrasts["contrast"])]
        ids = [k["id"] for k in fam]
        if len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
            raise ValueError("duplicate contrast identity")
        return fam, contrasts.get("coverage", "complete")
    fam = []
    for i in sorted(contrasts, key=str):
        for v in contrasts[i]:
            fam.append(dict(id="%s→%s" % (i, v), set={i: v}, evidence={}))
    return fam, "complete"

def numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover):
    """Numeric-difference item: counts only the contrasts that satisfy [17] Definition 6′(B) (changing only formed positions in T with depth > ε).
    Values: “numeric difference crosses threshold”, “numeric difference nonzero, below threshold”, “numeric difference zero”; if none crosses the threshold and the declared coverage is incomplete, records “contrast domain insufficiently covered”."""
    base = f_component(law, s)
    m = F(0)
    for k in fam:
        act_set = {i: v for i, v in k["set"].items() if v != s[i]}
        if all(i in T and i in formed and s[i] > eps for i in act_set):
            s2 = dict(s); s2.update(act_set)
            m = max(m, abs(f_component(law, s2) - base))
    if m > eps:
        return "numeric difference crosses threshold"
    if cover != "complete":
        return "contrast domain insufficiently covered"
    return "numeric difference nonzero, below threshold" if m > 0 else "numeric difference zero"

def numeric_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """Numeric-difference item: the numerical part of [17] Definition 6′(C), not a complete certificate (the contrast family is normalized by contrast_family)."""
    fam, cover = contrast_family(contrasts)
    return numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover)

def readback_atoms(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts, path_from, cert_read=1, cert_eff=1, evidence=None,
                   independent=None):
    """Read-back atoms and three-column summary of Theorem 18-4.
    Contrast family: normalized by contrast_family; for each contrast, the difference and the certification are computed on the same beat, the same process, the same target and that contrast's identity, and the disjunction is then taken over complete witnesses;
    certification is bound to the very contrast that yields the threshold-crossing difference and is never stitched together across contrasts.
    Atoms: numerical support Avail, read certification cert_read, path Path, HistCond_F (the original conjunction of [S2] D18), Act_F (with cert_eff),
    numeric difference (numeric_diff_family), the per-contrast details, and the clause-by-clause evidence for the four clauses of [17] Definition 6′ (same-beat, bearing position, route, action).
~~~
