~~~python
    evidence: common evidence of the same F event {key: 1/0/'⊥'}, declared as applying to every contrast; a contrast's own “evidence” takes precedence clause by clause. Clause defaults:
    same-beat is given by the construction of the contrast and recorded as 1; bearing position is judged per contrast by clause (B) (1 if only formed positions in T with depth > ε are changed, 0 otherwise); route is bound to the bearing positions that
    the contrast actually changes—1 if the path from these starting points to this event is 1, '⊥' otherwise (a path of 0 does not exclude another material route; a separately declared
    valid actual route must be supplied independently, with its own certificate); the aggregate Path is only an independent atom and does not replace the per-contrast route; action takes Act_F.
    The contrast details record, per contrast, (contrast identity, |Δ|, that contrast's witness value, that contrast's actual four clauses); “Def. 6′ four clauses” takes the four clauses of the witness contrast (of the first contrast if there is no witness contrast).
    “certificate status”: the identity of the witness contrast if there is one; otherwise the value of the disjunction of the listed contrast certificates, with “declared domain not yet covered” added when the declared coverage is incomplete.
    Merging the action clause: the F atom Act_F is the action evidence of the same event.
      Act_F = 0 (explicit refutation): an external claim cannot override it; if the external claim is 1, an “evidence conflict” is recorded and the blockade stands per the original refutation; the clause takes 0.
      Act_F = 1: if the external claim is 0, an “evidence conflict” is likewise recorded and the clause takes 0 (conservative blockade); if the external claim is 1 or absent, the clause takes 1.
      Act_F = ⊥ (unproven): an explicit external certification can supply it (its value is taken, and the source is recorded as “explicit certification”); if the external input is explicitly ⊥, the source is recorded as “explicitly unproven”;
      if nothing is given, the source is recorded as “unproven” and the clause is ⊥.
    independent: a witness from a different mechanism or an independent R-layer witness, which must be {event identity, mode, certification basis, action}; it is listed separately under the atom “independent witness”
      and is not merged into the four clauses, the complete certificate or the summary of this F event; if fields are missing, “independent witness incomplete” is recorded.
    complete certificate = the three-valued disjunction, over the contrasts, of [|Δ| > ε ∧ four clauses]. The summary follows the three-way split of values in [17] Definition 6′: if the complete certificate is 1, it records
    “effective difference certified”; otherwise, if the numeric difference is nonzero and below threshold (with complete declared coverage) or HistCond_F = 1, it records “functional support established”; in all other cases it records
    “effective difference not certified”. The summary does not override the atoms; conflicts, unproven items and refutations are shown in the atoms “evidence conflict” and “action source”, respectively."""
    evidence = dict(evidence or {})
    fam, cover = contrast_family(contrasts)
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    path = path_from(frozenset(avail)) if avail else 0
    hf = histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read)
    c = f_component(law, s)
    act = kleene_and([1 if abs(c) > eps else 0, cert_eff])
    nd = numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover)
    conflicts = []
    ext = evidence.get("action")
    if act == 0:
        act_item, src = 0, "F-atom refutation"
        if ext == 1:
            conflicts.append("action: F atom Act_F = 0 (explicit refutation) conflicts with external claim 1; blockade stands per the original refutation")
    elif act == 1:
        if ext == 0:
            act_item, src = 0, "external refutation"
            conflicts.append("action: F atom Act_F = 1 conflicts with external claim 0; conservative blockade")
        else:
            act_item, src = 1, "F atom"
    else:
        if ext is None:
            act_item, src = "⊥", "unproven"
        elif ext == "⊥":
            act_item, src = "⊥", "explicitly unproven"
        else:
            act_item, src = ext, "explicit certification"
    detail, certs, witness, wfour, first_four = [], [], None, None, None
    for k in fam:
        ev = k["evidence"]
        # actual difference set: keep only the positions whose value really differs from the pre-beat value (setting a position back to its pre-beat value is not a change)
        act_set = {i: v for i, v in k["set"].items() if v != s[i]}
        legal = all(i in T and i in formed and s[i] > eps for i in act_set)
        s2 = dict(s); s2.update(act_set)
        dk = abs(f_component(law, s2) - c)
        # the route is bound to the bearing positions this contrast actually changes: the path from these starting points to this event; the aggregate Path does not replace it
        bearers = frozenset(i for i in act_set if i in avail)
        p_k = path_from(bearers) if bearers else 0
        route_k = ev.get("route", evidence.get("route", 1 if p_k == 1 else "⊥"))
        four_k = {"same-beat": ev.get("same-beat", evidence.get("same-beat", 1)),
                  "bearing position": ev.get("bearing position", evidence.get("bearing position", 1)) if legal else 0,
                  "route": route_k, "action": act_item}
        ck = kleene_and([1 if dk > eps else 0] + list(four_k.values()))
        detail.append((k["id"], dk, ck, dict(four_k)))
        if first_four is None:
            first_four = dict(four_k)
        certs.append(ck)
        if ck == 1 and witness is None:
            witness, wfour = k["id"], dict(four_k)
    cert = kleene_or(certs)
    four = wfour if witness is not None else (first_four if first_four is not None else
           {"same-beat": evidence.get("same-beat", 1), "bearing position": 0, "route": "⊥", "action": act_item})
    status = ("witness contrast “%s”" % witness if witness is not None else
              "disjunction of listed contrast certificates = %s" % cert + ("; declared domain not yet covered" if cover != "complete" else ""))
    atoms = {"Avail": 1 if avail else 0, "cert_read": cert_read, "Path": path, "HistCond_F": hf,
             "Act_F": act, "numeric difference": nd, "Def. 6′ four clauses": four, "action source": src, "evidence conflict": conflicts,
             "contrast details": detail, "witness contrast": witness, "complete certificate": cert, "coverage": cover, "certificate status": status}
    if independent is not None:
        need = ("event identity", "mode", "certification basis", "action")
        atoms["independent witness"] = dict(independent) if all(k in independent for k in need) else "independent witness incomplete"
    if cert == 1:
        col = "effective difference certified"
    elif hf == 1 or nd == "numeric difference nonzero, below threshold":
        col = "functional support established"
    else:
        col = "effective difference not certified"
    return atoms, col

def f_post_match(post, d, raw_sum, f_sum):
    """Comparison under the actual equation in Theorem 18-4(4)(A): one original-mechanism beat post(d + Σorig) against one F-layer beat d + ΣF."""
    return post(d + raw_sum) == d + f_sum

# ---- J₁₈: verification through the [S2] F-layer evaluator, trajectory reproduction and qualification (Theorem 18-4(4)) ----
def _tri(v):
    return {1: True, 0: False, "⊥": None, True: True, False: False, None: None}[v]

def _untri(v):
    return {True: 1, False: 0, None: "⊥"}[v]

def j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """Compiles the declared events of one run of this paper into the Spec of [S2] Appendix A.1 (raw events, certifications, times and sources are passed explicitly, without compression).
    events: each entry is
      {"kind": "pulse", "name": event identifier, "beat": t, "target": position, "amount": a, "out-of-boundary": True/False, "occurs": 1/0/'⊥'}, or
      {"kind": "process", "name": event identifier, "beat": t, "target": position, "carrier inside": True/False, "law": 'constant'/'linear'/'bilinear',
       "reads": (position…), "coefficient": k, "bias": b, "cert_eff": 1/0/'⊥', "cert_read": 1/0/'⊥'}.
    links: [(source node, target node, certification)]; a node is 'state:position:beat' or an event identifier.
    closed_graph: whether the evidence graph is closed, which must be declared by the evidence protocol (True/False); if it is undeclared (None), nothing is compiled."""
    if closed_graph not in (True, False):
        raise ValueError("closure of the evidence graph not declared")
    import field_final as FF
    pulses = tuple(FF.Pulse(e["name"], e["beat"], e["target"], e["amount"], e.get("out-of-boundary", True), _tri(e.get("occurs", 1)))
                   for e in events if e["kind"] == "pulse")
    procs = tuple(FF.Process(e["name"], e["beat"], e["target"], e["carrier inside"], e["law"], tuple(e.get("reads", ())),
                             e.get("coefficient", F(0)), e.get("bias", F(0)), _tri(e.get("cert_eff", 1)), _tri(e.get("cert_read", 1)))
                  for e in events if e["kind"] == "process")
    lk = tuple(FF.Link(a, b, _tri(c)) for a, b, c in links)
    return FF.Spec(tuple(sites), tuple(initial), tuple(window), pulses, procs, lk, _tri(identity), _tri(trace_history),
                   closed_graph, eps, tuple(T))

def j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events):
    """The same run under this paper's original-mechanism semantics (post is the identity): at each beat every process computes its component from the pre-beat state by its law, occurring pulses are added, and all components are booked.
    Every number entering the computation is first normalized by exact of [S2] (a decimal is taken as the exact rational given by its decimal representation), the same interpretation as in the upstream evaluator."""
    from field_final import exact
    st = {x: exact(v) for x, v in zip(sites, initial)}
    traj = []
    for t in range(window[0] - 1, window[-1] + 1):
        before = dict(st)
        add = {x: F(0) for x in sites}
        for e in events:
            if e["beat"] != t:
                continue
            if e["kind"] == "pulse":
                if e.get("occurs", 1) == 1:
                    add[e["target"]] += exact(e["amount"])
            else:
                rd = e.get("reads", ()); k = exact(e.get("coefficient", F(0))); b = exact(e.get("bias", F(0)))
                v = b if e["law"] == "constant" else (b + k * sum(before[q] for q in rd) if e["law"] == "linear"
                                                     else b + k * before[rd[0]] * before[rd[1]])
                add[e["target"]] += v
        st = {x: before[x] + add[x] for x in sites}
        if t in window:
            traj.append(dict(st))
    return traj

def j18_check(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """The three conditions of Theorem 18-4(4), evaluated through the [S2] evaluator (field_final.check, run):
    (A) an F candidate event table can be generated: j18_spec compiles (whether the evidence graph is closed is declared by the evidence protocol), and field_final.check accepts the raw certifications and raw events under D20;
         when D20 rejects (e.g. the action certification is undetermined, even for a zero component), F is not run and the R layer is reported separately as pending;
    (B) the numerical trajectory of the original run is reproduced: the post-states at each beat of the same original-mechanism run equal, position by position, the post-states at each beat reported by the F run;
    (C) witnesses and qualification are preserved: field_final.run computes Trace_F, Part_F, HDyn_F and Q_F of D19 and the per-event atoms."""
    import field_final as FF
    try:
        spec = j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links, identity, trace_history, closed_graph)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "reject: %s" % exc, "R": "must be declared by the evidence protocol before evaluation", "event table": 0, "trajectory": "not run",
                "Trace_F": "not run", "Part_F": "not run", "HDyn_F": "not run", "Q_F": "not run", "events": []}
    try:
        FF.check(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "reject: %s" % exc, "R": "R layer pending (evaluated separately by R-layer event witnesses)", "event table": 0, "trajectory": "not run",
                "Trace_F": "not run", "Part_F": "not run", "HDyn_F": "not run", "Q_F": "not run", "events": []}
    try:
        out = FF.run(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "reject (left the domain during the run): %s" % exc, "R": "R layer pending (evaluated separately by R-layer event witnesses)", "event table": 1,
                "trajectory": "not run", "Trace_F": "not run", "Part_F": "not run", "HDyn_F": "not run", "Q_F": "not run", "events": []}
    f_traj = [rep["after"] for rep in out["reports"]]
    raw = j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events)
    traj = 1 if [{x: F(v) for x, v in a.items()} for a in f_traj] == raw else 0
    evs = [dict(id=e["id"], c=e["delta"], Act_F=_untri(e["act"]), Cond_F=_untri(e["cond"]), HistCond_F=_untri(e["histcond"]))
           for rep in out["reports"] for e in rep["events"]]
    return {"D20": "accept", "R": "—", "event table": 1, "trajectory": traj, "Trace_F": _untri(out["trace"]), "Part_F": _untri(out["part"]),
            "HDyn_F": _untri(out["hdyn"]), "Q_F": _untri(out["Q"]), "events": evs, "F trajectory": f_traj, "original-mechanism trajectory": raw}

def nonneg_encode(d):
    """Nonnegative encoding of signed depth for entry into the F layer: d = d⁺ − d⁻ with d⁺, d⁻ ≥ 0; returns (encoding, read-back function)."""
    enc = {}
    for x, v in d.items():
        enc[(x, "+")] = v if v > 0 else F(0)
        enc[(x, "−")] = -v if v < 0 else F(0)
    return enc, (lambda e: {x: e[(x, "+")] - e[(x, "−")] for x in d})
~~~

A.5  jxs\_models.py  Upstream frozen program: Appendix A.2 of *The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation* v1.0 (the FLY, FLYC, LOOP and CHAIN models)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Appendix program of The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation v1.0: declared host models (constructed in this paper; the parameters are constructed values, not measured values).
FLY: minimal model of the Drosophila mushroom-body type (KC × compartment synaptic depth; the MBON drives and the PPL1-α3 response make up the full activity).
FLYC: FLY plus the α3 consolidation state (law-layer Trace-Response, Theorem 18-8).
TERO: Tero-type flow network (conductance is the Trace-Response; flow rates and pressures are activity).
CHAIN: one-dimensional containment chain with existence variables (representation of Edge-Seepage, Theorem 18-9).
LOOP: γ1 self-loop toy (sign law of the local loop gain, Theorem 18-5).
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
# ---------------- FLY ----------------
KC = [0, 1, 2, 3]
COMP = ("g1", "a3", "P", "N")
ODOR = {"A": (0, 1), "B": (2, 3), "C": (1, 2)}
A_INN, B_DIS, ETA, R1 = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 4), F(9, 10)
FLY_POS = [(i, c) for i in KC for c in COMP]

def fly_zero():
    return {p: F(0) for p in FLY_POS}

def fly_read(d, r, s):
    """Full activity: the MBON drive of each compartment and the PPL1-α3 response; rest R has no activity reading (returns empty)."""
    if s == "R":
        return {}
    y = {"MBON_" + c: sum(1 - d[(i, c)] for i in ODOR[s]) for c in COMP}
    y["PPL1_a3"] = -A_INN + B_DIS * sum(d[(i, "g1")] for i in ODOR[s])
    return y

def fly_comps(d, y, s):
    """Write components: when s is presented, the α3 column (K(s)) receives η·PPL1-α3; every beat, γ1 relaxes by −(1 − r1)·d."""
    cs = {}
    if s != "R":
        for i in ODOR[s]:
            cs.setdefault((i, "a3"), []).append(("W_a3", ETA * y["PPL1_a3"]))
    for i in KC:
        cs.setdefault((i, "g1"), []).append(("RELAX_g1", -(1 - R1) * d[(i, "g1")]))
    return cs

def fly_post(dn, do):
    """Clipping: the α3 positions are clipped to [0, 1]."""
    out = dict(dn)
    for i in KC:
        out[(i, "a3")] = min(F(1), max(F(0), out[(i, "a3")]))
    return out

def make_fly():
    from jxs_core import Host
    return Host(FLY_POS, fly_read, fly_comps, fly_post)

# ---------------- FLYC: consolidation state ----------------
# Each α3 position also has a consolidation state c ∈ {0, 1} (law-layer Trace-Response); the per-beat retention ratio of α3 depression is ρ(c): c = 0 → 1/2, c = 1 → 9/10.
# Consolidation write (history version): when s is presented and PPL1-α3 > θ_c, c ← 1 for the α3 positions of K(s). Clock-controlled version: c ← 1 once k_c beats have elapsed since the first write.
RHO = {0: F(1, 2), 1: F(9, 10)}
THETA_C = F(1, 8)

def flyc_positions():
    return FLY_POS + [("c", i) for i in KC]

def flyc_zero():
    z = fly_zero()
    for i in KC:
        z[("c", i)] = F(0)
    return z

def flyc_read(d, r, s):
    return fly_read({p: d[p] for p in FLY_POS}, r, s)

def flyc_comps_history(d, y, s):
    cs = fly_comps({p: d[p] for p in FLY_POS}, y, s)
    for i in KC:
        rho = RHO[int(d[("c", i)])]
        cs.setdefault((i, "a3"), []).append(("RELAX_a3", -(1 - rho) * d[(i, "a3")]))
    if s != "R" and y["PPL1_a3"] > THETA_C:
        for i in ODOR[s]:
            if d[("c", i)] == 0:
                cs.setdefault(("c", i), []).append(("CONSOL", F(1)))
    return cs

def make_flyc(clock=None, block_dan=False):
    """clock=None: history version; clock=k: clock-controlled version (r counts the beats since the first α3 write). block_dan: DAN blockade during consolidation (neither the PPL1-α3 write nor the consolidation write occurs)."""
    from jxs_core import Host
    def comps(d, y, s):
        if block_dan and s != "R":
            y = dict(y); y["PPL1_a3"] = F(0)
        cs = flyc_comps_history(d, y, s) if clock is None else None
        if clock is not None:
            cs = fly_comps({p: d[p] for p in FLY_POS}, y, s)
            for i in KC:
                rho = RHO[int(d[("c", i)])]
                cs.setdefault((i, "a3"), []).append(("RELAX_a3", -(1 - rho) * d[(i, "a3")]))
        if block_dan and s != "R":
            for i in ODOR[s]:
                cs[(i, "a3")] = [p for p in cs.get((i, "a3"), []) if p[0] != "W_a3"]
        return cs
    def resid(r, y):
        if clock is None:
            return r
        return r  # in the clock-controlled version, timing is handled by post
    def post(dn, do):
        out = fly_post(dn, do)
        return out
    return Host(flyc_positions(), flyc_read, comps, post, resid)

def flyc_clock_run(d, hist, k_c, block_dan=False):
    """Explicit run of the clock-controlled consolidation version: c_i is set to 1 once k_c beats have elapsed since its α3 position first exceeded 0, regardless of whether DAN is active during consolidation."""
    from jxs_core import step
    host = make_flyc(clock=k_c, block_dan=block_dan)
    since = {i: None for i in KC}
    ys = []
    for s in hist:
        y, dn, _, _ = step(host, d, None, s)
        for i in KC:
            if since[i] is None and dn[(i, "a3")] > 0:
                since[i] = 0
            elif since[i] is not None:
                since[i] += 1
                if since[i] >= k_c:
                    dn[("c", i)] = F(1)
        d = dn
        ys.append(y)
    return ys, d

# ---------------- TERO ----------------
def solve(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I):
    adj = {n: set() for n in nodes}
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd > 0:
            adj[u].add(v); adj[v].add(u)
    comp, stack = {snk}, [snk]
    while stack:
        x = stack.pop()
        for yv in adj[x]:
            if yv not in comp:
                comp.add(yv); stack.append(yv)
    if src not in comp:
        raise ValueError("source and sink are not connected")
    unk = [n for n in nodes if n in comp and n != snk]
    idx = {n: k for k, n in enumerate(unk)}
    m = len(unk)
    A = [[F(0)] * m for _ in range(m)]
    b = [F(0)] * m
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd <= 0 or u not in comp:
            continue
        for x, yv in ((u, v), (v, u)):
            if x in idx:
                A[idx[x]][idx[x]] += dd
                if yv in idx:
                    A[idx[x]][idx[yv]] -= dd
    b[idx[src]] = F(I)
    for col in range(m):
        piv = next(r for r in range(col, m) if A[r][col] != 0)
        A[col], A[piv] = A[piv], A[col]; b[col], b[piv] = b[piv], b[col]
        for r in range(m):
            if r != col and A[r][col] != 0:
                fct = A[r][col] / A[col][col]
                for k in range(col, m):
                    A[r][k] -= fct * A[col][k]
                b[r] -= fct * b[col]
    p = {n: None for n in nodes}
    p[snk] = F(0)
    for n in unk:
        p[n] = b[idx[n]] / A[idx[n]][idx[n]]
    return p

def flows(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I):
    p = solve(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I)
    Q = []
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd <= 0 or p[u] is None or p[v] is None:
            Q.append(F(0))
        else:
            Q.append(dd * (p[u] - p[v]))
    return p, Q

def f_tero2(q):
    return q * q / (1 + q * q)

def make_tero(nodes, edges, src, snk, I, h, gam, eps_rm, grid=None, f=f_tero2):
    """Positions = tube-segment indices; d is the conductance (declared D⁰ = 0); activity = the flow rate in each tube and the node pressures; write h(f(|Q|) − γD);
    post: gridding and the removal threshold (conductance below eps_rm is set to 0: the shallowing limit, where the derived incidence changes, Theorem 18-9). The signal s is a multiple of the injection strength ('I' is constant injection)."""
    from jxs_core import Host
    pos = list(range(len(edges)))
    def read(d, r, s):
        D = [d[k] for k in pos]
        p, Q = flows(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I)
        return {"Q": tuple(Q), "p": tuple(p[n] for n in nodes)}
    def comps(d, y, s):
        return {k: [("ADAPT", h * (f(abs(y["Q"][k])) - gam * d[k]))] for k in pos if d[k] > 0}
    def post(dn, do):
        out = dict(dn)
        for k in pos:
            v = out[k]
            if grid is not None:
                v = F(round(v * grid), grid)
            out[k] = v if v >= eps_rm else F(0)
        return out
    return Host(pos, read, comps, post)

# ---------------- CHAIN: representation of Edge-Seepage ----------------
# Latent positions X̂ = {0, 1, 2, 3}; existence variables e_x ∈ {0,1} and depths d_x ∈ [0, CAP]; the signal 'S' injects 1/2 into the first position of the existing chain that is not full;
# when position x is full (d_x = CAP) and the signal continues, x+1 is generated if it does not exist (e_{x+1} ← 1): a new Structural Position is generated along the edge when full (minimal representation of Equation 10 of The Mathematical Form of the Field).
CAP = F(1)

def chain_positions():
    return [("d", x) for x in range(4)] + [("e", x) for x in range(4)]

def chain_zero():
    z = {p: F(0) for p in chain_positions()}
    z[("e", 0)] = F(1)
    return z

def chain_read(d, r, s):
    present = [x for x in range(4) if d[("e", x)] == 1]
    # Activity: the passage depth of signal S (which position it enters) and the outflow reading of each existing position (the deeper d_x, the easier the outflow: reading = d_x)
    if s == "R":
        return {"out": tuple(d[("d", x)] if x in present else None for x in range(4))}
    tgt = next((x for x in present if d[("d", x)] < CAP), None)
    return {"tgt": tgt, "out": tuple(d[("d", x)] if x in present else None for x in range(4))}

def chain_comps(d, y, s):
    cs = {}
    if s == "S":
        tgt = y["tgt"]
        if tgt is not None:
            room = CAP - d[("d", tgt)]
            cs[("d", tgt)] = [("FILL", min(F(1, 2), room))]
        else:
            last = max(x for x in range(4) if d[("e", x)] == 1)
            if last + 1 < 4:
                cs[("e", last + 1)] = [("BIRTH", F(1))]
    return cs

def make_chain():
    from jxs_core import Host
    return Host(chain_positions(), chain_read, chain_comps)

# ---------------- LOOP: γ1 self-loop toy (sign law) ----------------
# A single γ1 subset depth s ∈ [0, 1]; MBON drive m = 2(1 − s), M fires ⇔ m ≥ θ_M; DAN drive = c + u·[M fires], D fires ⇔ ≥ θ_D;
# unreinforced CS+ beat: if D fires, write +A (depression deepens, clipped at 1); otherwise only shallowing by ×(1 − ρ).
def loop_step(s, u, c=F(1, 2), thM=F(1), thD=F(1, 2), A=F(1, 4), rho=F(1, 10)):
    m = 2 * (1 - s)
    gM = 1 if m >= thM else 0
    gD = 1 if c + u * gM >= thD else 0
    s2 = (1 - rho) * s + (A if gD else 0)
    return min(F(1), s2), gM, gD

# ---------------- Conductance network with general boundaries (Theorem 18-9(2)) ----------------
def components(nodes, edges, D):
    adj = {n: set() for n in nodes}
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd > 0:
            adj[u].add(v); adj[v].add(u)
    seen, out = set(), []
    for n in nodes:
        if n in seen:
            continue
        comp, stack = {n}, [n]
        while stack:
            x = stack.pop()
            for yv in adj[x]:
                if yv not in comp:
                    comp.add(yv); stack.append(yv)
        seen |= comp
        out.append(comp)
    return out

def solve_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj):
    """Steady state of the conductance network under general boundaries: fixed = {node: fixed potential}, inj = {node: injection} (free nodes only).
    Solved component by component: a component containing a fixed-potential node is uniquely solvable; a component without one requires the injections within it to sum to 0 (conservation), otherwise ValueError;
    a component without a fixed-potential node sets the potential of its minimal node to 0 (the flow rates do not depend on this gauge)."""
    p = {}
    for comp in components(nodes, edges, D):
        fx = [n for n in comp if n in fixed]
        if not fx:
            if sum(F(inj.get(n, 0)) for n in comp) != 0:
                raise ValueError("sources and sinks of the component are not conserved; the steady state has no solution")
            ref = min(comp, key=str)
            known = {ref: F(0)}
        else:
            known = {n: F(fixed[n]) for n in fx}
        unk = sorted([n for n in comp if n not in known], key=str)
        idx = {n: k for k, n in enumerate(unk)}
        m = len(unk)
        A = [[F(0)] * m for _ in range(m)]
        b = [F(inj.get(n, 0)) for n in unk]
        for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
            if dd <= 0 or u not in comp:
                continue
            for x, yv in ((u, v), (v, u)):
                if x in idx:
                    A[idx[x]][idx[x]] += dd
                    if yv in idx:
                        A[idx[x]][idx[yv]] -= dd
                    else:
                        b[idx[x]] += dd * known[yv]
        for col in range(m):
            piv = next(r for r in range(col, m) if A[r][col] != 0)
            A[col], A[piv] = A[piv], A[col]; b[col], b[piv] = b[piv], b[col]
            for r in range(m):
                if r != col and A[r][col] != 0:
                    fct = A[r][col] / A[col][col]
                    for k in range(col, m):
                        A[r][k] -= fct * A[col][k]
                    b[r] -= fct * b[col]
        p.update(known)
        for n in unk:
            p[n] = b[idx[n]] / A[idx[n]][idx[n]]
    return p

def flows_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj):
    p = solve_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj)
    return [dd * (p[u] - p[v]) if dd > 0 else F(0) for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D)]

def removal_response(nodes, edges, D, fixed, k):
    """Incremental transfer under the same boundary: after the k-th edge e = (u, v) is removed, the increments at fixed-potential nodes stay 0 (homogeneous boundary);
    returns the flow rate on each edge when, among the free nodes, u injects a unit flow and v withdraws a unit flow (injection at a fixed-potential end is absorbed by the boundary and not counted)."""
    u, v = edges[k]
    Dc = list(D); Dc[k] = F(0)
    inj = {}
    if u not in fixed:
        inj[u] = F(1)
    if v not in fixed:
        inj[v] = inj.get(v, F(0)) - 1
    return flows_bc(nodes, edges, Dc, {n: F(0) for n in fixed}, inj)
~~~

A.6  field\_final.py  Upstream frozen program: Appendix A.4 of *The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation* v1.0, byte-for-byte identical to Appendix A.1 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* v1.0 (the F-layer evaluator)

~~~python
"""The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization v1.0, Appendix A: F-layer (finite separable layer) evaluator.
Formal simulation, not certification of material causation.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from math import isfinite
from collections import defaultdict
from fractions import Fraction
Tri = bool | None
def exact(v):
    """Each input number is taken as the exact rational given by its decimal representation; all F-layer arithmetic is carried out exactly over the rationals."""
    return Fraction(repr(v)) if isinstance(v, float) else Fraction(v)
def tri_and(*xs):
    if False in xs:return False
    if None in xs:return None
    return True
def tri_or(xs):
    xs=list(xs)
    if True in xs:return True
    if None in xs:return None
    return False
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Pulse:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; amount:float; external:bool=True; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Process:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; carrier_inside:bool; law:str
    reads:tuple[str,...]=(); coefficient:float=0.; bias:float=0.
    certified_effect:Tri=True; certified_read:Tri=True
    # rule: constant, linear (sum of read sites), bilinear (two sites)
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Link:
    source:str; target:str; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Spec:
    sites:tuple[str,...]; initial:tuple[float,...]; window:tuple[int,...]
    pulses:tuple[Pulse,...]; processes:tuple[Process,...]
    links:tuple[Link,...]=(); identity:Tri=True; trace_history:Tri=True
    closed_graph:bool=True; epsilon:float=1e-9
    declared_T:tuple[str,...]|None=None

def _num(v, nonneg=False):
    # Legal numbers: integers, rationals (Fraction), or finite decimal floats
    if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float, Fraction)): return False
    if isinstance(v, float) and not isfinite(v): return False
    return (not nonneg) or v >= 0
def check(x):
    if not x.sites or len(set(x.sites))!=len(x.sites):raise ValueError('sites')
    if x.declared_T is None or not x.declared_T or len(set(x.declared_T))!=len(x.declared_T) or any(s not in x.sites for s in x.declared_T):raise ValueError('declared T')
    if not x.window or tuple(range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+1))!=x.window:raise ValueError('window')
    if len(x.initial)!=len(x.sites) or not all(_num(z,True) for z in x.initial):raise ValueError('initial')
    if not _num(x.epsilon,True):raise ValueError('epsilon')
    if x.identity not in (True,False,None) or x.trace_history not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('identity')
    names=set()
    for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes):
        if not e.name or e.name in names:raise ValueError('event id')
        names.add(e.name)
        if e.step<x.window[0]-1 or e.step>x.window[-1]:raise ValueError('event time')
        if e.target not in x.sites:raise ValueError('target')
        if isinstance(e,Pulse):
            if not _num(e.amount,True):raise ValueError('pulse amount')
            # In F, certified is the certification of “whether the pulse actually occurs”: 1 = occurs, 0 = does not occur; ⊥ is outside the single-trajectory domain of F and must be enumerated by branch in R
            if e.certified is None:raise ValueError('F_UNDECIDED_PULSE: enumerate occurrence branches in R')
            if e.certified not in (True,False):raise ValueError('pulse evidence')
        else:
            if e.law not in ('constant','linear','bilinear'):raise ValueError('law')
            if any(s not in x.sites for s in e.reads):raise ValueError('read sites')
            if e.law=='bilinear' and len(e.reads)!=2:raise ValueError('bilinear arity')
            if e.law=='linear' and not e.reads:raise ValueError('linear reads')
            if e.law=='constant' and e.reads:raise ValueError('constant reads')
            if not _num(e.coefficient) or not _num(e.bias):raise ValueError('params')
            # D20: the action certification must be decided; a mechanism with undetermined action is outside the F domain (even when its component is zero)
            if e.certified_effect not in (True,False):raise ValueError('undecided mechanism effect')
            if e.certified_read not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('evidence')
    # All trace nodes are snapshots at integer t; process nodes at integer t+0.5.
    # Strict temporal order prevents fabricated same-step read-after-write.
    times={f'state:{s}:{t}':float(t) for s in x.sites for t in range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+2)}
    times.update({e.name:float(e.step)+0.5 for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes)})
    for link in x.links:
        if link.source not in times or link.target not in times:raise ValueError('link endpoint')
        if times[link.source]>=times[link.target]:raise ValueError('noncausal link')
        if link.certified not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('link evidence')
    return times
def path_status(links,starts,target,closed=True):
    adjacency=defaultdict(list)
    for l in links:adjacency[l.source].append(l)
    def reachable(allow):
        seen=set(starts);queue=list(starts)
        while queue:
            u=queue.pop()
            for l in adjacency[u]:
                if allow(l.certified) and l.target not in seen:
                    seen.add(l.target);queue.append(l.target)
        return target in seen
    if reachable(lambda w:w is True):return True
    if reachable(lambda w:w is not False):return None
    return False if closed else None
def run(x):
    check(x)
    eps=exact(x.epsilon)
    state={s:exact(v) for s,v in zip(x.sites,x.initial)}
    # For each site, certified external history must be identified as event, not inferred from nonzero state.
    formation=defaultdict(list); history_snapshots=defaultdict(list)
    signal_marks=defaultdict(list)  # out-of-boundary cause: changes left at this position by certified, effective out-of-boundary action components (pulses, or processes whose carrier is outside the boundary)
    part=[];hdyn=[];reports=[]
    for t in range(x.window[0]-1,x.window[-1]+1):
        before=dict(state); contributions=defaultdict(list);events=[]
        # Pulse first as an event, but processes read the prior snapshot, not pulse-modified value.
        for p in x.pulses:
            if p.step!=t:continue
            # Count only pulses certified as occurring and effective; a pulse confirmed not to occur contributes nothing and registers no formation or cause
            a=exact(p.amount)
            if p.certified is True and a>eps:
                contributions[p.target].append((p.name,a))
                formation[p.target].append((p.name,t,p.external))
                if p.external:signal_marks[p.target].append((p.name,t))
        for m in x.processes:
            if m.step!=t:continue
            k=exact(m.coefficient); b=exact(m.bias)
            if m.law=='constant':value=b
            elif m.law=='linear':value=b+k*sum(before[s] for s in m.reads)
            else:value=b+k*before[m.reads[0]]*before[m.reads[1]]
            is_action=tri_and(abs(value)>eps,m.certified_effect)
            # Declared reads do not establish a genuine dependence: compute the law's support.
            support=()
            # Support for the organizational condition: the numerical law depends on the read positions, or the process is a pure read (component not an effective change, |c| ≤ ε); read certification enters as a separate conjunct, and an undetermined value is not truncated to false in advance
            # Functional support is decided by a mathematically nonzero partial derivative (coefficient and partner reading exactly nonzero), not conflated with the effective-change threshold ε
            if m.law=='linear' and (k!=0 or abs(value)<=eps): support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            # For every element in the declared T, historical formation must precede the read.
            active_T=set(x.declared_T)
            available=[s for s in support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            is_cond=tri_and(bool(available),m.certified_read)
            # No automatic path from a read-site to an act. Explicit event links are required.
            old_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in available]
            route=path_status(x.links,old_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if old_nodes else False
            # Action provenance must be supported by a nonzero functional dependence,
            # not merely a certified read that has no effect on the action.
            action_support=()
            if m.law=='linear' and k!=0:action_support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                action_support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            action_available=[s for s in action_support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            action_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in action_available]
            action_route=path_status(x.links,action_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if action_nodes else False
            is_hist=tri_and(bool(action_available),m.certified_read,action_route)
            if is_action is True:
                contributions[m.target].append((m.name,value))
                # Formation of a new element: with its carrier inside B it is formation by in-field continuation; with its carrier outside the boundary it is formation by signal
                if value>eps and before[m.target]<=eps:
                    formation[m.target].append((m.name,t,not m.carrier_inside))
                # An effective component of a process whose carrier is outside the boundary is a change left by a signal, whether the element is new or old and whether the component is positive or negative
                if not m.carrier_inside:
                    signal_marks[m.target].append((m.name,t))
            elif is_action is None: # defensive check: an undetermined action certification is already excluded from the domain by check()
                raise ValueError('unresolved effect cannot determine numeric trajectory')
            if t in x.window:
                if m.carrier_inside:part.append(is_cond)
                hdyn.append(tri_and(m.target in active_T,is_action,is_hist))
            events.append({'id':m.name,'delta':value,'act':is_action,'cond':is_cond,'histcond':is_hist,'route':route})
        for s in x.sites:
            new=before[s]+sum(v for _,v in contributions[s])
            # Depth must not be negative: an exact updated value below zero leaves the domain; no truncation to zero, no tolerance
            if new < 0:raise ValueError('negative trace depth')
            state[s]=new
            if state[s]>eps and formation[s]:history_snapshots[s].append(t+1)
        if t in x.window:reports.append({'step':t,'before':before,'after':dict(state),'contributions':dict(contributions),'events':events})
    ts=x.declared_T
    eligible=all(state[s]>eps and formation[s] for s in ts)
    sources={origin for site in ts for origin,t in signal_marks[site]}
    trace=tri_and(bool(sources),eligible,x.identity,x.trace_history)
    pw=tri_or(part); hw=tri_or(hdyn)
    return {'trace':trace,'part':pw,'hdyn':hw,'Q':tri_and(trace,pw,hw),'reports':reports,'physical_certificate':False}
~~~

A.7  k17\_core.py  Library of this paper: one-beat transitions, events and read sets of the binary class 𝒦\_B

~~~python
"""Appendix library k17_core.py of The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits v1.0: one-beat transition of the binary class 𝒦_B, R-layer events and read sets, and paths in the actual event graph.
Python standard library only; all numerical values are fractions.Fraction.
𝒦_B (Definition 19-0 in the main text): on top of Definitions 5 and 7 (𝒦_PA^3, 𝒦_PA^≺) of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization v1.0,
  (1) the relaxation coefficients ρ_ij are declared per Structural Position (𝒦_PA^≺ uses a single global ρ);
  (2) the gating units for potentiation and depression are declared separately per column, gp[j] (used by PLUS) and gm[j] (used by MINUS); gp = gm gives gate_of of 𝒦_PA^3;
  everything else (entries, gating units, the same-beat directed acyclic firing input U, the beat-wise order, clipping) is unchanged.
Beat-wise order: (1) read in s; (2–3) unit by unit along order (the declared topological order), vt_j = λ_j v_j + Σ_i w_ij s_i + Σ_{k≺j} U_kj g_k + I_j, g_j = [vt_j ≥ θ_j];
  (4) reset; (5) x′ = d_x x + s, y′ = d_y y + g; (6) P⁺_ij = A₊ x′_i when plastic ∧ bap ∧ g_{gp[j]}, P⁻_ij = −A₋ y′_{gm[j]} when plastic ∧ bap ∧ s_i,
  P^r_ij = −ρ_ij (w_ij − b_ij) when plastic; (7) clipping to [lo, hi]; (8) read out g.
Events and read sets (read convention of Definition 19-0 in the main text): the gating read set is the ≺ closure gr_j = {v_j} ∪ {w_ij : s_i = 1} ∪ ⋃_{k: U_kj ≠ 0} gr_k.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
ZERO = Fr(0)
ONE = Fr(1)

def F(v):
    return v if isinstance(v, Fr) else Fr(v)

def clip(a, lo, hi):
    return min(hi, max(lo, a))

class Member:
    """Declared member. units: list of gating-unit names; order: topological order (unit indices); U: {(k, j): coefficient}; gp, gm: the potentiation and depression gating units of each column;
    rho, b: {(i, j): value}; pat: {letter: 0/1 tuple}; Bu, Bi: index sets of the gating units and entry units inside the boundary."""
    def __init__(self, n_in, units, order, U, gp, gm, theta, lam, vres, dx, dy, Ap, Am, rho, lo, hi, b, pat,
                 plastic=True, bap=True, Bu=None, Bi=None, name=''):
        self.n_in = n_in
        self.units = list(units)
        self.n_out = len(units)
        self.order = list(order)
        self.U = {k: F(v) for k, v in U.items() if F(v) != 0}
        self.gp = list(gp)
        self.gm = list(gm)
        self.theta = [F(t) for t in theta]
        self.lam = [F(t) for t in lam]
        self.vres = [F(t) for t in vres]
        self.dx = [F(t) for t in dx]
        self.dy = [F(t) for t in dy]
        self.Ap = F(Ap)
        self.Am = F(Am)
        self.rho = {(i, j): F(rho.get((i, j), 0)) for i in range(n_in) for j in range(self.n_out)}
        self.lo = F(lo)
        self.hi = F(hi)
        self.b = {(i, j): F(b.get((i, j), 0)) for i in range(n_in) for j in range(self.n_out)}
        self.pat = {a: tuple(p) for a, p in pat.items()}
        self.plastic = plastic
        self.bap = bap
        self.Bu = set(range(self.n_out)) if Bu is None else set(Bu)
        self.Bi = set(range(n_in)) if Bi is None else set(Bi)
        self.name = name
        self.validate()
    def validate(self):
        pos = {j: n for n, j in enumerate(self.order)}
        assert sorted(self.order) == list(range(self.n_out)), 'order'
        for (k, j) in self.U:
            assert pos[k] < pos[j], 'firing input must follow the topological order (directed acyclic)'
        for j in range(self.n_out):
            assert 0 <= self.lam[j] <= 1 and 0 <= self.dy[j] <= 1
            assert 0 <= self.gp[j] < self.n_out and 0 <= self.gm[j] < self.n_out
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            assert 0 <= self.dx[i] <= 1
        assert self.Ap >= 0 and self.Am >= 0
        for key, r in self.rho.items():
            assert 0 <= r <= 1
            assert self.lo <= self.b[key] <= self.hi
        assert 'none' in self.pat and all(v == 0 for v in self.pat['none'])
    def init_state(self):
        w = {(i, j): self.b[(i, j)] for i in range(self.n_in) for j in range(self.n_out)}
        return (w, [ZERO] * self.n_out, [ZERO] * self.n_in, [ZERO] * self.n_out)

def copy_state(st):
    w, v, x, y = st
    return (dict(w), list(v), list(x), list(y))

def gate_reads(M, s):
    """Gating read sets under the ≺ closure (determined by the letter and the declared U alone)."""
    gr = {}
    for j in M.order:
        r = {('v', j)} | {('w', i, j) for i in range(M.n_in) if s[i]}
        for (k, jj), u in M.U.items():
            if jj == j:
                r |= gr[k]
        gr[j] = frozenset(r)
    return gr

def car_of_unit(M, j):
    return 'B' if j in M.Bu else 'EXT'

def car_of_pos(M, i, j):
    return 'B' if (j in M.Bu and i in M.Bi) else 'EXT'

def step(M, st, letter, I):
    """One beat. I: injection list of length n_out. Returns (new state, g, vt, ev). ev[(kind, index)] = dict(occ, c, reads, tgt, car)."""
    w, v, x, y = st
    s = M.pat[letter]
    I = [F(t) for t in I]
    vt = [None] * M.n_out
    g = [None] * M.n_out
    for j in M.order:
        val = M.lam[j] * v[j] + sum((w[(i, j)] for i in range(M.n_in) if s[i]), ZERO) + I[j]
        for (k, jj), u in M.U.items():
            if jj == j and g[k]:
                val += u
        vt[j] = val
        g[j] = 1 if val >= M.theta[j] else 0
    v2 = [M.vres[j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(M.n_out)]
    x2 = [M.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(M.n_in)]
    y2 = [M.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(M.n_out)]
    gr = gate_reads(M, s)
    ev = {}
    def add(kind, idx, occ, c, reads, tgt, car):
        ev[(kind,) + tuple(idx)] = dict(occ=bool(occ), c=F(c) if occ else ZERO, reads=frozenset(reads), tgt=tgt, car=car)
    for j in range(M.n_out):
        cu = car_of_unit(M, j)
        add('LEAK', (j,), True, (M.lam[j] - 1) * v[j], {('v', j)}, ('v', j), cu)
        for i in range(M.n_in):
            if s[i]:
                add('SYN', (i, j), True, w[(i, j)], {('w', i, j)}, ('v', j), car_of_pos(M, i, j))
        if I[j] != 0:
            add('INJ', (j,), True, I[j], set(), ('v', j), 'EXT')
        if g[j]:
            add('RESET', (j,), True, M.vres[j] - vt[j], gr[j], ('v', j), cu)
            add('YIN', (j,), True, ONE, gr[j], ('y', j), cu)
        add('YDEC', (j,), True, (M.dy[j] - 1) * y[j], {('y', j)}, ('y', j), cu)
        for (k, jj), u in M.U.items():
            if jj == j:
                add('FIN', (k, j), g[k], u, gr[k], ('v', j), cu)
    for i in range(M.n_in):
        ci = 'B' if i in M.Bi else 'EXT'
        add('XDEC', (i,), True, (M.dx[i] - 1) * x[i], {('x', i)}, ('x', i), ci)
        if s[i]:
            add('XIN', (i,), True, ONE, set(), ('x', i), ci)
    w2 = {}
    pl, bp = M.plastic, M.bap
    for i in range(M.n_in):
        for j in range(M.n_out):
            cp = car_of_pos(M, i, j)
            S = w[(i, j)]
            op = bool(pl and bp and g[M.gp[j]])
            pp = M.Ap * x2[i] if op else ZERO
            om = bool(pl and bp and s[i])
            pm = -M.Am * y2[M.gm[j]] if om else ZERO
            rr = M.rho[(i, j)]
            orr = bool(pl and rr != 0)
            pr = -rr * (w[(i, j)] - M.b[(i, j)]) if pl else ZERO
            S = S + pp + pm + pr
            w2[(i, j)] = clip(S, M.lo, M.hi)
            cc = w2[(i, j)] - S
            tgt = ('w', i, j)
            if op:
                add('PLUS', (i, j), True, pp, gr[M.gp[j]] | {('x', i)}, tgt, cp)
            if om:
                add('MINUS', (i, j), True, pm, gr[M.gm[j]] | {('y', M.gm[j])}, tgt, cp)
            if orr:
                add('RELAX', (i, j), True, pr, {('w', i, j)}, tgt, cp)
            if cc != 0:
                add('CLIP', (i, j), True, cc, gr[M.gp[j]] | gr[M.gm[j]] | {('w', i, j), ('x', i), ('y', M.gm[j])}, tgt, cp)
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2), g, vt, ev

def run(M, st0, seq, u=None, setting=None):
    """seq: [(letter, I)]. Before beat u starts, apply setting ({(i, j): value}) to the Structural Positions. Returns the beat-by-beat records."""
    st = copy_state(st0)
    out = []
    for t, (a, I) in enumerate(seq):
        if u is not None and t == u:
            w, v, x, y = copy_state(st)
            for k, val in (setting or {}).items():
                w[k] = F(val)
            st = (w, v, x, y)
        pre = copy_state(st)
        st, g, vt, ev = step(M, st, a, I)
        out.append(dict(pre=pre, g=g, vt=vt, ev=ev, post=copy_state(st), letter=a, I=list(I)))
    return out

def r1_check(M, rec):
    """Lemma R1′-type closure: pre-beat value + sum of the components directed at that node = post-beat value. Returns the number of mismatches."""
    bad = 0
    pre, post = rec['pre'], rec['post']
    tot = {}
    for e in rec['ev'].values():
        if e['occ']:
            tot[e['tgt']] = tot.get(e['tgt'], ZERO) + e['c']
    w, v, x, y = pre
    w2, v2, x2, y2 = post
    for (i, j) in w:
        if w[(i, j)] + tot.get(('w', i, j), ZERO) != w2[(i, j)]:
            bad += 1
    for j in range(M.n_out):
        if v[j] + tot.get(('v', j), ZERO) != v2[j]:
            bad += 1
        if y[j] + tot.get(('y', j), ZERO) != y2[j]:
            bad += 1
    for i in range(M.n_in):
        if x[i] + tot.get(('x', i), ZERO) != x2[i]:
            bad += 1
    return bad

def path_act(trace, sources, t_e, reads):
    """Whether, on the actual event graph (actually occurring events only; read edges, write edges, persistence edges of Structural Positions), the read set reads (beat t_e) is reachable from sources = {(node, beat)}.
    Same rule as decision.path_act in the appendix of The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts."""
    if not sources:
        return 0
    reach = set()
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    for t in range(t0, t_e + 1):
        reach |= {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        if t == t_e:
            return 1 if (reads & reach) else 0
        nxt = {k for k in reach if k[0] == 'w'}
        for e in trace[t]['ev'].values():
            if e['occ'] and (e['reads'] & reach):
                nxt.add(e['tgt'])
        reach = nxt
    return 0

def slot_reads_tick(M, letter):
    """Slot hypergraph: for a given letter, the pre-beat nodes on which each post-state node depends (the union of the read sets of all event slots that can occur; Structural Positions additionally depend on themselves through persistence)."""
    s = M.pat[letter]
    gr = gate_reads(M, s)
    dep = {}
    def addd(n, r):
        dep.setdefault(n, set()).update(r)
    for j in range(M.n_out):
        addd(('v', j), {('v', j)} | gr[j])
        addd(('y', j), {('y', j)} | gr[j])
    for i in range(M.n_in):
        addd(('x', i), {('x', i)})
        for j in range(M.n_out):
            r = {('w', i, j)}
            if M.plastic and M.bap:
                r |= gr[M.gp[j]] | {('x', i)}
                if s[i]:
                    r |= gr[M.gm[j]] | {('y', M.gm[j])}
            addd(('w', i, j), r)
    return dep

def path_slot(M, seq, sources, t_e, reads):
    """Whether, on the slot hypergraph, the read set of beat t_e is reachable from sources."""
    if not sources:
        return 0
    reach = set()
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    for t in range(t0, t_e + 1):
        reach |= {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        if t == t_e:
            return 1 if (reads & reach) else 0
        dep = slot_reads_tick(M, seq[t][0])
        reach = {n for n, r in dep.items() if r & reach}
    return 0
~~~

A.8  k17\_models.py  Library of this paper: the frozen declared members Φ\_γ, Φ\_γα3^0, Φ\_γα3^±, Φ\_α, Φ\_sr of 𝒦\_B

~~~python
"""Appendix library k17_models.py of The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits v1.0: frozen declared members and declared histories of the binary class 𝒦_B. Parameters are constructed values (dimensionless, rational), not material measurements.
Common conventions: 4 KC entries (CS+ activates A = {0, 1}, CS− activates B = {2, 3}); letters 'A', 'B', 'none';
λ = 0, v_reset = 0, d_x = d_y = 0 (coarse beat: one beat is one odor-presentation slot, longer than the membrane and eligibility time constants);
KC→MBON positions take the depression-depth reading D⁻: baseline b = hi = 1/2, depth s = b − w ∈ [0, 1/2];
KC→DAN positions carry a static drive (the gating unit of their column is Z, which never fires, so there is no potentiation, the depression component is 0, and ρ = 0).
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from k17_core import Member
h = Fr(1, 2)
EPS = Fr(1, 100)
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}
A = (0, 1)
KC = range(4)

def _b(cols):
    """cols: {unit: baseline value at KC→that unit}."""
    return {(i, j): Fr(v) for j, v in cols.items() for i in KC}

def gamma(rho1=Fr(1, 8), cD=Fr(1, 16), u=Fr(-1), thM=Fr(3, 4), thD=h, Am=Fr(1, 8)):
    """Φ_γ: single γ1pedc compartment. Units M = MBON-γ1pedc>α/β (0), D = PPL1-γ1pedc (1), Z (2); U_MD = u < 0 (GABAergic inhibition, Pavlowsky et al. 2018)."""
    b = _b({0: h, 1: cD, 2: 0})
    rho = {(i, 0): rho1 for i in KC}
    return Member(4, ['MBON-g1', 'PPL1-g1', 'Z'], [0, 1, 2], {(0, 1): u}, gp=[1, 2, 2], gm=[1, 2, 2],
                  theta=[thM, thD, 1], lam=[0, 0, 0], vres=[0, 0, 0], dx=[0] * 4, dy=[0] * 3,
                  Ap=0, Am=Am, rho=rho, lo=0, hi=h, b=b, pat=PAT, name='Φ_γ')

def selfrenew(rho1=Fr(1, 8), cD=Fr(1, 4), u=Fr(-1), thM=Fr(3, 4), thD=h, Am=Fr(1, 8)):
    """Φ_sr: inhibitory self-loop; the disinhibited drive suffices to make the DAN fire (c_A ≥ θ_D > c_A + u). Model-layer witness of Theorem 19-5; not declared as any Drosophila compartment."""
    M = gamma(rho1=rho1, cD=cD, u=u, thM=thM, thD=thD, Am=Am)
    M.name = 'Φ_sr'
    return M

def gamma_alpha3(rho1=Fr(1, 8), rho2=Fr(0), c1=Fr(1, 16), c2=Fr(1, 4), u11=Fr(-1), u12=Fr(-1),
                 signed=False, Ap=Fr(1, 16), cm=Fr(1, 8), um=Fr(1), Am=Fr(1, 8), block=False, Bu=None):
    """Φ_γα3: M1 = MBON-γ1pedc>α/β (0), D1 = PPL1-γ1pedc (1), M2 = MBON-α3 (2), D2 = PPL1-α3 (3)[, D2m (4)], Z.
    U_{M1,D1} = u11, U_{M1,D2} = u12 (the fitted weights in Supplementary Table 1 of Huang et al. 2024 are negative). signed=True adds D2m:
    a binary reading of “PPL1-α3 below baseline” (fires when M1 fires and there is odor input), which gates potentiation of the MBON-α3 column (the A₊ component, i.e., the weakening of T₂).
    block=True: all firing outputs of M1 are set to 0 (blockade of MBON-γ1pedc>α/β transmission; law rewriting)."""
    if not signed:
        units = ['MBON-g1', 'PPL1-g1', 'MBON-a3', 'PPL1-a3', 'Z']
        Z = 4
        U = {(0, 1): u11, (0, 3): u12}
        gp = [Z, Z, Z, Z, Z]
        gm = [1, Z, 3, Z, Z]
        theta = [Fr(3, 4), h, Fr(3, 4), h, 1]
        b = _b({0: h, 1: c1, 2: h, 3: c2, 4: 0})
    else:
        units = ['MBON-g1', 'PPL1-g1', 'MBON-a3', 'PPL1-a3', 'PPL1-a3-dip', 'Z']
        Z = 5
        U = {(0, 1): u11, (0, 3): u12, (0, 4): um}
        gp = [Z, Z, 4, Z, Z, Z]
        gm = [1, Z, 3, Z, Z, Z]
        theta = [Fr(3, 4), h, Fr(3, 4), h, 1, 1]
        b = _b({0: h, 1: c1, 2: h, 3: c2, 4: cm, 5: 0})
    if block:
        U = {k: Fr(0) for k in U}
    rho = {}
    for i in KC:
        rho[(i, 0)] = rho1
        rho[(i, 2)] = rho2
    n = len(units)
    return Member(4, units, list(range(n)), U, gp=gp, gm=gm, theta=theta, lam=[0] * n, vres=[0] * n,
                  dx=[0] * 4, dy=[0] * n, Ap=(Ap if signed else 0), Am=Am, rho=rho, lo=0, hi=h, b=b, pat=PAT,
                  Bu=Bu, name='Φ_γα3' + ('^±' if signed else '^0') + ('[blockade]' if block else ''))

def alpha1(regime='I', rho1=Fr(1, 32), cD=Fr(1, 16), u=h, thM=Fr(3, 4), thD=Fr(1), Am=Fr(1, 8)):
    """Φ_α: single α1 compartment. M = MBON-α1 (0), D = PAM-α1 (1), Z (2); U_MD = u > 0 (declared reading of glutamate as excitatory via NMDA receptors, Ichinose et al. 2015).
    regime 'I': teaching alone is not enough to make the DAN fire and must coincide with MBON firing (J + c_A < θ_D ≤ J + c_A + u); 'II': teaching alone is enough."""
    b = _b({0: h, 1: cD, 2: 0})
    rho = {(i, 0): rho1 for i in KC}
    M = Member(4, ['MBON-a1', 'PAM-a1', 'Z'], [0, 1, 2], {(0, 1): u}, gp=[1, 2, 2], gm=[1, 2, 2],
               theta=[thM, thD, 1], lam=[0, 0, 0], vres=[0, 0, 0], dx=[0] * 4, dy=[0] * 3,
               Ap=0, Am=Am, rho=rho, lo=0, hi=h, b=b, pat=PAT, name='Φ_α^' + regime)
    return M

def US(n_out, j, J):
    I = [Fr(0)] * n_out
    I[j] = Fr(J)
    return I

def Z0(n_out):
    return [Fr(0)] * n_out

def hist_gamma(n_out=3, J=Fr(3, 2)):
    """Declared history of χ_γ: pairing ×3 (separated by 1 rest beat), rest for 2 beats, CS+ test, rest, CS− test, rest, CS+ test, rest. W = beats 6–12."""
    P = ('A', US(n_out, 1, J))
    R = ('none', Z0(n_out))
    seq = [P, R, P, R, P, R, R, ('A', Z0(n_out)), R, ('B', Z0(n_out)), R, ('A', Z0(n_out)), R]
    return seq, (6, 12)

def hist_gamma_alpha3(n_out, J1=Fr(3, 2), late=14):
    """Declared history of χ_γα3: pairing ×3 (CS+ paired with shock, I_{D1} = J1; I_{D2} = 0), rest for 1 beat; early non-reinforced CS+ (beat 6), rest, CS−, rest;
    after a long rest, late non-reinforced CS+ (beat 9 + late), rest. W = beat 5 to the last beat."""
    P = ('A', US(n_out, 1, J1))
    R = ('none', Z0(n_out))
    seq = [P, R, P, R, P, R, ('A', Z0(n_out)), R, ('B', Z0(n_out))] + [R] * late + [('A', Z0(n_out)), R]
    return seq, (5, len(seq) - 1)

def hist_alpha1(n_out=3, J=h):
    """Declared history of χ_α: pairing ×4 (separated by 1 beat), rest for 2 beats, CS+ test, rest, CS− test, rest. W = beats 0–12 (training included; the declared W covers training through test)."""
    P = ('A', US(n_out, 1, J))
    R = ('none', Z0(n_out))
    seq = [P, R, P, R, P, R, P, R, R, ('A', Z0(n_out)), R, ('B', Z0(n_out)), R]
    return seq, (0, 12)

def hist_selfrenew(n_out=3, J=Fr(3, 2), gaps=(2, 2, 2, 2)):
    """Declared history of Φ_sr: pairing ×3, then CS+ re-exposures separated by the numbers of rest beats listed in gaps. W = from the first re-exposure to the last beat."""
    P = ('A', US(n_out, 1, J))
    R = ('none', Z0(n_out))
    seq = [P, R, P, R, P]
    t0 = None
    for gp in gaps:
        seq += [R] * gp
        if t0 is None:
            t0 = len(seq)
        seq.append(('A', Z0(n_out)))
    seq.append(R)
    return seq, (t0, len(seq) - 1)
~~~

A.9  qs19\_models.py  Library of this paper: the graded class 𝒩₃F (Graded3F), the cerebellar comparison (Cereb), the consolidation window (Consol), KCAdapter, to\_jxs, and the frozen candidates of Theorem 19-D (WindowCand)

~~~python
"""Appendix library qs19_models.py of The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits v1.0: the declared host models of this paper. All parameters are constructed values (dimensionless rationals), not material measurements.
Standard library only; every numeric value is a fractions.Fraction; no random numbers (random members are generated by qs19_run.py with a fixed seed and passed in as parameters).
Interface: [17] appendix jhc_core.Host (X, lo, hi, ground, orient, init, grid, act(w, s), comps(w, y, s) -> [(process, position, component)]),
      used for the three-way split, depth, organization hypergraph, Definition 6′ certificate and two-branch report of [17];
      to_jxs(host) rewrites the same model as [18] appendix jxs_core.Host (positions, read, comps -> {position: [(process, component)]}, post),
      used for the tendency, Act/Aff, the two resolution semantics and the run of [18]. Both interfaces read the same set of law functions; the laws are not written twice.
Models (for the corresponding clauses of the main text, see the docstring of each class):
  Graded3F      the graded class 𝒩₃F of compartmentalized third-factor circuits (main text Definition 19-1, Theorems 19-4, 19-7′, 19-H):
                truncated-linear MBON read-out, truncated-linear DAN (with return U), write = η·DAN (unsigned) or η·(DAN − θ*) (signed), position-wise relaxation, clipping to [0, 1].
  Cereb         comparison member for LTD at the cerebellar parallel-fiber–Purkinje synapse (main text 4.7, cross-host comparison): truncated-linear PC, PC ⊣ DCN ⊣ IO (climbing fiber),
                LTD ∝ CF, LTP when a parallel fiber is presented alone. Serves only as a cross-host structural comparison.
  Consol        consolidation-window model (main text Theorem 19-10): α-compartment depths a_i, law-layer components c_i (consolidation state); in-window DAN activity is
                'perm' (permissive, independent of the Trace-Response), 'inh' (reads the Trace-Response through inhibitory return) or 'exc' (reads the Trace-Response through excitatory return).
  KCAdapter     rewrites a member of the binary class 𝒦_B (k17_core.Member, coarse beat λ = 0, d_x = d_y = 0) as a host of the [18] run system
                (the cross-check in main text 3.3): the complete state contains only the depths d = b − w at KC→unit positions.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from jhc_core import Host
F = Fr
ZERO = Fr(0)

def relu(x):
    return x if x > 0 else ZERO

class Graded3F(Host):
    """Declared member of 𝒩₃F. comps_names: list of compartment names; KC: number of KC entries; odors: {signal prefix: KC subset};
    theta[c]: MBON truncation threshold; beta[c]: DAN baseline drive; U[(c', c)]: return coefficient MBON_c' → DAN_c; us[c]: injection of the reinforcement signal into DAN_c;
    eta[c]: write rate; rho[c]: per-beat relaxation fraction; signed[c]: zero point θ*_c for signed writing (None means unsigned writing);
    orient: 'dep' (depression type: read-out falls with depth) or 'pot' (potentiation type: read-out rises with depth); bg: activation of each KC under the background signal 'bg' (spontaneous activity in the odor-free period).
    Signals: 'rest', odor names, odor name + '+' (paired with reinforcement), 'bg'. Positions are named 'compartment:KC'."""
    def __init__(self, comps_names, KC, odors, theta, beta, U, us, eta, rho, signed=None, orient='dep', bg=ZERO,
                 name='Graded3F', grid=None):
        self.name = name
        self.C = list(comps_names)
        self.KC = list(range(KC))
        self.odors = {k: tuple(v) for k, v in odors.items()}
        self.theta = {c: F(theta[c]) for c in self.C}
        self.beta = {c: F(beta[c]) for c in self.C}
        self.U = {k: F(v) for k, v in U.items()}
        self.us = {c: F(us.get(c, 0)) for c in self.C}
        self.eta = {c: F(eta[c]) for c in self.C}
        self.rho = {c: F(rho[c]) for c in self.C}
        self.signed = {c: (None if (signed is None or signed.get(c) is None) else F(signed[c])) for c in self.C}
        self.orient_kind = orient
        self.bgv = F(bg)
        self.X = ['%s:%d' % (c, i) for c in self.C for i in self.KC]
        self.lo = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
        self.grid = grid
    def kc_act(self, s):
        """Activation of each KC under signal s (0, 1 or the background value)."""
        if s == 'bg':
            return {i: self.bgv for i in self.KC}
        base = s[:-1] if s.endswith('+') else s
        K = self.odors.get(base, ())
        return {i: (F(1) if i in K else ZERO) for i in self.KC}
    def act(self, w, s):
        a = self.kc_act(s)
        y = {}
        for c in self.C:
            if self.orient_kind == 'dep':
                drive = sum((a[i] * (1 - w['%s:%d' % (c, i)]) for i in self.KC), ZERO)
            else:
                drive = sum((a[i] * (1 + w['%s:%d' % (c, i)]) for i in self.KC), ZERO)
            y['MBON_' + c] = relu(drive - self.theta[c])
        shock = s.endswith('+')
        for c in self.C:
            v = self.beta[c] + (self.us[c] if shock else ZERO)
            for (c1, c2), u in self.U.items():
                if c2 == c:
                    v += u * y['MBON_' + c1]
            y['DAN_' + c] = relu(v)
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        a = self.kc_act(s)
        out = []
        for c in self.C:
            dan = y['DAN_' + c]
            drive = dan if self.signed[c] is None else dan - self.signed[c]
            for i in self.KC:
                x = '%s:%d' % (c, i)
                if a[i] != 0:
                    out.append(('W_' + c, x, self.eta[c] * a[i] * drive))
                if self.rho[c] != 0:
                    out.append(('R_' + c, x, -self.rho[c] * w[x]))
        return out

class Cereb(Host):
    """Cerebellar comparison member: position 'pf:i' is the LTD depth of the parallel fiber i → Purkinje cell synapse (depression type).
    PC = max(0, Σ_{K(s)} (1 − d_i) + p0 − θ_P); DCN = max(0, n0 − κ·PC); CF (inferior olive) = max(0, o0 − λ·DCN + us·[reinforcement]);
    Write: at each position in K(s), an LTD component η·CF and an LTP component −η_p (depth decrease; it is the net write when a parallel fiber is presented alone and CF = 0); no relaxation.
    Effective return sign u_eff = (−κ)·(−λ) = κλ > 0 (net excitation through two inhibitory stages)."""
    def __init__(self, KC=4, odors=None, p0=F(0), thP=F(0), n0=F(2), kappa=F(1, 2), o0=F(1, 2), lam=F(1, 2), us=F(1),
                 eta=F(1, 8), eta_p=F(1, 32), name='Cereb'):
        self.name = name
        self.KC = list(range(KC))
        self.odors = odors or {'A': (0, 1), 'B': (2, 3)}
        self.p0, self.thP, self.n0, self.kappa, self.o0, self.lam, self.us = p0, thP, n0, kappa, o0, lam, us
        self.eta, self.eta_p = eta, eta_p
        self.X = ['pf:%d' % i for i in self.KC]
        self.lo = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
        self.grid = None
    def K(self, s):
        return self.odors.get(s[:-1] if s.endswith('+') else s, ())
    def act(self, w, s):
        K = self.K(s)
        pc = relu(sum((1 - w['pf:%d' % i] for i in K), ZERO) + self.p0 - self.thP)
        dcn = relu(self.n0 - self.kappa * pc)
        cf = relu(self.o0 - self.lam * dcn + (self.us if s.endswith('+') else ZERO))
        return {'PC': pc, 'DCN': dcn, 'CF': cf}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for i in self.K(s):
            x = 'pf:%d' % i
            out.append(('LTD', x, self.eta * y['CF']))
            out.append(('LTP', x, -self.eta_p))
        return out

class Consol(Host):
    """Consolidation-window model: positions 'a:i' (depression depth of KC i in the α compartment) and 'c:i' (law-layer component: consolidation state, 0 or 1).
    Read-out: MBON_α = Σ_i act_i·(1 − a_i); in-window DAN_w:
      'perm': DAN_w = κ (independent of the Trace-Response; maintained, e.g., by an oscillation or an external drive independent of T);
      'inh': DAN_w = max(0, κ + u·MBON_α), u < 0 (inhibitory return: the deeper the Trace-Response, the weaker MBON and the stronger DAN);
      'exc': DAN_w = max(0, κ + u·MBON_α), u > 0 (excitatory return).
    Under the background signal 'bg' each KC has activation bg; the training signal 'A+' writes only a (η·[i ∈ K(A)]), not c.
    Consolidation write CONS@c:i: under the window signal 'bg', when DAN_w ≥ θ_c, a_i > tag and c_i = 0, the component is +1 (the law layer is set).
    Relaxation R@a:i: −(1 − ρ(c_i))·a_i, ρ(0) = rho0, ρ(1) = rho1 (the consolidation state rewrites the shallowing law, following [18] D18-11).
    block=True: blockade of DAN output in the window (DAN_w reads as 0).
    theta_M: truncation threshold of MBON_α, MBON_α = max(0, Σ_i act_i·(1 − a_i) − θ_M); default 0 (Σ is always ≥ 0, so the truncation has no effect)."""
    def __init__(self, mode='perm', KC=4, K_A=(0, 1), kappa=F(1, 2), u=ZERO, bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(1, 2), tag=F(1, 20),
                 eta=F(1, 2), rho0=F(1, 2), rho1=F(9, 10), block=False, name=None, theta_M=ZERO):
        self.mode, self.KC, self.K_A = mode, list(range(KC)), tuple(K_A)
        self.kappa, self.u, self.bgv, self.theta_c, self.tag = F(kappa), F(u), F(bg), F(theta_c), F(tag)
        self.eta, self.rho0, self.rho1, self.block = F(eta), F(rho0), F(rho1), block
        self.theta_M = F(theta_M)
        self.name = name or ('Consol_' + mode + ('_blockade' if block else ''))
        self.X = ['a:%d' % i for i in self.KC] + ['c:%d' % i for i in self.KC]
        self.lo = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(1) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
        self.grid = None
    def kc(self, s):
        if s == 'bg':
            return {i: self.bgv for i in self.KC}
        if s in ('A', 'A+'):
            return {i: (F(1) if i in self.K_A else ZERO) for i in self.KC}
        return {i: ZERO for i in self.KC}
    def act(self, w, s):
        k = self.kc(s)
        m = relu(sum((k[i] * (1 - w['a:%d' % i]) for i in self.KC), ZERO) - self.theta_M)
        if s != 'bg':
            dw = ZERO
        elif self.mode == 'perm':
            dw = self.kappa
        else:
            dw = relu(self.kappa + self.u * m)
        if self.block:
            dw = ZERO
        return {'MBON_a': m, 'DAN_w': dw}
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = []
        for i in self.KC:
            a, c = 'a:%d' % i, 'c:%d' % i
            if s == 'A+' and i in self.K_A:
                out.append(('W', a, self.eta))
            rho = self.rho1 if w[c] >= 1 else self.rho0
            out.append(('R', a, -(1 - rho) * w[a]))
            if s == 'bg' and y['DAN_w'] >= self.theta_c and w[a] > self.tag and w[c] < 1:
                out.append(('CONS', c, F(1)))
        return out

def to_jxs(host):
    """Rewrites a host with the jhc interface as [18] appendix jxs_core.Host: same positions; read is act; comps are grouped by position into {position: [(process, component)]};
    post is clipping to [lo, hi] (position-wise post-processing, LP)."""
    from jxs_core import Host as JHost
    def read(d, r, s):
        return host.act(d, s)
    def comps(d, y, s):
        cs = {}
        for (p, x, c) in host.comps(d, y, s):
            cs.setdefault(x, []).append((p, c))
        return cs
    def post(dn, do):
        return {x: min(host.hi[x], max(host.lo[x], dn[x])) for x in dn}
    return JHost(host.X, read, comps, post)

class KCAdapter:
    """Independent rewrite of a coarse-beat member of the binary class 𝒦_B (λ = 0, v_reset = 0, d_x = d_y = 0) in the [18] run system:
    complete state d[(i, j)] = b_ij − w_ij; a signal is (letter, injection tuple); the read-out computes the gate g_j of each unit in topological order;
    write components are given process by process per [18] Equation 4.2: PLUS (depth −A₊·x′_i, gated by g_{gp(j)}), MINUS (depth +A₋·y′_{gm(j)}, when the KC is active),
    RELAX (depth −ρ_ij·d_ij); post clips w to [lo, hi] (i.e., clips d to [b − hi, b − lo])."""
    def __init__(self, M):
        self.M = M
        for j in range(M.n_out):
            assert M.lam[j] == 0 and M.dy[j] == 0
        for i in range(M.n_in):
            assert M.dx[i] == 0
        self.positions = [(i, j) for i in range(M.n_in) for j in range(M.n_out)]
    def gates(self, d, s):
        M = self.M
        letter, I = s
        pat = M.pat[letter]
        g = {}
        for j in M.order:
            val = sum(((M.b[(i, j)] - d[(i, j)]) for i in range(M.n_in) if pat[i]), ZERO) + F(I[j])
            for (k, jj), u in M.U.items():
                if jj == j and g[k]:
                    val += u
            g[j] = 1 if val >= M.theta[j] else 0
        return g
    def host(self):
        from jxs_core import Host as JHost
        M = self
        def read(d, r, s):
            g = M.gates(d, s)
            return {('g', j): g[j] for j in g}
        def comps(d, y, s):
            Mm = M.M
            pat = Mm.pat[s[0]]
            cs = {}
            for (i, j) in M.positions:
                lst = []
                if Mm.plastic and Mm.bap and y[('g', Mm.gp[j])]:
                    lst.append(('PLUS', -Mm.Ap * pat[i]))
                if Mm.plastic and Mm.bap and pat[i]:
                    lst.append(('MINUS', Mm.Am * y[('g', Mm.gm[j])]))
                if Mm.plastic and Mm.rho[(i, j)] != 0:
                    lst.append(('RELAX', -Mm.rho[(i, j)] * d[(i, j)]))
                if lst:
                    cs[(i, j)] = lst
            return cs
        def post(dn, do):
            Mm = M.M
            return {k: min(Mm.b[k] - Mm.lo, max(Mm.b[k] - Mm.hi, v)) for k, v in dn.items()}
        return JHost(self.positions, read, comps, post)
    def zero(self):
        return {p: ZERO for p in self.positions}

class WindowCand:
    """Frozen candidates of Discrimination Theorem 19-D (DAN reading within the consolidation window). Same host, same window signal bg, same background activation vector b (one value per KC).
    Candidate 'P' (permissive): DAN_w(d) = Q(κ); candidate 'R' (return type, return coefficient u ≠ 0): DAN_w(d) = Q(κ + u·R(Σ_i b_i(1 − d_i) − θ_M)).
    R and Q are truncated linear, max(0, ·). Observation: the difference between the two arms (training d^T, sham training d^S), Δ = DAN_w(d^T) − DAN_w(d^S)."""
    def __init__(self, kind, kappa, u=ZERO, b=(), theta_M=ZERO):
        assert kind in ('P', 'R')
        self.kind, self.kappa, self.u, self.b, self.theta_M = kind, F(kappa), F(u), tuple(F(x) for x in b), F(theta_M)
    def mbon(self, d):
        return relu(sum((bi * (1 - di) for bi, di in zip(self.b, d)), ZERO) - self.theta_M)
    def dan_input(self, d):
        return self.kappa if self.kind == 'P' else self.kappa + self.u * self.mbon(d)
    def dan(self, d):
        return relu(self.dan_input(d))
    def delta(self, dT, dS):
        return self.dan(dT) - self.dan(dS)
~~~

A.10  qs19\_run.py  Run entry: checks Q0–Q12

~~~python
"""The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits v1.0, appendix program qs19_run.py: run entry.
Run: python3 qs19_run.py (in the same directory as the other files of Appendix A); prints “pass” or “fail” for each item and a summary at the end; the exit code is 1 if any item fails.
Sections: Q0 SHA-256 of the program files and of the frozen upstream programs; Q1 finding the Trace-Responses; Q2 single Trace-Responses; Q3 set organization; Q4 formation of tendency; Q5 tendency carried out as activity;
    Q6 activity acting again on the set; Q7 dynamics types and verification; Q8 Theorems 19-5, 19-6 and 19-7 on the binary class 𝒦_B; Q9 in-model verification of the quantitative predictions;
    Q10–Q12 boundary examples for each clause (both sides).
Discipline: standard library only; all numbers are fractions.Fraction (decimals only for display); random members are generated by random.Random with fixed seeds;
      the output contains no timings or absolute paths and is byte-for-byte reproducible. The finite verifications cover only the generated domain; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
The frozen upstream programs (jhc_core, jhc_models, jhc_bridge, jxs_core, jxs_models, field_final) are byte-for-byte identical to the appendices of the upstream main texts; this program only calls them."""
import hashlib, os, random, sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
RES = []

def check(tag, cond):
    RES.append(bool(cond))
    print(('  pass  ' if cond else '  fail  ') + tag, flush=True)

def sec(t):
    print('\n' + t, flush=True)

def fs(x):
    x = F(x)
    return str(x.numerator) if x.denominator == 1 else '%d/%d' % (x.numerator, x.denominator)

def dec(x, n=4):
    return ('%.' + str(n) + 'f') % float(x)

def sha(p):
    with open(p, 'rb') as f:
        return hashlib.sha256(f.read()).hexdigest()

print('The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits v1.0, appendix program qs19_run.py')
# ======================================================================
sec('Q0  SHA-256 of the program files; the frozen upstream programs match the values listed in the appendices of their upstream main texts')
UPSTREAM = {
    'jhc_core.py': ('792266771224a7403d8405c4d1c6e0b1dcd06d2ff057e2e14ee3567f76269edd', 'The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields v1.0, Appendix A.1'),
    'jhc_models.py': ('b61606394135717f1428c68d0d694d49dd70e5f74f1cca34232cf5196731674e', 'The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields v1.0, Appendix A.2'),
    'jhc_bridge.py': ('9f821296023fe152b51a2d1c377e5fa1abc96347300338e220af7c296a47f13e', 'The Adjudication Form for Identifying Concretized Fields v1.0, Appendix A.3'),
    'jxs_core.py': ('69614a9f07b390075b2d2dd7c340265812e6ff276e3cd6c8b2f685f28a6f3580', 'The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation v1.0, Appendix A.1'),
    'jxs_models.py': ('42b47a80fa09c54f951c6359d2306a9f16436b9d16b02c2a5bcdb49ee78d8537', 'The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation v1.0, Appendix A.2'),
    'field_final.py': ('dfd11fe6866a508542363f9d32bd3bcf1e2f2917445ad61b8fcda78ab859acd1', 'The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation v1.0, Appendix A.4 = The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization v1.0, Appendix A.1'),
}
for fn, (h, where) in UPSTREAM.items():
    got = sha(os.path.join(HERE, fn))
    check('%s (%s) SHA-256 %s… %s' % (fn, where, got[:8], 'match' if got == h else 'mismatch'), got == h)
~~~
