~~~text
# cross-check: the independent rewrite (KCAdapter) of the coarse-beat members of the binary class 𝒦_B in the [18] run system agrees beat by beat with k17_core.step
rng = random.Random(19001)
mism, beats, gate_mism = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(120):
    kind = rng.choice(['g', 'ga3', 'ga3s', 'a1'])
    if kind == 'g':
        M = KM.gamma(rho1=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 2)]), cD=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 4)]),
                     u=rng.choice([F(-1), F(-1, 2)]), Am=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)]))
    elif kind == 'ga3':
        M = KM.gamma_alpha3(rho1=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)]), rho2=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 64), F(1, 2), F(1)]),
                            Am=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8)]))
    elif kind == 'ga3s':
        M = KM.gamma_alpha3(signed=True, rho2=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8)]), Ap=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8)]))
    else:
        M = KM.alpha1(regime=rng.choice(['I', 'II']), rho1=rng.choice([F(1, 32), F(1, 8)]))
    AD = BM.KCAdapter(M)
    H = AD.host()
    st = M.init_state()
    d = AD.zero()
    for t in range(25):
        letter = rng.choice(['A', 'A', 'B', 'none'])
        J = rng.choice([F(0), F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)])
        I = [F(0)] * M.n_out
        I[1] = J
        st2, g, vt, ev = KC.step(M, st, letter, I)
        y, d2, _, rec = XC.step(H, d, None, (letter, tuple(I)))
        beats += 1
        if any(M.b[k] - st2[0][k] != d2[k] for k in AD.positions):
            mism += 1
        if any(y[('g', j)] != g[j] for j in range(M.n_out)):
            gate_mism += 1
        st, d = st2, d2
check('Q2e cross-check: 120 coarse-beat members of the binary class 𝒦_B × 25 beats (%d beats); the post-states of the [18] step (original-mechanism semantics, LP clipping) disagree with k17_core.step on %d beats, the gating on %d beats' %
      (beats, mism, gate_mism), mism == 0 and gate_mism == 0)
# Theorem 19-2 (three-way split for a single Trace-Response, stated per [18] D18-8): the write component occurs ⇔ x ∈ Act^ε(MINUS); deeper than the no-write control; actual net deepening ⇔ Δd_x > 0
rng = random.Random(19002)
n_beats, bad, cnt = 0, 0, {'write': 0, 'deeper than control': 0, 'net deepening': 0, 'written without net deepening': 0}
for trial in range(400):
    rho2 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 64), F(1, 8), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    Am = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    M = KM.gamma_alpha3(rho1=rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)]), rho2=rho2, Am=Am)
    AD = BM.KCAdapter(M)
    H = AD.host()
    d = AD.zero()
    hist = [('A', (0, F(3, 2), 0, 0, 0))] * rng.choice([2, 3, 4]) + [('none', (0,) * 5)] * rng.randrange(0, 6)
    hist += [('A', (0,) * 5)]
    for s in hist[:-1]:
        _, d, _, _ = XC.step(H, d, None, s)
    s = hist[-1]
    y, d2, _, rec = XC.step(H, d, None, s)
    x = (0, 2)
    lst = rec['comps'].get(x, [])
    minus = sum(c for p, c in lst if p == 'MINUS')
    others = sum(c for p, c in lst if p != 'MINUS')
    post = H.post
    with_w = post({x: d[x] + minus + others}, None)[x]
    without = post({x: d[x] + others}, None)[x]
    s2, hi = d[x], M.b[x] - M.lo
    n_beats += 1
    wr = minus != 0
    deeper = with_w > without
    net = d2[x] > d[x]
    pred_deeper = wr and (1 - rho2) * s2 < hi
    pred_net = wr and s2 < hi and Am > rho2 * s2
    if (wr != (x in rec['Act0'] and minus != 0)) or deeper != pred_deeper or net != pred_net or net != (x in rec['Aff0'] and d2[x] > d[x]):
        bad += 1
    cnt['write'] += wr
    cnt['deeper than control'] += deeper
    cnt['net deepening'] += net
    cnt['written without net deepening'] += (wr and not net)
check('Q2f the three-way split of Theorem 19-2 on [18] run records: on the unreinforced CS+ beats of 400 random Φ_γα3-type members, “write component occurs” %d, “deeper than the no-write control” %d, “actual net deepening” %d, '
      '“written without net deepening” (Act ⊄ Aff) %d; mismatches with the criteria (1 − ρ₂)s₂ < hi and s₂ < hi ∧ A₋ > ρ₂s₂: %d' %
      (cnt['write'], cnt['deeper than control'], cnt['net deepening'], cnt['written without net deepening'], bad), bad == 0 and cnt['written without net deepening'] > 0)
~~~

~~~python
sec('Q3  Step 3  Set organization: overlap, compartments, convergence and intervention between subsets ([17] Theorem 17-4′; [18] D18-4, D18-12, Theorem 18-9; Proposition 19-3)')

def org_after(host, hist, probes):
    S_, R_, pos_, neg_, sig_ = JC.trace_candidates(host, hist, EPS)
    hy_, cps_ = JC.org_graph_exact(host, S_[-1], pos_, probes, EPS)
    return pos_, hy_, cps_

p3b, hy3b, c3b = org_after(JM.Fly('full'), ['A+', 'B+', 'rest', 'rest'], ['A', 'B'])
p3c, hy3c, c3c = org_after(JM.Fly('full', overlap=True), ['A+', 'B+', 'rest', 'rest'], ['A', 'B'])
check('Q3b overlap determines the organization boundary: %d components with disjoint KCs, %d with overlapping KCs' % (len(c3b), len(c3c)), len(c3b) == 2 and len(c3c) == 1)

ov1 = keff(dd, 'A', 'g1') & keff(dd, 'C', 'g1')
~~~

~~~text
sec('Q4  Step 4  Historical dynamics forms tendency: the tendency received at the own position, the direction law and cross-compartment bearing ([18] D18-6, D18-13, Theorem 18-5 (3); Theorem 19-4)')
# Theorem 19-4 (graded sign law): random single-compartment members; compare the write components on an unreinforced CS+ beat at two common depths s < s′
rng = random.Random(19004)
viol, strict = 0, {('dep', -1): 0, ('dep', 1): 0, ('pot', -1): 0, ('pot', 1): 0}
plateau = 0
for trial in range(600):
    orient = rng.choice(['dep', 'pot'])
    u = rng.choice([F(-1), F(-1, 2), F(-1, 4), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    th = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    be = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    sg_ = rng.choice([None, F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    Gm = BM.Graded3F(['c'], 4, {'A': (0, 1)}, {'c': th}, {'c': be}, {('c', 'c'): u}, {'c': F(1)},
                     {'c': rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])}, {'c': F(0)}, signed={'c': sg_}, orient=orient)
    a_, b_ = sorted(rng.sample([F(k, 16) for k in range(17)], 2))
    wa, wb = at(Gm, {'c:0': a_, 'c:1': a_}), at(Gm, {'c:0': b_, 'c:1': b_})
    ca = JC.total_comp(Gm, wa, 'A', 'W_c', 'c:0')
    cb = JC.total_comp(Gm, wb, 'A', 'W_c', 'c:0')
    dirn = (cb > ca) - (cb < ca)
    expect = (-1 if u > 0 else 1) if orient == 'dep' else (1 if u > 0 else -1)
    if dirn == 0:
        plateau += 1
    elif dirn != expect:
        viol += 1
    else:
        strict[(orient, 1 if u > 0 else -1)] += 1
check('Q4a Theorem 19-4 (graded sign law): on 600 random pairs of members and depths, the direction of the write in depth = m(ψ)·m(w); cases contrary to “depression type −sgn u, potentiation type +sgn u”: %d; '
      'strict witnesses (depression u<0 continued writing %d, depression u>0 self-limiting %d, potentiation u<0 self-limiting %d, potentiation u>0 continued writing %d), plateau (zero change) %d' %
      (viol, strict[('dep', -1)], strict[('dep', 1)], strict[('pot', -1)], strict[('pot', 1)], plateau),
      viol == 0 and all(v > 0 for v in strict.values()))
# cross-check of the binary limit against the [18] γ1 self-loop toy LOOP (the three implementations agree in direction)
agree = True
for u in (F(-1), F(1, 2)):
    for s_lo, s_hi in ((F(0), F(3, 4)), (F(1, 10), F(9, 10))):
        _, gM_lo, gD_lo = XM.loop_step(s_lo, u)
        _, gM_hi, gD_hi = XM.loop_step(s_hi, u)
        loop_dir = gD_hi - gD_lo
        if (u < 0 and loop_dir < 0) or (u > 0 and loop_dir > 0):
            agree = False
for (M, sgn) in ((KM.selfrenew(), -1), (KM.alpha1('I'), 1)):
    AD = BM.KCAdapter(M)
    H = AD.host()
    shallow = AD.zero()
    deep = AD.zero()
    for i in (0, 1):
        deep[(i, 0)] = F(1, 4)
    I0 = (0,) * M.n_out
    g_sh = AD.gates(shallow, ('A', I0))[1]
    g_dp = AD.gates(deep, ('A', I0))[1]
    if (sgn < 0 and not (g_dp >= g_sh and g_dp == 1)) or (sgn > 0 and not (g_dp <= g_sh)):
        agree = False
check('Q4b binary limit and toy cross-check: [18] LOOP (u = −1 continued writing, u = 1/2 self-limiting), binary members Φ_sr (inhibitory; when deep, DAN fires) and Φ_α^I (excitatory; when deep, DAN does not increase): all three directions agree with Theorem 19-4', agree)
~~~

~~~text
# odor-free period: with zero background KC activity the return has no effect on T; with background activity the existing class can already carry internal continuation via the Trace-Response (class (B))
Gi0 = BM.Graded3F(['g1'], 4, {'A': (0, 1)}, {'g1': F(1, 4)}, {'g1': F(2)}, {('g1', 'g1'): F(-1)}, {'g1': F(1)},
                  {'g1': F(1, 2)}, {'g1': F(1, 16)}, bg=F(0), name='Graded3F_inhibitory_return_no_background')

                  {'g1': F(1, 2)}, {'g1': F(1, 16)}, bg=F(1, 2), name='Graded3F_inhibitory_return_with_background')

    res[Gm.name] = (st_, wproc, sw)
~~~

~~~python
# computation of the cross-record chain certificate (R7) of [17] 8.2.4
target = JM.Fly('full')

def trained(k):
    w_ = dict(target.ground)
    for s in ['A+'] + ['rest'] * k:
        w_ = target.step(w_, s)
    return w_

r1, r2, zeta, off = F(1, 20), F(1, 100), F(1, 40), F(1, 50)
ggrid = [F(k, 20) for k in range(-10, 11)]

def record2(kw):
    out = {}
    for j, u in enumerate(ggrid):
        Mh = JM.FlyRec2(u, **kw)
        w_ = dict(Mh.ground)
        w_['a0'] = w_['a1'] = F(1, 2)
        before = Mh.act(w_, 'A')['MBON_a3']
        w2_ = Mh.step(w_, 'A')
        after = Mh.act(w2_, 'A')['MBON_a3']
        out[u] = (before - after) / 2 + r2 * ((j % 3) - 1) / 2
    return out

gmeas = record2({'offset': off})
chain = {}
for k in (2, 6, 12):
    wT_ = trained(k)
    y1 = target.act(wT_, 'A')['PPL1_a3']
    y2 = target.act(JC.setT(target, wT_, ['g0', 'g1']), 'A')['PPL1_a3']
    chain[k] = JB.chain_certificate(y1 + r1 / 2, y2 - r1 / 3, r1, gmeas, r2, zeta, EPS, full_component=True)
check('Q6b chain certificate of [17] Theorem 17-8′: delay 2 beats “%s”, actual action “%s”; delay 6 beats “%s”, actual action “%s”; delay 12 beats “%s”' %
      (chain[2]['why'], chain[2]['act'], chain[6]['why'], chain[6]['act'], chain[12]['why']),
      chain[2]['v'] == '1' and chain[2]['act_ok'] and chain[6]['v'] == '1' and not chain[6]['act_ok'] and chain[12]['v'] == '⊥')

# Theorem 19-7′ (graded cross-compartment gating): slope of the re-exposure write in the γ1 depth, and the flip delay under signed writing
def two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sgn2=None):
    return BM.Graded3F(['g1', 'a3'], 4, {'A': (0, 1), 'B': (2, 3)}, {'g1': th1, 'a3': F(1, 2)}, {'g1': F(0), 'a3': beta2},
                       {('g1', 'a3'): U12}, {'g1': F(1)}, {'g1': F(1, 2), 'a3': eta2}, {'g1': rho1, 'a3': F(0)},
                       signed={'a3': sgn2}, name='Graded3F_γ1α3')

rng = random.Random(19007)
slope_bad, slope_n, flip_bad, flip_inf, flip_fin = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    U12 = rng.choice([F(-1, 2), F(-1), F(-2)])
    th1 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    beta2 = rng.choice([F(2), F(5, 2), F(3)])
    rho1 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 2)])
    Gm = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1)
    s_a, s_b = sorted(rng.sample([F(k, 32) for k in range(0, 25)], 2))
    wa = at(Gm, {'g1:0': s_a, 'g1:1': s_a})
    wb = at(Gm, {'g1:0': s_b, 'g1:1': s_b})
    ca = JC.total_comp(Gm, wa, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    cb = JC.total_comp(Gm, wb, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    m_a, m_b = Gm.act(wa, 'A')['MBON_g1'], Gm.act(wb, 'A')['MBON_g1']
    dan_a, dan_b = Gm.act(wa, 'A')['DAN_a3'], Gm.act(wb, 'A')['DAN_a3']
    if m_a > 0 and m_b > 0 and dan_a > 0 and dan_b > 0:
        slope_n += 1
        if cb - ca != eta2 * (-U12) * 2 * (s_b - s_a):
            slope_bad += 1
    elif cb < ca:
        slope_bad += 1
    # signed writing: the zero point θ* is taken within the reachable range of DAN; flip delay = first beat at which MBON-γ1 returns to m*
    sgn2 = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)])
    Gs2 = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2 - 2, th1, rho1, sgn2=sgn2)
    sigma = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(7, 8)])
    m_star = (Gs2.beta['a3'] - sgn2) / (-U12)
    k_alpha, k_gamma = None, None
    for k in range(0, 200):
        sk = sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k
        wk = at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sk, 'g1:1': sk})
        yk = Gs2.act(wk, 'A')
        ck = JC.total_comp(Gs2, wk, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
        if k_alpha is None and ck <= 0:
            k_alpha = k
        if k_gamma is None and yk['MBON_g1'] >= m_star:
            k_gamma = k
    if k_alpha != k_gamma:
        flip_bad += 1
    if k_alpha is None:
        flip_inf += 1
    else:
        flip_fin += 1
check('Q6c Theorem 19-7′ (1): on 300 graded two-compartment members, the slope of the re-exposure write in the common γ1 depth within the linear regime is always η₂·|U₁₂|·n_A (%d cases, mismatches %d); monotonically nondecreasing on the whole domain' %
      (slope_n, slope_bad), slope_bad == 0 and slope_n > 0)
check('Q6d Theorem 19-7′ (2): under signed writing, the first beat at which the α3 effect turns from deepening to not deepening equals the first beat at which the MBON-γ1 drive returns to m* = (β₂ − θ*)/|U₁₂| (mismatches %d; finite %d, not reached within 200 beats %d, the latter including the never-flipping branch with ρ₁ = 0)' %
      (flip_bad, flip_fin, flip_inf), flip_bad == 0 and flip_inf > 0 and flip_fin > 0)

def nstar(D):
    n_ = 0
    while -XM.A_INN + 2 * XM.B_DIS * D * XM.R1 ** n_ > 0:
        n_ += 1
    return n_

check('Q6e first-arrival intervals of [18] FLY (following [18] PN-1, T19b): n*(D) agrees point by point with the interval formula a/(2b r1^(n−1)) < D ≤ a/(2b r1^n) for D = 0.40..1.00—in the graded reading the flip delay is a step function of the initial γ1 depth',
      all((XM.A_INN / (2 * XM.B_DIS * XM.R1 ** (nstar(D) - 1)) if nstar(D) >= 1 else F(0)) < D <= XM.A_INN / (2 * XM.B_DIS * XM.R1 ** nstar(D))
          for D in [F(k, 200) for k in range(80, 201)]))
~~~

~~~python
def retain(Cm, w0_, nwin, nrest):
    w_ = dict(w0_)
    for _ in range(nwin):
        w_ = Cm.step(w_, 'bg')
    for _ in range(nrest):
        w_ = Cm.step(w_, 'rest')
    return w_['a:0']

blk = {k_: (retain(Cm, w_tr, 2, 20), retain(BM.Consol(Cm.mode, kappa=Cm.kappa, u=Cm.u, theta_c=Cm.theta_c, block=True), w_tr, 2, 20))
       for k_, Cm in cons.items()}
check('Q6m Theorem 19-10 (5) blockade of DAN in the window: depth after 22 beats under the three readings (no blockade vs blockade) %s—all three give “blockade impairs long-term retention”, so this reading does not discriminate among the three readings (the blockade result of Ichinose et al. 2015 is compatible with all three)' %
      '; '.join('%s %s vs %s' % (k_, dec(a_), dec(b_)) for k_, (a_, b_) in blk.items()),
      all(a_ > b_ for (a_, b_) in blk.values()))
# detection condition of the delayed reading under [17] Definition 2′: delayed reading = drop of the MBON-α3 drive n_A·(1 − ρ₂)^N·s_end, detectable if and only if > τ_det;
# with the geometric envelope as tail evidence (Definition 2′ (C)), the self-retention of the reading (relative to τ_det) is the first undetectable beat
rng = random.Random(19009)
r03_bad, r03_n, r03_lb = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(120):
    rho2 = rng.choice([F(1, 64), F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    tau = rng.choice([F(1, 100), F(1, 20), F(1, 8)])
    M = KM.gamma_alpha3(rho2=rho2)
    AD = BM.KCAdapter(M)
    H = AD.host()
    d = AD.zero()
    for s_ in [('A', (0, F(3, 2), 0, 0, 0))] * 3:
        _, d, _, _ = XC.step(H, d, None, s_)
    s_end = d[(0, 2)]
    readout = []
    for N in range(80):
        readout.append(d[(0, 2)] + d[(1, 2)])
        _, d, _, _ = XC.step(H, d, None, ('none', (0,) * 5))
    lb, stt = JC.retention(readout, tau, tail=('envelope', readout[-1], 1 - rho2) if rho2 < 1 else ('envelope', readout[-1], F(0)))
    closed = next((N for N in range(80) if 2 * (1 - rho2) ** N * s_end <= tau), None)
    r03_n += 1
    if closed is None:
        r03_lb += 1
        if not (stt == 'lower bound' and lb == 80):
            r03_bad += 1
    elif stt != 'complete' or lb != closed:
        r03_bad += 1
check('Q6o detection condition of the delayed reading (envelope tail evidence of [17] Definition 2′): on 120 members, mismatches between the number of detectable beats of the delayed reading and the closed form min{N : n_A(1 − ρ₂)^N s_end ≤ τ_det}: %d; '
      '%d members still detectable at the end of the record get only a lower bound (“complete” is not issued)' % (r03_bad, r03_lb), r03_bad == 0 and r03_lb > 0)
# parallel opposing Trace-Responses and same-synapse rewriting ([18] Theorem 18-7 (5); Theorem 19-12)
yN = lambda n, dN, rN, dP, rP: 2 * (dN * rN ** n - dP * rP ** n)
eq = all(yN(n, F(1, 2), F(9, 10), F(1, 2), F(9, 10)) == 0 for n in range(30))
rec_ = [yN(n, F(1, 2), F(99, 100), F(1, 2), F(4, 5)) for n in range(30)]
Gsig = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), F(1, 8), sgn2=F(1))
w_ = at(Gsig, {'a3:0': F(1, 2), 'a3:1': F(1, 2)})
w_ = Gsig.step(w_, 'A')
seq_a = []
for _ in range(30):
    w_ = Gsig.step(w_, 'rest')
    seq_a.append(w_['a3:0'])
check('Q6n Theorem 19-12: with parallel opposing layers at equal rates and equal depths the reading is always 0 (hidden) %s; at unequal rates (the opposing layer decays fast) the reading turns positive spontaneously after extinction (beat 29: %s); after signed weakening of the same synapse the depth does not rise again over a long rest (%s → %s)' %
      (eq, dec(rec_[-1]), dec(seq_a[0]), dec(seq_a[-1])), eq and rec_[0] == 0 and rec_[-1] > 0 and all(seq_a[i + 1] <= seq_a[i] for i in range(29)))
~~~

~~~python
sec('Q8  Theorems 19-5, 19-6 and 19-7 on the binary class 𝒦_B (direct computation with the one-beat transition of k17_core)')

def kb_set(M, cols_depth):
    st = M.init_state()
    for (i, j), s_ in cols_depth.items():
        st[0][(i, j)] = M.b[(i, j)] - s_
    return st

def kb_step(M, st, letter, inj=None):
    I = [F(0)] * M.n_out
    for j, v in (inj or {}).items():
        I[j] = F(v)
    st2, g, vt, ev = KC.step(M, st, letter, I)
    return st2, g

def kb_depth(M, st, i, j):
    return M.b[(i, j)] - st[0][(i, j)]

# Theorem 19-5: use-dependent protection on the inhibitory self-loop (Φ_sr)
Msr8 = KM.selfrenew()
hi8 = Msr8.hi
sstar = (2 * hi8 - Msr8.theta[0]) / 2
dm8 = Msr8.Am - Msr8.Ap
rho8 = Msr8.rho[(0, 0)]
L8 = min(hi8, (1 - rho8) * sstar + dm8)
KP = {m: (1 - rho8) ** m * L8 > sstar for m in range(1, 8)}
rng = random.Random(19801)
pbad8, pn8 = 0, 0
for trial in range(240):
    m = rng.choice([m_ for m_ in KP if KP[m_]])
    s0 = rng.choice([sstar + F(1, 64), F(3, 16), F(1, 4), F(3, 8)])
    st = kb_set(Msr8, {(0, 0): s0, (1, 0): s0})
    for _ in range(12):
        st, g = kb_step(Msr8, st, 'A', {1: rng.choice([0, 0, F(1, 2)])})
        pn8 += 1
        pbad8 += not (g[1] == 1 and kb_depth(Msr8, st, 0, 0) >= L8)
        for _ in range(rng.randrange(0, m + 1)):
            st, g = kb_step(Msr8, st, rng.choice(['none', 'B']))
        pbad8 += not kb_depth(Msr8, st, 0, 0) > sstar
def f_m(s_, m):
    return (1 - rho8) ** m * min(hi8, (1 - rho8) * s_ + dm8)
per = {}
for m in (2, 6):
    st = kb_set(Msr8, {(0, 0): F(1, 4), (1, 0): F(1, 4)})
    fires = []
    for cyc in range(30):
        st, g = kb_step(Msr8, st, 'A')
        fires.append(g[1])
        for _ in range(m):
            st, g = kb_step(Msr8, st, 'none')
    per[m] = (f_m(sstar, m) > sstar, all(fires), fires[-1])
check('Q8a Theorem 19-5 (Φ_sr: u < 0, c_A + u < θ_D ≤ c_A): s* = %s, L = %s; the m for which K_P(m) holds = %s; on %d beats of random input histories (rest ≤ m between adjacent CS+, arbitrary nonnegative injection), mismatches of “on every CS+ beat D fires and s ≥ L after the beat, and s > s* before the next CS+”: %d; '
      'periodic protocol m = 2: f_m(s*) > s* %s, continued writing throughout 30 periods %s; m = 6: f_m(s*) > s* %s, still continued writing in the last period %s' %
      (fs(sstar), fs(L8), [m for m in KP if KP[m]], pn8, pbad8, per[2][0], per[2][1], per[6][0], per[6][2]),
      pbad8 == 0 and per[2][0] and per[2][1] and not per[6][0] and per[6][2] == 0)
# Theorem 19-6: depression Trace-Response on the excitatory self-loop (Φ_α): no protection, Type I self-limiting, Type II total write
Ma8 = KM.alpha1('I')
sst_a = (2 * Ma8.hi - Ma8.theta[0]) / 2
dm_a = Ma8.Am - Ma8.Ap
rho_a = Ma8.rho[(0, 0)]
slim = min(Ma8.hi, (1 - rho_a) * sst_a + dm_a)
rng = random.Random(19802)
b1, b2, b3, b4, n6 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    s0 = rng.choice([F(k, 32) for k in range(0, 17)])
    st = kb_set(Ma8, {(0, 0): s0, (1, 0): s0})
    st2, g = kb_step(Ma8, st, 'A')
    b1 += g[1] != 0 or kb_depth(Ma8, st2, 0, 0) != (1 - rho_a) * s0
    st3, g3 = kb_step(Ma8, st, 'A', {1: F(1, 2)})
    b2 += (g3[1] == 1) != (s0 <= sst_a)
    st4, g4 = kb_step(Ma8, st, 'A', {1: F(1)})
    b3 += g4[1] != 1
    n6 += 1
st = Ma8.init_state()
for k in range(200):
    st, g = kb_step(Ma8, st, rng.choice(['A', 'A', 'none']), {1: rng.choice([0, F(1, 2)])})
    b4 += kb_depth(Ma8, st, 0, 0) > slim
check('Q8b Theorem 19-6 (Φ_α: u > 0): over %d depths, (1) on CS+ beats without injection D does not fire and there is only relaxation, mismatches %d; (2) Type I (J = 1/2): reinforced CS+ writes ⟺ s ≤ s* = %s, mismatches %d; (4) Type II (J = 1): total write, mismatches %d; on 200 beats of random training history s ≤ s_lim = %s, mismatches %d' %
      (n6, b1, fs(sst_a), b2, b3, fs(slim), b4), b1 == b2 == b3 == b4 == 0)
# Theorem 19-7: cross-compartment gating—MVP2 fires on re-exposure ⟺ k ≥ k*, PPL1-α3 gate = 1 − g_M1
rng = random.Random(19803)
b7, n7, inf7 = 0, 0, 0
for r1 in (F(0), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
    M7 = KM.gamma_alpha3(rho1=r1)
    s1s = (2 * M7.hi - M7.theta[0]) / 2
    for sig in (F(1, 16), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)):
        kst = next((k for k in range(60) if sig * (1 - r1) ** k <= s1s), None)
        inf7 += kst is None
        st = kb_set(M7, {(0, 0): sig, (1, 0): sig})
        for k in range(40):
            _, g = kb_step(M7, st, 'A')
            n7 += 1
            want = kst is not None and k >= kst
            b7 += (g[0] == 1) != want or g[3] != 1 - g[0]
            st, _ = kb_step(M7, st, 'none')
check('Q8c Theorem 19-7 (Φ_γα3^0: X1, X2): s₁* = 1/8; over 5 values of ρ₁ × 3 end-of-training depths σ × 40 numbers of rest beats, mismatches of “MVP2 fires on the re-exposed CS+ ⟺ k ≥ k* := min{k : σ(1 − ρ₁)^k ≤ s₁*}” and “g_PPL1-α3 = 1 − g_MVP2”: %d (%d beats; groups with k* = ∞: %d, i.e. ρ₁ = 0 and σ > s₁*)' %
      (b7, n7, inf7), b7 == 0 and inf7 > 0)
# ======================================================================
sec('Q9  In-model verification of the quantitative predictions (quantitative forms of the predictions in Section 10 of the main text; model layer [verified], not empirical)')
# Q1 intercept of the dose–response = flip level: on graded signed two-compartment members the re-exposure write is a straight line in the MBON-γ1 reading, and its zero m₀ equals the flip level m* of the delay scan
rng = random.Random(19101)
q1_bad, q1_n = 0, 0
for trial in range(200):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    U12 = rng.choice([F(-1, 2), F(-1), F(-2)])
    th1 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4)])
    beta2 = rng.choice([F(1), F(3, 2), F(2)])
    sgn2 = rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    rho1 = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    Gs2 = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sgn2=sgn2)
    pts = []
    for sq in (F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
        wq = at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sq, 'g1:1': sq})
        yq = Gs2.act(wq, 'A')
        if yq['MBON_g1'] > 0 and yq['DAN_a3'] > 0:
            pts.append((yq['MBON_g1'], JC.total_comp(Gs2, wq, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')))
    if len(pts) < 2:
        continue
    (m1, W1), (m2, W2) = pts
    m0 = m1 - W1 * (m2 - m1) / (W2 - W1)
    m_star = (beta2 - sgn2) / (-U12)
    sigma = F(7, 8)
    kf = None
    for k in range(400):
        sk = sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k
        if JC.total_comp(Gs2, at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sk, 'g1:1': sk}), 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0') <= 0:
            kf = k
            break
    q1_n += 1
    if m0 != m_star:
        q1_bad += 1
    elif kf is not None and kf > 0:
        mb = lambda k: Gs2.act(at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k, 'g1:1': sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k}), 'A')['MBON_g1']
        if not (mb(kf - 1) < m0 <= mb(kf)):
            q1_bad += 1
check('Q9a consistency of predictions P19-1/P19-2: on %d graded signed members, the zero m₀ of the dose–response line (write against the MBON-γ1 reading) equals m* = (β₂ − θ*)/|U₁₂|, and the flip beat of the delay scan falls exactly where MBON-γ1 crosses m₀ (mismatches %d)' %
      (q1_n, q1_bad), q1_bad == 0 and q1_n > 100)
# Q2 shift law of the flip delay: doubling the initial γ1 depth increases the flip delay by n₂ − 1 or n₂ beats, n₂ = min{n : (1 − ρ₁)^n ≤ 1/2} (continuous-time limit Δt = τ_γ1·ln 2)
rng = random.Random(19102)
q2_bad, q2_n = 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    rho1 = rng.choice([F(1, 32), F(1, 16), F(1, 10), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    s_m = rng.choice([F(1, 64), F(1, 32), F(1, 16), F(1, 10)])
    sigma = rng.choice([F(k, 64) for k in range(5, 33)])
    if not (sigma > s_m and 2 * sigma <= 1):
        continue
    kst = lambda sg: next(k for k in range(10 ** 4) if sg * (1 - rho1) ** k <= s_m)
    n2 = next(n for n in range(10 ** 4) if (1 - rho1) ** n <= F(1, 2))
    dk = kst(2 * sigma) - kst(sigma)
    q2_n += 1
    if not (n2 - 1 <= dk <= n2):
        q2_bad += 1
check('Q9b shift law of the flip delay (corollary of Theorem 19-7′): in %d cases, mismatches of k*(2σ) − k*(σ) ∈ {n₂ − 1, n₂} (n₂ the number of beats in which the γ1 Trace-Response halves): %d' % (q2_n, q2_bad),
      q2_bad == 0 and q2_n > 100)
# Q3 interference of an early test: on [18] FLY, one early CS+ test at a delay of k beats changes the subsequent α3 depth by η(−a + b·Σ_{K(A)} d_γ1(k)), with the sign flipping at n*(D)
q3_bad, q3_n, q3_signs = 0, 0, set()
Hf = XM.make_fly()
for D in [F(k, 20) for k in range(8, 21)]:
    for k in range(0, 12):
        d0 = XM.fly_zero()
        for i in XM.ODOR['A']:
            d0[(i, 'g1')] = D
            d0[(i, 'a3')] = F(1, 2)
        _, dT, _ = XC.run(Hf, d0, None, ['R'] * k + ['A'] + ['R'] * 5)
        _, dN, _ = XC.run(Hf, d0, None, ['R'] * (k + 6))
        pred = XM.ETA * (-XM.A_INN + XM.B_DIS * 2 * D * XM.R1 ** k)
        q3_n += 1
        if dT[(0, 'a3')] - dN[(0, 'a3')] != pred or ((pred > 0) != (k < nstar(D))):
            q3_bad += 1
        q3_signs.add(pred > 0)
check('Q9c interference of an early test (quantitative form of [18] Theorem 18-3 (4)): over %d groups (γ1 depth D × early-test delay k), the difference in the final α3 state between the groups with and without an early test = η(−a + b·n_A·D·r₁^k), turning from positive to negative at k = n*(D) (mismatches %d)' %
      (q3_n, q3_bad), q3_bad == 0 and q3_signs == {True, False})
# Q4 magnitude of the DAN difference in the consolidation window: trained − sham-trained = −u·b·n_A·D (linear regime)
q4_bad, q4_n = 0, 0
for k_, Cm in cons.items():
    for D in [F(k, 8) for k in range(1, 9)]:
        wt = at(Cm, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D})
        dt_, dp_ = Cm.act(wt, 'bg')['DAN_w'], Cm.act(dict(Cm.init), 'bg')['DAN_w']
        raw_t = Cm.kappa + Cm.u * Cm.act(wt, 'bg')['MBON_a']
        raw_p = Cm.kappa + Cm.u * Cm.act(dict(Cm.init), 'bg')['MBON_a']
        if k_ != 'perm' and (raw_t < 0 or raw_p < 0):
            continue
        q4_n += 1
        want = F(0) if k_ == 'perm' else -Cm.u * Cm.bgv * 2 * D
        if dt_ - dp_ != want:
            q4_bad += 1
check('Q9d magnitude of the DAN difference in the consolidation window (quantitative form of Theorem 19-10 (3)): in %d cases, mismatches of “trained − sham-trained” = −u·b·n_A·D (0 for the permissive type): %d—proportional to the training depth and to the background KC activity' %
      (q4_n, q4_bad), q4_bad == 0 and q4_n >= 16)
# Q5 non-monotonicity of long-term memory in training strength (under the excitatory-return reading): depth after a window of L beats and N rest beats
#     LTM(D) = D·ρ₀^{t+1}·ρ₁^{L−1−t+N} (t = min{t < L : D·ρ₀^t ≤ D_c}); if no such t exists in the window, D·ρ₀^{L+N}; D_c = (κ + u·b·N_KC − θ_c)/(u·b·n_A)
Dc_formula = (exc_hi.kappa + exc_hi.u * exc_hi.bgv * 4 - exc_hi.theta_c) / (exc_hi.u * exc_hi.bgv * 2)
q5_bad, ltm = 0, {}
for L in (1, 2, 3):
    for D in [F(k, 8) for k in range(1, 9)]:
        got = retain(exc_hi, at(exc_hi, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), L, 20)
        t = next((t_ for t_ in range(L) if D * exc_hi.rho0 ** t_ <= Dc_formula), None)
        want = D * exc_hi.rho0 ** (L + 20) if t is None else D * exc_hi.rho0 ** (t + 1) * exc_hi.rho1 ** (L - 1 - t + 20)
        ltm[(L, D)] = got
        if got != want:
            q5_bad += 1
nonmono = ltm[(1, F(5, 8))] < ltm[(1, F(1, 2))] and ltm[(2, F(5, 8))] < ltm[(2, F(1, 2))]
~~~

check('Q9e non-monotonicity of long-term memory in training strength (quantitative form of Theorem 19-10 (4)): D\_c = (κ + u·b·N − θ\_c)/(u·b·n\_A) = %s; for window lengths L = 1, 2, 3 beats, mismatches of 24 cases with the closed form LTM(D) = D·ρ₀^{t+1}·ρ₁^{L−1−t+N}: %d; '

~~~text
      'LTM(1/2)/LTM(5/8) = %s at L = 1 and %s at L = 2—a jump down once D_c is passed (a deep Trace-Response first shallows below D_c and only then is consolidated; the number of sawtooth teeth is cut off by the tag gate and by D ≤ 1 and does not grow beat by beat with the window length, see Q11l)' %
      (fs(Dc_formula), q5_bad, dec(ltm[(1, F(1, 2))] / ltm[(1, F(5, 8))], 1), dec(ltm[(2, F(1, 2))] / ltm[(2, F(5, 8))], 2)),
      q5_bad == 0 and nonmono and Dc_formula == F(1, 2))
# Q6 odor specificity and overlap grading of α3 depression: under the synaptic Trace-Response reading, the drop in the MBON-α3 drive for the test odor = o·D (o the number of KCs shared with CS+);
#     under the MBON intrinsic-excitability reading (competition theory) the drop is independent of o
Gov = BM.Graded3F(['a3'], 8, {'A': (0, 1, 2, 3), 'X0': (4, 5, 6, 7), 'X1': (3, 4, 5, 6), 'X2': (2, 3, 4, 5), 'X3': (1, 2, 3, 4), 'X4': (0, 1, 2, 3)},
                  {'a3': F(0)}, {'a3': F(0)}, {}, {'a3': F(1)}, {'a3': F(1, 4)}, {'a3': F(0)}, name='Graded3F_overlap')
D6 = F(3, 5)
wD = at(Gov, {'a3:%d' % i: D6 for i in (0, 1, 2, 3)})
red = [Gov.act(Gov.init, 'X%d' % o)['MBON_a3'] - Gov.act(wD, 'X%d' % o)['MBON_a3'] for o in range(5)]
intrinsic = [F(4) * D6 / 4 * 4 for o in range(5)]  # intrinsic-excitability reading: every odor is lowered by the same gain (independent of o)
~~~

check('Q9f overlap grading (distinguishing “synaptic Trace-Response” from “MBON intrinsic excitability”): when the test odor shares o = 0..4 KCs with CS+, the drop in the MBON-α3 drive is %s (= o·D, linear); the intrinsic-excitability reading predicts the same value for every o' %

~~~json
      [fs(v) for v in red], red == [o * D6 for o in range(5)] and len(set(intrinsic)) == 1)
# Q7 excitatory return of α3 itself (MBON-α3 → PPL1-α3 positive in the accompanying model) ⇒ α3 is self-limiting: the re-exposure write falls with the existing α3 depth at slope −η₂·u₂₂·n_A
q7_bad, q7_n = 0, 0
for u22 in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
    for eta2 in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4)):
        Gm = BM.Graded3F(['g1', 'a3'], 4, {'A': (0, 1), 'B': (2, 3)}, {'g1': F(0), 'a3': F(0)}, {'g1': F(0), 'a3': F(1)},
                         {('g1', 'a3'): F(-1), ('a3', 'a3'): u22}, {'g1': F(1)}, {'g1': F(1, 2), 'a3': eta2}, {'g1': F(1, 8), 'a3': F(0)})
        for sa, sb in ((F(0), F(1, 4)), (F(1, 4), F(1, 2))):
            wa_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': F(1, 2), 'g1:1': F(1, 2), 'a3:0': sa, 'a3:1': sa})
            wb_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': F(1, 2), 'g1:1': F(1, 2), 'a3:0': sb, 'a3:1': sb})
            ca_ = JC.total_comp(Gm, wa_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
            cb_ = JC.total_comp(Gm, wb_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
            q7_n += 1
            if Gm.act(wb_, 'A')['DAN_a3'] > 0 and (cb_ - ca_) != -eta2 * u22 * 2 * (sb - sa):
                q7_bad += 1
check('Q9g α3 self-limiting (Theorem 19-4 applied to the positive weight MBON-α3 → PPL1-α3 of the accompanying model): in %d cases the slope of the re-exposure write in the existing α3 depth = −η₂·u₂₂·n_A (mismatches %d)—under the same γ1 depression, the deeper the α3 memory, the smaller the strengthening by early re-exposure' %
      (q7_n, q7_bad), q7_bad == 0)
# Q8 condition for disinhibitory continued writing: under inhibitory return within the same compartment, unreinforced re-exposure deepens the Trace-Response net ⟺ η·DAN(s) > ρ·s (and not saturated); DAN(s) − DAN(0) = |u|·n_A·s (linear regime)
rng = random.Random(19108)
q8_bad, q8_n, q8_yes = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    u = rng.choice([F(-1, 4), F(-1, 2), F(-1)])
    be = rng.choice([F(1), F(3, 2), F(2), F(5, 2)])
    eta = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    rho = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    Gm = BM.Graded3F(['g1'], 4, {'A': (0, 1)}, {'g1': F(0)}, {'g1': be}, {('g1', 'g1'): u}, {'g1': F(1)}, {'g1': eta}, {'g1': rho})
    sq = rng.choice([F(k, 16) for k in range(1, 16)])
    wq = at(Gm, {'g1:0': sq, 'g1:1': sq})
    dan = Gm.act(wq, 'A')['DAN_g1']
    deeper = Gm.step(wq, 'A')['g1:0'] > sq
    q8_n += 1
    q8_yes += deeper
    lin_ok = (be + u * 2 < 0) or (dan - Gm.act(Gm.init, 'A')['DAN_g1'] == -u * 2 * sq)
    if deeper != (eta * dan > rho * sq) or not lin_ok:
        q8_bad += 1
check('Q9h condition for disinhibitory continued writing (graded form of Theorem 19-5): in %d cases, mismatches of “unreinforced re-exposure deepens the depression net” ⟺ η·DAN(s) > ρ·s: %d (continued writing in %d cases); the disinhibitory increment of DAN = |u|·n_A·s' %
      (q8_n, q8_bad, q8_yes), q8_bad == 0 and 0 < q8_yes < q8_n)
# ======================================================================
sec('Q10  Boundary examples (1): qualification diagnosis 19-H, Discrimination Theorem 19-D, consolidation 19-10, cross-compartment gating 19-7′ and local restrictions')
# ---- Theorem 19-H: (1) effective difference for HDyn, (2) element certificates and organization component of TraceSet, (3) SED_W additionally requires oriented W > ε, (4) U = 0 as a further control domain
def ex_signed(beta_a=F(5, 2)):
    return BM.Graded3F(['g', 'a'], 1, {'A': (0,)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1}, {('g', 'a'): F(-1, 2)}, {'g': 1, 'a': beta_a},
                       {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 4)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, signed={'a': 1})

Hs = ex_signed()
sg_ = ['A+', 'rest', 'A']
Ss = JC.run(Hs, Hs.init, sg_)
act_, ctl_ = Ss[2], dict(Ss[2], **{'g:0': F(0)})
W_act = JC.comp_of(Hs, act_, Hs.act(act_, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
br_ = JC.branches(Hs, act_, ctl_, 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
cert_ = JC.hdyn_cert(Hs, Ss, sg_, Hs.X, EPS)[0]
sed_ = JC.sed(Hs, [(act_, 'A')], Hs.X, EPS)

def h19_parts(host, states, sigs, k, T_gamma, x, eps):
    """Values of the four parts of Theorem 19-H. Returns a dict:
    i: the Definition 6′ effective difference of the α write at beat k (the control only sets T_gamma back to baseline; same-beat component |W| > ε; two branches);
    i′: the target x already exceeds ε in the pre-beat state of beat k (a position already left, N1′);
    iii: SED_W additionally requires oriented W > ε and an effective A branch."""
    w = states[k]
    s = sigs[k]
    y = host.act(w, s)
    W = JC.comp_of(host, w, y, s, 'W_a', x) if any(p == 'W_a' for (p, xx, c) in host.comps(w, y, s) if xx == x) else ZERO_
    w2 = dict(w, **{g: F(0) for g in T_gamma})
    A_, D_, tot_ = JC.branches(host, w, w2, s, 'W_a', x)
    one = abs(W) > eps and abs(tot_) > eps and all(w[g] > eps for g in T_gamma)
    return {'W': W, 'A': A_, 'D': D_, 'tot': tot_, 'i': one, 'i′': w[x] > eps, 'iii': one and W > eps and abs(A_) > eps}

ZERO_ = F(0)
pr = h19_parts(Hs, Ss, sg_, 2, ['g:0'], 'a:0', EPS)
~~~

check('Q10a Theorem 19-H: Example X2 (signed, negative write): W = %s, control W = %s, A = %s, D = %s; values: (1) effective difference %s, (3) SED\_W %s (oriented W \< 0); \[17\] program hdyn\_cert = %s, sed = %s—once the two clauses are separated, they agree' %

~~~text
      (fs(W_act), fs(JC.comp_of(Hs, ctl_, Hs.act(ctl_, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')), fs(br_[0]), fs(br_[1]), pr['i'], pr['iii'], cert_, sed_),
      pr['i'] and not pr['iii'] and cert_ == '1' and sed_ is None and W_act == F(-1, 16) and br_ == (F(-1, 16), 0, F(-1, 16)))
# Random coverage of the signed branch: (1) always holds; (3) if and only if W > ε
rng = random.Random(19201)
b10b_bad, b10b_pos, b10b_neg, b10b_sub, b10b_n1 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(200):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    U12 = rng.choice([F(-1, 2), F(-1)])
    sgn2 = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2), F(2)])
    beta2 = rng.choice([F(2), F(5, 2), F(3)])
    Gm = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, F(1, 4), F(1, 8), sgn2=sgn2)
    S_ = JC.run(Gm, Gm.init, ['A+', 'rest', 'A', 'rest'])
    y_ = Gm.act(S_[2], 'A')
    W = JC.comp_of(Gm, S_[2], y_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    w2 = dict(S_[2], **{'g1:0': F(0), 'g1:1': F(0)})
    A_, D_, tot_ = JC.branches(Gm, S_[2], w2, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    lin = Gm.act(S_[2], 'A')['DAN_a3'] > 0 and Gm.act(w2, 'A')['DAN_a3'] > 0 and Gm.act(w2, 'A')['MBON_g1'] > 0
    if not lin:
        continue
    one = abs(W) > EPS and abs(tot_) > EPS
    sw = JC.sed(Gm, [(S_[2], 'A')], ['g1:0', 'g1:1', 'a3:0', 'a3:1'], EPS)
    if (sw is not None) != (one and W > EPS) or D_ != 0 or tot_ != eta2 * U12 * (S_[2]['g1:0'] + S_[2]['g1:1']):
        b10b_bad += 1
    b10b_pos += one and W > EPS
    b10b_neg += one and W < -EPS
    b10b_sub += not one
    b10b_n1 += one and S_[2]['a3:0'] <= EPS
check('Q10b Theorem 19-H, coverage of the signed branch (signed complement to Q7a): on members in the linear regime, Δ_tot = η₂U₁₂Σd_γ and the D branch is always 0; (1) if and only if |W| > ε (positive write %d, negative write %d, same-beat component not exceeding ε %d); SED_W if and only if (1) and W > ε (mismatches %d); '
      '(1) holds while α has not exceeded ε before the beat (N1′ fails, 19-H does not issue): %d cases' % (b10b_pos, b10b_neg, b10b_sub, b10b_bad, b10b_n1),
      b10b_bad == 0 and b10b_pos > 0 and b10b_neg > 0)
# (2) element certificates for the same T (self-retention lower bound ≥ τ_W, K2 relative to the CS+ probe, K3 relative to the paired write), organization component (exact enumeration of joint settings), carrier in B
Gm = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 8))
T_ = ['g1:0', 'g1:1', 'a3:0', 'a3:1']
wT_ = JC.run(Gm, Gm.init, ['A+', 'rest'])[-1]
tauW = 6
cert2 = {}
for x in T_:
    w_ = dict(wT_)
    seq_ = []
    for t in range(40):
        seq_.append(w_[x] - Gm.ground[x])
        w_ = Gm.step(w_, 'rest')
    lb_, st_ = JC.retention(seq_, EPS)
    k2 = not JC.order_conflicts([(q, abs(Gm.act(at(Gm, {x: q}), 'A')['MBON_' + x.split(':')[0]] - Gm.act(Gm.init, 'A')['MBON_' + x.split(':')[0]]))
                                   for q in [F(k, 8) for k in range(9)]])
    k3 = all(Gm.step(at(Gm, {x: q}), 'A+')[x] >= q for q in [F(k, 8) for k in range(9)])
    cert2[x] = (lb_ >= tauW, k2, k3)
hyT, cpT = JC.org_graph_exact(Gm, wT_, T_, ['A'], EPS)
carrier = set(T_) <= set(Gm.X)
~~~

check('Q10c Theorem 19-H (2), element certificates listed position by position (τ\_W = %d beats): %s; organization component (CS+ probe, exact enumeration of joint settings) %s; carrier in B (T ⊆ declared Structural Positions) %s—formation or threshold crossing does not stand in for them' %

~~~text
      (tauW, {x: v for x, v in cert2.items()}, cpT, carrier),
      all(all(v) for v in cert2.values()) and any(set(T_) <= set(c) for c in cpT) and carrier)
~~~

~~~text
# (4) U = 0 as a further control domain: when γ relaxation is present (ρ_γ > 0), Definition 6′ is obtained through the direct read of the relaxation; without relaxation (ρ_γ = 0) and with U = 0 there is no in-window certificate for T: record ⊥, not 0
G0r = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(0), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 8))
G0n = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(0), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), F(0))

c0n = JC.hdyn_cert(G0n, JC.run(G0n, G0n.init, h_[1:]), h_[1:], T_, EPS)
~~~

check('Q10e Theorem 19-H (4), U = 0 control domain: with ρ\_γ = 1/8 the certificate is %s (witness process %s, direct read of γ relaxation); with ρ\_γ = 0 the in-window certificate after training is “%s”—Fld is not issued; ⊥ is recorded (not 0)' %

~~~text
      (c0r[0], c0r[1][2] if c0r[0] == '1' else None, c0n[0]),
      c0r[0] == '1' and c0r[1][2] == 'R_g1' and c0n[0] != '1')
# ---- Theorem 19-D (the load-bearing theorem of the Discrimination Form): two-arm difference for permissive and return-type candidates
rng = random.Random(19202)
d_bad, d_n = 0, 0
for trial in range(400):
    N = rng.choice([4, 6, 8])
    b = [rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]) for _ in range(N)]
    if sum(b) == 0:
        continue
    dS = [rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8)]) for _ in range(N)]
    dT = [min(F(1), x + rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])) for x in dS]
    db = sum((bi * (t - s_) for bi, t, s_ in zip(b, dT, dS)), F(0))
    if db <= 0:
        continue
    kappa = rng.choice([F(1), F(2), F(3)])
    for u in (F(-1, 2), F(-1), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)):
        Rc = BM.WindowCand('R', kappa, u, b, F(0))
        Pc = BM.WindowCand('P', kappa, 0, b, F(0))
        if not (Rc.dan_input(dT) > 0 and Rc.dan_input(dS) > 0):
            continue
        d_n += 1
        if Pc.delta(dT, dS) != 0 or Rc.delta(dT, dS) != -u * db:
            d_bad += 1
check('Q10f Theorem 19-D (1), exact two-arm difference: in %d cases (antecedents D1: training adds depression Δb > 0 on background-activated positions; D2: u ≠ 0; D3: the composite read-outs of both arms lie in the linear regime), the permissive type gives Δ = 0 and the return type Δ = −u·Δb (sign −sgn u): mismatches %d' %
      (d_n, d_bad), d_bad == 0 and d_n > 200)
rng = random.Random(19203)
res_bad, res_n, gap_hits = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    e = rng.choice([F(1, 100), F(1, 40), F(1, 20)])
    u_min, g = rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]), rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    m = u_min * g
    if not m > 2 * e:
        continue
    truth = rng.choice(['P', 'R+', 'R-'])
    u = {'P': F(0), 'R+': u_min * rng.choice([1, 2]), 'R-': -u_min * rng.choice([1, 2])}[truth]
    true_delta = -u * g
    noise = e * F(rng.randrange(-10, 11), 10)
    obs = true_delta + noise
    call = 'P' if abs(obs) <= e else ('R-' if obs >= m - e else ('R+' if obs <= -(m - e) else 'undecidable'))
    res_n += 1
    if call != truth:
        res_bad += 1
check('Q10g Theorem 19-D (2), resolution version: for m = u_min·g > 2e the three output sets {|x| ≤ e}, {x ≥ m − e}, {x ≤ −(m − e)} are pairwise disjoint; in %d readings with error (|δ| ≤ e): misjudged %d, in the undecidable region 0' %
      (res_n, res_bad), res_bad == 0 and res_n > 100)
plat = BM.WindowCand('R', 0, -1, (F(1, 4),) * 4, 0)
dT_, dS_ = (F(1, 2), F(1, 2), 0, 0), (0, 0, 0, 0)
P0 = BM.WindowCand('P', 0, 0, (F(1, 4),) * 4, 0)
nob = BM.WindowCand('R', 1, -1, (0, 0, 0, 0), 0)
u0 = BM.WindowCand('R', 1, 0, (F(1, 4),) * 4, 0)
m_small = F(1, 40) * F(1, 2)
~~~

check('Q10h Theorem 19-D (3), the antecedents cannot be dropped: D3 fails (Example X4, inhibitory plateau κ = 0, u = −1, b = 1/4): DAN is 0 in both arms, and Δ = %s reads the same as the permissive %s; D1 fails (background b = 0): Δ = %s; D2 fails (u = 0): Δ = %s; for m ≤ 2e the output sets intersect (the region of identical readings is nonempty)' %

~~~text
      (fs(plat.delta(dT_, dS_)), fs(P0.delta(dT_, dS_)), fs(nob.delta(dT_, dS_)), fs(u0.delta(dT_, dS_))),
      plat.delta(dT_, dS_) == 0 == P0.delta(dT_, dS_) and nob.delta(dT_, dS_) == 0 and u0.delta(dT_, dS_) == 0 and not m_small > 2 * F(1, 40))
# 19-10 (4): Example X5, excitatory (κ = 1, u = 1/2, b = 1/4, θ_c = 1/2): still CONS at D = 1; D_c exists ⟺ κ + u·b·(N − n_A) < θ_c ≤ κ + u·b·N (D = 0 consolidated, D = 1 not consolidated)
ex_rev = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=F(1), u=F(1, 2), bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(1, 2))
st1 = {x: F(x.startswith('a:')) for x in ex_rev.X}
cons_d1 = ex_rev.step(st1, 'bg')['c:0'] == 1
rng = random.Random(19204)
dc_bad = 0
for trial in range(200):
    kap = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    uu = rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)])
    th = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)])
    Cm = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=kap, u=uu, bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=th)
    fails = [D for D in [F(k, 16) for k in range(1, 17)] if JC.total_comp(Cm, at(Cm, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), 'bg', 'CONS', 'c:0') == 0]
    pred_exists = kap + uu * F(1, 4) * 2 < th <= kap + uu * F(1, 4) * 4
    if bool(fails) != (kap + uu * F(1, 4) * 2 < th) or (pred_exists and (F(1) not in fails)):
        dc_bad += 1
check('Q10i Theorem 19-10 (4): Example X5 (excitatory) at D = 1: DAN_w = %s ≥ θ_c, CONS still set %s; over 200 cases, “some depth is not consolidated” ⟺ κ + u·b·(N − n_A) < θ_c: mismatches %d—monotone decrease does not imply crossing the threshold' %
      (fs(ex_rev.act(st1, 'bg')['DAN_w']), cons_d1, dc_bad), cons_d1 and dc_bad == 0)
th0 = BM.Consol('perm', kappa=F(1, 2), theta_c=F(0), block=True)
w_tr0 = at(th0, {'a:0': F(3, 4), 'a:1': F(3, 4)})
cons_blocked = JC.total_comp(th0, w_tr0, 'bg', 'CONS', 'c:0')
permlow = BM.Consol('perm', kappa=F(1, 8), theta_c=F(1, 4))
tag_low = JC.branches(permlow, w_tr, JC.setT(permlow, w_tr, ['a:0']), 'bg', 'CONS', 'c:0')
~~~

check('Q10j antecedents of Theorem 19-10 (1) and (5): with θ\_c = 0, blockade (DAN\_w = 0) still sets CONS (%s), so (5) requires θ\_c \> 0; for the permissive type with Q(κ) \< θ\_c, the tag crossing its threshold does not change CONS (two branches %s), so (1) requires Q(κ) ≥ θ\_c and c\_x settable' %

~~~text
      (fs(cons_blocked), tuple(fs(v) for v in tag_low)), cons_blocked == 1 and tag_low == (0, 0, 0))
# ---- 19-7′: under upper-end clipping W > 0 without deepening; k₊ (first beat with no positive write) and k₋ (first reverse write) listed separately; asymptotic zero versus ∞ with a tail proof
Hp = BM.Graded3F(['g', 'a'], 1, {'A': (0,)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1}, {('g', 'a'): F(-1, 2)}, {'g': 1, 'a': 1},
                 {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 4)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, signed={'a': F(1, 2)})

Wsat = JC.comp_of(Hp, satw, Hp.act(satw, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
~~~

check('Q10k Theorem 19-7′ (3), upper-end clipping (Example X6): W = %s \> 0 while the α post-state goes %s → %s (no deepening)—“a write component occurs” does not imply “actual deepening”' %

~~~python
      (fs(Wsat), fs(satw['a:0']), fs(Hp.step(satw, 'A')['a:0'])), Wsat == F(1, 16) and Hp.step(satw, 'A')['a:0'] == 1)

def kplus_kminus(Gs2, sigma, rho1, K=300):
    kp = km = None
    for k in range(K):
        sk = sigma * (1 - rho1) ** k
        W = JC.total_comp(Gs2, at(Gs2, {'g1:0': sk, 'g1:1': sk}), 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
        if kp is None and W <= 0:
            kp = k
        if km is None and W < 0:
            km = k
    return kp, km

# First beat exactly at zero: choose θ* so that W = 0 at some beat
Gz = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(1), F(0), F(1, 2), sgn2=F(1, 2))  # m* = 1/2; MBON = 2(1 − s)
kp_z, km_z = kplus_kminus(Gz, F(3, 4), F(1, 2))
# Asymptotic zero: m* = MBON(0) = n_A − θ, s_m = 0; W > 0 always holds and tends to 0 (tail proof: σ(1 − ρ)^k > 0)
Ga = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(5, 2), F(0), F(1, 2), sgn2=F(1, 2))  # m* = 2 = MBON(0)
kp_a, km_a = kplus_kminus(Ga, F(3, 4), F(1, 2), 60)
~~~

check('Q10l Theorem 19-7′ (2), k₊ and k₋ listed separately: in the example whose first beat is exactly at zero, k₊ = %s, k₋ = %s (a beat with W = 0 only ends positive writing); in the asymptotic-zero example (m\* = MBON upper bound, s\_m = 0), k₊ = %s within 60 beats—the tail proof that W \> 0 always holds is σ(1 − ρ)^k \> 0, recorded as ∞ and kept apart from “not seen in a finite window”' %

~~~text
      (kp_z, km_z, kp_a), kp_z is not None and km_z is not None and km_z > kp_z and kp_a is None)
# Matched no-re-exposure arm of P19-1: training strength changes the final α state; re-exposure arm minus matched arm = same-beat write W (unclipped, no α relaxation)
p1_bad = 0
for nA in (1, 2, 3):
    Gm = two_comp(F(1, 16), F(-1), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 8))
    pre = JC.run(Gm, Gm.init, ['A+'] * nA + ['rest'])[-1]
    Wp = JC.total_comp(Gm, pre, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0')
    re_ = JC.run(Gm, pre, ['A'] + ['rest'] * 6)[-1]
    no_ = JC.run(Gm, pre, ['rest'] * 7)[-1]
    if re_['a3:0'] - no_['a3:0'] != Wp:
        p1_bad += 1
alpha_end = [JC.run(Gm, Gm.init, ['A+'] * nA + ['rest'])[-1]['a3:0'] for nA in (1, 2, 3)]
~~~

~~~text
      ([fs(a) for a in alpha_end], p1_bad), len(set(alpha_end)) == 3 and p1_bad == 0)
# Recovery condition of 19-12: initial cancellation d_N = d_P, original Trace-Response positive, in-window reading above the detection threshold; equal rates or no threshold crossing give no recovery
yN2 = lambda n, dN, rN, dP, rP: 2 * (dN * rN ** n - dP * rP ** n)
vis = lambda dN, rN, dP, rP, tau, Wn: any(yN2(n, dN, rN, dP, rP) > tau for n in range(Wn))
~~~

~~~text
      vis(F(1, 2), F(99, 100), F(1, 2), F(4, 5), F(1, 10), 30) and not vis(F(1, 2), F(9, 10), F(1, 2), F(9, 10), F(1, 10), 30) and not vis(F(1, 2), F(99, 100), F(1, 2), F(4, 5), F(1), 30))

        c2_bad += 1
~~~

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(fs(brp\[2\]), len(\[h for h in hyP if h\[0\] == 'history condition'\])), brp == (0, 0, 0) and not \[h for h in hyP if h\[0\] == 'history condition'\])

~~~text
sec('Q11  Boundary examples (2): protocol antecedents of 19-D, zero points and late reading, the three consolidation gates, certificates and organization, boundaries of the binary clauses')
# ---- Antecedents of Theorem 19-D and the experimental protocol
# Q11a Example X9: θ_M = 2, DAN = 1 in both arms (above the detection limit), MBON on the lower plateau in both arms; Δb = 1/4 but Δ = 0 (D3 fails at the MBON level)
b2_ = (F(1, 2), F(1, 2))
dT2, dS2 = (F(1, 2), F(0)), (F(0), F(0))
cpl = BM.WindowCand('R', 1, -1, b2_, theta_M=2)
mb_in = [sum(bi * (1 - di) for bi, di in zip(b2_, d)) - 2 for d in (dT2, dS2)]
db2 = sum(bi * (dt - ds) for bi, dt, ds in zip(b2_, dT2, dS2))
~~~

check('Q11a D3 (A) must be checked independently at the MBON level: Example X9, θ\_M = 2, DAN in the two arms = %s, %s (visible), MBON input in the two arms %s, %s (lower plateau), Δb = %s but Δ = %s—DAN above the detection limit does not stand in for checking that the MBON is off the plateau' %

~~~text
      (fs(cpl.dan(dT2)), fs(cpl.dan(dS2)), fs(mb_in[0]), fs(mb_in[1]), fs(db2), fs(cpl.delta(dT2, dS2))),
      cpl.dan(dT2) == cpl.dan(dS2) == 1 and all(v <= 0 for v in mb_in) and db2 == F(1, 4) and cpl.delta(dT2, dS2) == 0)
# Q11b Example X10 (nonzero MBON threshold): θ_M = 1/4, g = 1/4, u_min = 1/2, e = 1/32; the three candidates 0, 1/4, −1/8 fall in separated envelopes
resp = [BM.WindowCand(k_, 1, u_, b2_, F(1, 4)).delta(dT2, dS2) for k_, u_ in [('P', 0), ('R', -1), ('R', F(1, 2))]]
m_b, e_b = F(1, 4) * F(1, 2), F(1, 32)
lin_b = all(BM.WindowCand('R', 1, u_, b2_, F(1, 4)).mbon(d) > 0 and BM.WindowCand('R', 1, u_, b2_, F(1, 4)).dan_input(d) > 0
            for u_ in (-1, F(1, 2)) for d in (dT2, dS2))
check('Q11b Example X10 (nonzero MBON threshold): P, R₋, R₊ give %s; m = %s > 2e = %s; envelopes [−e, e], [m − e, ∞), (−∞, −(m − e)] = [−1/32, 1/32], [%s, ∞), (−∞, −%s], pairwise disjoint under |δ| ≤ e; both levels in the linear regime %s' %
      (', '.join(fs(r) for r in resp), fs(m_b), fs(2 * e_b), fs(m_b - e_b), fs(m_b - e_b), lin_b),
      resp == [0, F(1, 4), F(-1, 8)] and m_b > 2 * e_b and resp[1] - e_b >= m_b - e_b and resp[2] + e_b <= -(m_b - e_b) and lin_b)
# Q11c 0 < |u| < u_min is still a return: Δ ≠ 0, but the reading may fall in O_P or the undetermined region (outside the guaranteed region; not P)
weak = []
for u_ in (F(-1, 16), F(-1, 8), F(-1, 4), F(1, 16), F(1, 8)):
    dl = BM.WindowCand('R', 1, u_, b2_, F(1, 4)).delta(dT2, dS2)
    zone = 'P' if abs(dl) <= e_b else ('undetermined' if abs(dl) < m_b - e_b else ('R₋' if dl > 0 else 'R₊'))
    weak.append((fs(u_), fs(dl), zone))
check('Q11c weak return 0 < |u| < u_min = 1/2 (g = 1/4, e = 1/32): (u, Δ, reading zone) = %s—Δ is always nonzero (a return, not P), and the reading may fall in O_P or the undetermined region: this lies outside the guaranteed region, and P is not inferred from the failure of D2' %
      weak, all(F(x[1]) != 0 for x in weak) and any(x[2] == 'P' for x in weak) and any(x[2] == 'undetermined' for x in weak))
# Q11d common-window statistic: in-window depths at all grid points change by a common relaxation, nonnegative weights; P gives 0, R_u gives −u·Σ w_j Δb_j, |Δ̄| ≥ u_min·g
rng = random.Random(19301)
wbad, wn = 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    N_ = rng.choice([2, 3, 4])
    bb = tuple(rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]) for _ in range(N_))
    dS_ = tuple(rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8)]) for _ in range(N_))
    dT_ = tuple(min(F(1), s_ + rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])) for s_ in dS_)
    rr = rng.choice([F(1), F(7, 8), F(3, 4)])
    J = rng.choice([1, 2, 3])
    wts = [F(rng.randrange(1, 4)) for _ in range(J)]
    wts = [x / sum(wts) for x in wts]
    kap = rng.choice([F(1), F(2), F(3)])
    thM = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8)])
    for kind, u_ in [('P', 0), ('R', rng.choice([F(-1), F(-1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]))]:
        C_ = BM.WindowCand(kind, kap, u_, bb, thM)
        gridT = [tuple(d * rr ** j for d in dT_) for j in range(J)]
        gridS = [tuple(d * rr ** j for d in dS_) for j in range(J)]
        lin = all(C_.mbon(d) > 0 and C_.dan_input(d) > 0 for d in gridT + gridS) if kind == 'R' else True
        dbj = [sum(bi * (t - s_) for bi, t, s_ in zip(bb, gT, gS)) for gT, gS in zip(gridT, gridS)]
        if not lin or min(dbj) <= 0:
            continue
        wn += 1
        bar = sum(wj * C_.delta(gT, gS) for wj, gT, gS in zip(wts, gridT, gridS))
        want = 0 if kind == 'P' else -u_ * sum(wj * x for wj, x in zip(wts, dbj))
        if bar != want or (kind == 'R' and abs(bar) < abs(u_) * min(dbj)):
            wbad += 1
check('Q11d common-window statistic (time bins and nonnegative weights chosen in advance, shared by both arms, common relaxation in the window): in %d cases P gives Δ̄ = 0 and R_u gives Δ̄ = −u·Σ w_j Δb_j with |Δ̄| ≥ |u|·min_j Δb_j; mismatches %d' % (wn, wbad),
      wbad == 0 and wn > 100)
# Q11e “no misjudgment” holds only when the error bound holds and the candidate set is covered; m ≤ 2e only fails to guarantee separation and does not prove identical readings
mis_in = []
for dl in resp:
    for k in range(-4, 5):
        obs = dl + e_b * F(k, 4)
        z = 'P' if abs(obs) <= e_b else ('R₋' if obs >= m_b - e_b else ('R₊' if obs <= -(m_b - e_b) else 'undetermined'))
        mis_in.append(z)
true_z = ['P'] * 9 + ['R₋'] * 9 + ['R₊'] * 9
viol = resp[1] - 7 * e_b
z_viol = 'P' if abs(viol) <= e_b else ('R₋' if viol >= m_b - e_b else 'undetermined')
big_e = F(1, 8)
sep_mem = BM.WindowCand('R', 1, -1, b2_, F(1, 4)).delta(dT2, dS2)
~~~

check('Q11e error bound and coverage: 27 readings within the bound (\|δ\| ≤ e), misjudged %d; with the error beyond the bound (δ = −7e), R₋ reads as “%s”—“no misjudgment” holds only when the bound holds; e = 1/8 makes m ≤ 2e (separation not guaranteed), yet this frozen R₋ member still has Δ = %s, different from the 0 of P—m ≤ 2e does not prove identical readings' %

~~~python
      (sum(a != b for a, b in zip(mis_in, true_z)), z_viol, fs(sep_mem)),
      mis_in == true_z and z_viol != 'R₋' and m_b <= 2 * big_e and sep_mem != 0)
# ---- Zero points, first beats and late reading
def mk_host(alpha_rho=0):
    return BM.Graded3F(['g', 'a'], 1, {'A': (0,)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1}, {('g', 'a'): -1},
                       {'g': 1, 'a': 1}, {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 2)}, {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': alpha_rho}, signed={'a': F(1, 2)}, bg=1)
hp_ = mk_host()
s0_ = {'g:0': F(3, 4), 'a:0': F(1, 2)}
rows_ = []
st_ = dict(s0_)
for k in range(2):
    rows_.append((hp_.act(st_, 'A')['MBON_g'], JC.comp_of(hp_, st_, hp_.act(st_, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')))
    st_ = hp_.step(st_, 'rest')
mstar_ = F(1, 2)
kp_ = next(k for k, (m_, W_) in enumerate(rows_) if W_ <= 0)
km_ = next(k for k, (m_, W_) in enumerate(rows_) if W_ < 0)
iv_ = (rows_[kp_ - 1][0], rows_[kp_][0])
# Random members: m(k₊−1) < m* ≤ m(k₊), m(k₋−1) ≤ m* < m(k₋)
rng = random.Random(19302)
ibad, in_ = 0, 0
for trial in range(600):
    eta2 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4)])
    U12 = rng.choice([F(-1, 2), F(-1)])
    th1 = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 4)])
    beta2 = rng.choice([F(2), F(5, 2), F(3)])
    rho1 = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    sg2 = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)])
    Gm = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sgn2=sg2)
    sig = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
    wk_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': sig, 'g1:1': sig})
    ms, Ws = [], []
    for k in range(40):
        ms.append(Gm.act(wk_, 'A')['MBON_g1'])
        Ws.append(JC.total_comp(Gm, wk_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0'))
        wk_ = Gm.step(wk_, 'rest')
    mst = (beta2 - sg2) / (-U12)
    if not all(beta2 + U12 * m_ > 0 for m_ in ms):
        continue
    kp = next((k for k in range(40) if Ws[k] <= 0), None)
    km = next((k for k in range(40) if Ws[k] < 0), None)
    if kp is None or kp == 0:
        continue
    in_ += 1
    ok = ms[kp - 1] < mst <= ms[kp] and (km is None or (km >= kp and ms[km - 1] <= mst < ms[km]))
    ibad += not ok
check('Q11f bracketing interval of P19-2: Example X11, m* = 1/2, (m, W) = (%s, %s), (%s, %s), k₊ = k₋ = %d; only %s < m* < %s is obtained (the zero point is not sampled; m₀ = m(t_flip) is not issued); on %d random members, m(k₊−1) < m* ≤ m(k₊) and m(k₋−1) ≤ m* < m(k₋): mismatches %d' %
      (fs(rows_[0][0]), fs(rows_[0][1]), fs(rows_[1][0]), fs(rows_[1][1]), kp_, fs(iv_[0]), fs(iv_[1]), in_, ibad),
      rows_ == [(F(1, 4), F(1, 8)), (F(5, 8), F(-1, 16))] and kp_ == km_ == 1 and iv_[0] < mstar_ < iv_[1] and all(m_ != mstar_ for m_, _ in rows_) and ibad == 0 and in_ > 50)
# Q11g rounded form of the shift law: k₊(σ) = ⌈ln(σ/s_m)/ln(1/(1 − ρ))⌉, k₊(cσ) − k₊(σ) ∈ {⌊L⌋, ⌈L⌉}; everything decided exactly by rational inequalities
def kp_iter(sig, sm, q):
    k = 0
    while sig * q ** k > sm:
        k += 1
    return k

def ceil_log(r, q):  # min{n ≥ 0 : q^{-n} ≥ r}, i.e. ⌈ln r / ln(1/q)⌉ (r ≥ 1)
    n = 0
    while (1 / q) ** n < r:
        n += 1
    return n

def floor_log(r, q):  # max{n ≥ 0 : q^{-n} ≤ r}
    n = 0
    while (1 / q) ** (n + 1) <= r:
        n += 1
    return n

rev_g = [kp_iter(s_, F(1, 4), F(1, 4)) for s_ in (F(1, 2), F(1))]
tbad, tn = 0, 0
for rho_ in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
    q_ = 1 - rho_
    for sm in (F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8)):
        for sig in (F(k, 16) for k in range(1, 17)):
            if not sig > sm:
                continue
            for c_ in (F(5, 4), F(3, 2), F(2), F(3), F(4)):
                if c_ * sig > 1:
                    continue
                tn += 1
                k1, k2 = kp_iter(sig, sm, q_), kp_iter(c_ * sig, sm, q_)
                if k1 != ceil_log(sig / sm, q_) or (k2 - k1) not in (floor_log(c_, q_), ceil_log(c_, q_)):
                    tbad += 1
# Model cross-check: k₊ of signed two_comp against the rounded formula with s_m = 1 − (m* + θ_γ)/n_A
mb_bad = 0
~~~

for (eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sg2) in \[(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(5, 2), F(1, 4), F(1, 8), F(1)), (F(1, 8), F(-1), F(2), F(0), F(1, 4), F(1)),

~~~python
                                            (F(1, 4), F(-1), F(3), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 2))]:
    Gm = two_comp(eta2, U12, beta2, th1, rho1, sgn2=sg2)
    mst = (beta2 - sg2) / (-U12)
    sm = 1 - (mst + th1) / 2
    for sig in (F(3, 4), F(1)):
        wk_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': sig, 'g1:1': sig})
        kk = 0
        while JC.total_comp(Gm, wk_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0') > 0 and kk < 500:
            wk_ = Gm.step(wk_, 'rest')
            kk += 1
        mb_bad += not (0 < sm < 1) or kk != (0 if sig <= sm else ceil_log(sig / sm, 1 - rho1))
check('Q11g rounded form of Corollary 19-7″: Example X12, ρ = 3/4, s_m = 1/4: first-arrival beats for σ = 1/2 and 1 are %s (L = 1/2 < 1 beat; equal delays still conform); on %d groups (ρ, s_m, σ, c), k₊ = ⌈x⌉ and difference ∈ {⌊L⌋, ⌈L⌉}: mismatches %d; on signed two_comp, model first arrival against the rounded formula: mismatches %d' %
      (rev_g, tn, tbad, mb_bad), rev_g == [1, 1] and tbad == 0 and tn > 100 and mb_bad == 0)
# Q11h tail-proof condition, and “staying at the zero” versus “tending to zero from the positive side”
Gt = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(3), F(0), F(1, 8), sgn2=F(1))   # m* = 2 = MBON upper bound (n_A = 2, θ_γ = 0), s_m = 0
def Wseq(Gm, sig, n):
    wk_ = at(Gm, {'g1:0': sig, 'g1:1': sig})
    out = []
    for _ in range(n):
        out.append(JC.total_comp(Gm, wk_, 'A', 'W_a3', 'a3:0'))
        wk_ = Gm.step(wk_, 'rest')
    return out
pos_tail = Wseq(Gt, F(1, 2), 60)
G1r = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(3), F(0), F(1), sgn2=F(1))     # ρ_γ = 1
G0z = two_comp(F(1, 4), F(-1), F(3), F(0), F(0), sgn2=F(3, 2))  # ρ_γ = 0, m* = 3/2, s_m = 1/4
zero_line = Wseq(G0z, F(1, 4), 60)
~~~

check('Q11h tail-proof condition of Theorem 19-7′ (C): with s\_m = 0, σ = 1/2, 0 \< ρ\_γ \< 1, W stays \> 0 and strictly decreases within 60 beats (tends to zero from the positive side; recording k₊ = ∞ requires the tail proof σ(1 − ρ)^k \> 0) %s; with σ = 0, first-beat W = %s (k₊ = 0); with ρ\_γ = 1, W at beat 1 = %s (finite); with ρ\_γ = 0, σ = s\_m, W is always 0 (staying at the zero: k₊ = 0, k₋ = ∞) %s' %

~~~text
      (all(x > 0 for x in pos_tail) and all(a_ > b_ for a_, b_ in zip(pos_tail, pos_tail[1:])), fs(Wseq(Gt, F(0), 1)[0]), fs(Wseq(G1r, F(1, 2), 2)[1]), all(x == 0 for x in zero_line)),
      all(x > 0 for x in pos_tail) and all(a_ > b_ for a_, b_ in zip(pos_tail, pos_tail[1:])) and Wseq(Gt, F(0), 1)[0] == 0 and Wseq(G1r, F(1, 2), 2)[1] == 0 and all(x == 0 for x in zero_line))
# Q11i propagation of the difference to a late reading: Example X13, one quiet relaxation beat takes 1/8 → 1/16; general formula Σ_s W_s∏_{j>s} r_j (no clipping); when clipping is hit, the actual transition must be used
dec_ = mk_host(alpha_rho=F(1, 2))
e1, c1 = dec_.step(s0_, 'A'), dec_.step(s0_, 'rest')
gap0 = e1['a:0'] - c1['a:0']
gap1 = dec_.step(e1, 'rest')['a:0'] - dec_.step(c1, 'rest')['a:0']
rng = random.Random(19303)
pbad, pn, pclamp = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(200):
    ra = rng.choice([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    Gd = BM.Graded3F(['g', 'a'], 1, {'A': (0,)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1}, {('g', 'a'): -1},
                     {'g': 1, 'a': 1}, {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 8)}, {'g': rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]), 'a': ra}, signed={'a': F(1, 2)})
    st0 = {'g:0': rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4)]), 'a:0': F(1, 2)}
    L_ = 6
    tests = sorted(rng.sample(range(L_), rng.choice([1, 2])))
    seqT = ['A' if j in tests else 'rest' for j in range(L_)]
    wT, wN = dict(st0), dict(st0)
    Ws_ = {}
    clamped = False
    for j, sgl in enumerate(seqT):
        if sgl == 'A':
            Ws_[j] = JC.comp_of(Gd, wT, Gd.act(wT, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
            raw = wT['a:0'] + Ws_[j] - ra * wT['a:0']
            clamped = clamped or not (0 <= raw <= 1)
        wT, wN = Gd.step(wT, sgl), Gd.step(wN, 'rest')
    if clamped:
        pclamp += 1
        continue
    pred = sum(Ws_[j] * (1 - ra) ** (L_ - 1 - j) for j in Ws_)
    pn += 1
    pbad += (wT['a:0'] - wN['a:0']) != pred
Gc = BM.Graded3F(['g', 'a'], 1, {'A': (0,)}, {'g': 0, 'a': 0}, {'g': 0, 'a': 1}, {('g', 'a'): -1},
                 {'g': 1, 'a': 1}, {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 2)}, {'g': F(1, 2), 'a': F(1, 2)}, signed={'a': F(1, 2)})
stc = {'g:0': F(0), 'a:0': F(1, 32)}
Wc = JC.comp_of(Gc, stc, Gc.act(stc, 'A'), 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
gapc = Gc.step(Gc.step(stc, 'A'), 'rest')['a:0'] - Gc.step(Gc.step(stc, 'rest'), 'rest')['a:0']
~~~

~~~text
      (fs(gap0), fs(gap1), pn, pbad, pclamp, fs(Wc), fs(gapc), fs(Wc * F(1, 2))),

# ---- Theorem 19-10: the DAN gate, tag gate and settability gate as separate layers
crev = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=1, u=F(1, 2), theta_c=F(1, 2), tag=F(3, 4))
srev = dict(crev.init, **{'a:0': F(1, 2), 'a:1': F(1, 2)})
tag_case = (crev.act(srev, 'bg')['DAN_w'], crev.step(srev, 'bg')['c:0'])
thm_wc = BM.WindowCand('R', F(1, 4), 1, (F(1, 4),) * 4, F(1, 2)).dan((1, 1, 0, 0))
thm_cs = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=F(1, 4), u=F(1), bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(1, 2), theta_M=F(1, 2))
thm_cd = thm_cs.act(at(thm_cs, {'a:0': F(1), 'a:1': F(1)}), 'bg')['DAN_w']
old_floor = F(1, 4) + F(1) * F(1, 4) * (4 - 2)
# Example X5 under two constructions: K set deep and all set deep
c1r = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=F(1), u=F(1, 2), bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(1, 2))
dan_K = c1r.act(at(c1r, {'a:0': F(1), 'a:1': F(1)}), 'bg')['DAN_w']
dan_all = c1r.act({x: F(x.startswith('a:')) for x in c1r.X}, 'bg')['DAN_w']
# Complete equivalence for the setting set (K set deep, one beat, c = 0): CONS(D) ⟺ D > τ_tag ∧ (κ ≥ θ_c ∨ D ≤ D_c)
jbad, jn, jex = 0, 0, 0
for kap in (F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
    for uu in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
        for bgv in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2)):
            for thM in (F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)):
                for th in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
                    for tg in (F(1, 20), F(1, 4), F(3, 4)):
                        Cm = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=kap, u=uu, bg=bgv, theta_c=th, tag=tg, theta_M=thM)
                        Dc = (kap + uu * (bgv * 4 - thM) - th) / (uu * bgv * 2)
                        Ds = sorted(set([F(k, 16) for k in range(17)] + ([Dc] if 0 <= Dc <= 1 else []) + ([tg] if tg <= 1 else [])))
                        got_set = []
                        for D in Ds:
                            jn += 1
                            got = JC.total_comp(Cm, at(Cm, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), 'bg', 'CONS', 'c:0') == 1
                            want = D > tg and (kap >= th or D <= Dc)
                            jbad += got != want
                            got_set.append(got)
                        Y1 = Cm.act(at(Cm, {'a:0': F(1), 'a:1': F(1)}), 'bg')['DAN_w']
                        exists = any((D > tg) and not g_ for D, g_ in zip(Ds, got_set))
                        jex += exists != (tg < 1 and Y1 < th)
check('Q11j setting set of Theorem 19-10 (4): Example X14 (tag gate) at D = 1/2: DAN_w = %s ≥ θ_c but the tag gate fails, c′ = %s; Example X15 (θ_M) at D = 1: true DAN_w = %s (WindowCand), %s (Consol, θ_M = 1/2), while the lower-bound formula that omits θ_M and the non-plateau domain gives %s; Example X5 at D = 1: K set deep DAN_w = %s, all set deep DAN_w = %s (the two constructions labeled separately); '
      'on %d points (parameters × depths, including the endpoints D_c and τ_tag), “CONS ⟺ D > τ_tag ∧ (κ ≥ θ_c ∨ D ≤ D_c)”: mismatches %d; “some depth above the tag threshold is not consolidated ⟺ τ_tag < 1 ∧ Y(1) < θ_c”: mismatches %d' %
~~~

~~~python
# Q11l window-end retention (Theorem 19-10 (6)): the first setting beat includes the tag gate; one more beat of L need not add a sawtooth; when setting affects no relaxation beat there is no strict jump down
ce = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=F(0), u=F(1, 2), theta_c=F(3, 8), tag=F(1, 4))
Dc_e = (ce.kappa + ce.u * (ce.bgv * 4 - ce.theta_M) - ce.theta_c) / (ce.u * ce.bgv * 2)
def ltm_pred(Cm, D, L, Np, Dc):
    t = next((j for j in range(L) if Cm.tag < D * Cm.rho0 ** j <= Dc), None)
    return D * Cm.rho0 ** (L + Np) if t is None else D * Cm.rho0 ** (t + 1) * Cm.rho1 ** (L - 1 - t + Np)
lbad = 0
jumps = {}
Dgrid = [F(k, 32) for k in range(1, 33)]
for L in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
    vals = []
    for D in Dgrid:
        got = retain(ce, at(ce, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), L, 10)
        lbad += got != ltm_pred(ce, D, L, 10, Dc_e)
        vals.append(got)
    jumps[L] = sum(1 for a_, b_ in zip(vals, vals[1:]) if b_ < a_)
lastbeat = (retain(ce, at(ce, {'a:0': F(1, 2), 'a:1': F(1, 2)}), 1, 0), F(1, 2) * ce.rho0)
~~~

~~~python
# Q11v 19-H (4): both arms trained first, pre-beat history kept, witnesses restricted to the post-training evidence window
def cert_window(host, hist, T, k0):
    states = JC.run(host, host.init, hist)
    for k in range(k0, len(hist)):
        sub_states, sub_sigs = states[:k + 1], hist[:k + 1]
        Uav = JC.avail_at(host, states, k, T, EPS)
        if not Uav:
            continue
        w = states[k]
        s = hist[k]
        y = host.act(w, s)
        for (p, x, _c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T or abs(JC.comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)) <= EPS:
                continue
            st, _wt = JC.hc_status(host, w, s, p, x, Uav, EPS)
            if st == 'effective difference certified':
                return '1', (k, s, p)
    return 'not certified', None
hv_ = ['A+', 'rest', 'A', 'rest']
cv_r = cert_window(G0r, hv_, T_, 1)
cv_n = cert_window(G0n, hv_, T_, 1)
~~~

~~~python
# ---- Boundaries of the binary clauses
# Q11q Lemma 19-2a (3): s′ = clip((1 − ρ)s + dm, 0, hi); cross-check against k17_core (including negative dm, lower clipping, D not firing)
def kc_member(Ap, Am, rho1, cD=F(1, 16), thM=F(3, 4), thD=F(1, 2), u=F(-1)):
    b_ = {(i, j): v for j, v in {0: F(1, 2), 1: cD, 2: F(0)}.items() for i in range(4)}
    return KC.Member(4, ['M', 'D', 'Z'], [0, 1, 2], {(0, 1): u}, gp=[1, 2, 2], gm=[1, 2, 2], theta=[thM, thD, 1],
                     lam=[0, 0, 0], vres=[0, 0, 0], dx=[0] * 4, dy=[0] * 3, Ap=Ap, Am=Am, rho={(i, 0): rho1 for i in range(4)},
                     lo=0, hi=F(1, 2), b=b_, pat=KM.PAT, name='K_C')
rng = random.Random(19304)
qbad, qn, qneg = 0, 0, 0
for trial in range(300):
    Ap_, Am_ = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)]), rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8)])
    r1_ = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 8), F(1, 2)])
    Mq = kc_member(Ap_, Am_, r1_)
    st = Mq.init_state()
    sv = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)])
    for i in (0, 1):
        st[0][(i, 0)] = F(1, 2) - sv
    J = rng.choice([F(0), F(3, 2)])
    st2, g, vt, ev = KC.step(Mq, st, 'A', [F(0), J, F(0)])
    s_new = F(1, 2) - st2[0][(0, 0)]
    dm = Am_ - Ap_
    want = min(F(1, 2), max(F(0), (1 - r1_) * sv + dm)) if g[1] else (1 - r1_) * sv
    qn += 1
    qbad += s_new != want
    qneg += bool(g[1]) and (1 - r1_) * sv + dm < 0
Mneg = kc_member(F(1, 4), F(1, 8), F(0))
stn = Mneg.init_state()
stn2, gn, _, _ = KC.step(Mneg, stn, 'A', [F(0), F(3, 2), F(0)])
~~~

~~~python
# Q11t Theorem 19-7 (2)(5): strict weakening of a signed late s₂ > 0 requires A₊ > 0 or ρ₂ > 0; after blockade, relaxation can still lower s₂
def s2_after(M, s1, s2):
    st = M.init_state()
    for i in (0, 1):
        st[0][(i, 0)] = F(1, 2) - s1
        st[0][(i, 2)] = F(1, 2) - s2
    st2, g, _, _ = KC.step(M, st, 'A', [F(0)] * M.n_out)
    return F(1, 2) - st2[0][(0, 2)]

z1 = s2_after(KM.gamma_alpha3(signed=True, Ap=F(1, 16), rho2=F(0)), F(0), F(1, 4))
z2 = s2_after(KM.gamma_alpha3(signed=True, Ap=F(0), rho2=F(1, 8)), F(0), F(1, 4))
zb = s2_after(KM.gamma_alpha3(signed=True, rho2=F(1, 2), block=True), F(0), F(1, 2))
~~~

~~~python
# ======================================================================
sec('Q12  Boundary examples (3): timing and detection, the constant branch, branch comparison')
# ---- T5, P19-1/2/5/14: timing, sign and detection
h12 = mk_host()
def mw(host, w):
    y = host.act(w, 'A')
    return y['MBON_g'], JC.comp_of(host, w, y, 'A', 'W_a', 'a:0')
early12 = {'g:0': F(3, 4), 'a:0': F(1, 2)}
late12 = h12.step(h12.step(early12, 'A'), 'rest')
thr12 = {'g:0': F(1, 2), 'a:0': F(1, 2)}
def tdiff(host, w0, pre, post):  # test arm (one A after pre beats) minus untested arm: difference in final α depth
    wT, wN = dict(w0), dict(w0)
    for _ in range(pre):
        wT, wN = host.step(wT, 'rest'), host.step(wN, 'rest')
    wT, wN = host.step(wT, 'A'), host.step(wN, 'rest')
    for _ in range(post):
        wT, wN = host.step(wT, 'rest'), host.step(wN, 'rest')
    return wT['a:0'] - wN['a:0']
d_thr = tdiff(h12, thr12, 0, 3)
~~~

check('Q12a timing and zero point: Example X18, early reading (m, W) = (%s, %s); at the write beat after that probe and one rest beat, (%s, %s)—the signs are opposite, so the regressor must be the pre-beat m\_γ1 of the re-exposure beat (read synchronously or propagated through a calibrated map); exactly at the threshold (m, W) = (%s, %s), late-read depth difference, tested minus untested, %s—exactly at the zero point the difference can be zero' %

~~~text
      mw(h12, early12) == (F(1, 4), F(1, 8)) and mw(h12, late12) == (F(13, 16), F(-5, 32)) and mw(h12, thr12) == (F(1, 2), 0) and d_thr == 0)

hcl = mk_host()
wcl = {'g:0': F(0), 'a:0': F(0)}
Wcl = mw(hcl, wcl)[1]
d_cl = tdiff(hcl, wcl, 0, 2)
hds = mk_host(alpha_rho=F(1))
wds = {'g:0': F(3, 4), 'a:0': F(1, 2)}
Wds = mw(hds, wds)[1]
d_ds = tdiff(hds, wds, 0, 1)
~~~

check('Q12b other sources of no difference: with α at the lower end, the same-beat component W = %s is cut off by clipping, late-read difference %s; with α fully dissipating (ρ\_α = 1), same-beat component W = %s, late-read difference one beat later %s—“no difference means breach” holds only when the absolute lower bound of the predicted effect exceeds the detection and control errors' %

~~~python
      (fs(Wcl), fs(d_cl), fs(Wds), fs(d_ds)), Wcl < -EPS and d_cl == 0 and abs(Wds) > EPS and d_ds == 0)
# ---- The constant Y₀ = Q(κ + u·R(−θ_M)) of Theorem 19-10 (1)
cst = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=0, u=1, bg=0, theta_M=-1, theta_c=F(1, 2))
act_c = dict(cst.init, **{'a:0': F(1, 2), 'a:1': F(1, 2)})
c_dan, c_set = cst.act(act_c, 'bg')['DAN_w'], cst.step(act_c, 'bg')['c:0']
cbad, cn, cdiff = 0, 0, 0
for thM in (F(-1), F(-1, 2), F(0), F(1, 2)):
    for uu in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)):
        for kap in (F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2)):
            for th in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
                Cm = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=kap, u=uu, bg=0, theta_M=thM, theta_c=th)
                Y0 = max(F(0), kap + uu * max(F(0), -thM))
                for D in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
                    w_ = at(Cm, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D})
                    got = JC.total_comp(Cm, w_, 'bg', 'CONS', 'c:0') == 1
                    cn += 1
                    cbad += got != (Y0 >= th and D > Cm.tag)
                    cdiff += (Y0 >= th) != (max(F(0), kap) >= th)
                    if D == F(1, 2):
                        A_c = JC.branches(Cm, w_, JC.setT(Cm, w_, ['a:0', 'a:1']), 'bg', 'CONS', 'c:0')[0]
                        cbad += A_c != 0
check('Q12c constant branch of Theorem 19-10 (1): Example X19 with b = 0, u = 1, θ_M = −1, κ = 0, θ_c = 1/2: actual DAN_w = %s, CONS set %s, whereas Q(κ) = 0 would give not set; over %d cases (the constant branch u·b = 0, including the permissive u = 0), “CONS ⟺ Y₀ ≥ θ_c ∧ tag” and A = 0: mismatches %d; Y₀ and Q(κ) give different gate decisions in %d of them' %
      (fs(c_dan), c_set, cn, cbad, cdiff), c_dan == 1 and c_set == 1 and cbad == 0 and cdiff > 0)
# ---- P19-12: branch comparison by t(D)
p12 = dict(kappa=0, u=1, bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(3, 4), tag=F(1, 4), rho1=F(9, 10))
c12 = BM.Consol('exc', rho0=F(1, 2), **p12)
r12 = [retain(c12, at(c12, {'a:0': d, 'a:1': d}), 1, 1) for d in (F(1, 4), F(13, 50), F(1, 2), F(51, 100))]
z12 = BM.Consol('exc', rho0=0, **p12)
z_set = z12.step(at(z12, {'a:0': F(1, 2), 'a:1': F(1, 2)}), 'bg')['c:0']
z_all = all(retain(z12, at(z12, {'a:0': F(i, 16), 'a:1': F(i, 16)}), 1, 1) == 0 for i in range(17))
check('Q12d Example X20 (three groups) (ρ₀ = 1/2, ρ₁ = 9/10, τ_tag = 1/4, D_c = 1/2, L = 1, N_post = 1): D = 1/4 → 13/50, retention %s → %s (jump up across the tag gate); D = 1/2 → 51/100, retention %s → %s (jump down across the DAN upper limit); with ρ₀ = 0, first-beat setting %s while the late reads at all 17 depths are 0 %s' %
      (fs(r12[0]), fs(r12[1]), fs(r12[2]), fs(r12[3]), z_set, z_all),
      r12 == [F(1, 16), F(117, 1000), F(9, 40), F(51, 400)] and z_set == 1 and z_all)
def t_of(Cm, D, L, Dc):
    return next((j for j in range(L) if Cm.tag < D * Cm.rho0 ** j <= Dc), None)
ebad, eup, edown, en = 0, 0, 0, 0
for r0 in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
    for r1 in (F(9, 10), F(1)):
        for tg in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4)):
            Cm = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=0, u=1, bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(3, 4), tag=tg, rho0=r0, rho1=r1)
            Dc = (Cm.kappa + Cm.u * (Cm.bgv * 4 - Cm.theta_M) - Cm.theta_c) / (Cm.u * Cm.bgv * 2)
            for L in (1, 2, 3):
                for Np in (1, 3):
                    Ds = [F(k, 64) for k in range(1, 65)]
                    vals = [retain(Cm, at(Cm, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), L, Np) for D in Ds]
                    ts = [t_of(Cm, D, L, Dc) for D in Ds]
                    for (D1, v1, t1), (D2, v2, t2) in zip(zip(Ds, vals, ts), list(zip(Ds, vals, ts))[1:]):
                        en += 1
                        if t1 == t2:
                            ebad += not v2 > v1          # strictly increasing within the same branch (ρ₀ > 0)
                        else:
                            pred = ltm_pred(Cm, D2, L, Np, Dc) - ltm_pred(Cm, D1, L, Np, Dc)
                            later = (t2 is None) or (t1 is not None and t2 > t1)
                            ebad += (v2 - v1) != pred
                            eup += v2 > v1
                            edown += v2 < v1
                            ebad += later and not v2 < v1   # on the fine grid, crossing to a later setting or to no setting: jump down
check('Q12e Theorem 19-10 (6), branch comparison (%d adjacent depth pairs; finite generated domain 0 < ρ₀ < ρ₁ ≤ 1, several τ_tag, L, N_post): strict increase within the same t(D) branch, the difference across a boundary equal to the difference of the closed forms of the left and right branches, a jump down when crossing to a later setting or to no setting: mismatches %d; %d jumps up and %d jumps down across boundaries—they are not uniformly jumps down' %
      (en, ebad, eup, edown), ebad == 0 and eup > 0 and edown > 0)
# Unified ratio formula: H = L + N_post; if set at beat t, h(D) = H − 1 − t; if not set, h(D) = 0; LTM(D) = D·ρ₀^{H−h(D)}·ρ₁^{h(D)};
#           LTM(D₂)/LTM(D₁) = (D₂/D₁)·(ρ₁/ρ₀)^{h(D₂)−h(D₁)}; temporal order by itself does not guarantee a jump down on a pair of finite depths
def h_of(Cm, D, L, Np, Dc):
    t = t_of(Cm, D, L, Dc)
    return 0 if t is None else L + Np - 1 - t
fbad, fn_ = 0, 0
for r0 in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
    for tg in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4)):
        Cm = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=0, u=1, bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(3, 4), tag=tg, rho0=r0, rho1=F(9, 10))
        Dc = (Cm.kappa + Cm.u * (Cm.bgv * 4 - Cm.theta_M) - Cm.theta_c) / (Cm.u * Cm.bgv * 2)
        for L in (1, 2, 3):
            for Np in (1, 2):
                H = L + Np
                Ds = [F(k, 32) for k in range(1, 33)]
                lt = {D: retain(Cm, at(Cm, {'a:0': D, 'a:1': D}), L, Np) for D in Ds}
                for D in Ds:
                    fn_ += 1
                    hD = h_of(Cm, D, L, Np, Dc)
                    fbad += lt[D] != D * Cm.rho0 ** (H - hD) * Cm.rho1 ** hD
                for D1, D2 in zip(Ds, Ds[3:]):
                    fbad += lt[D2] / lt[D1] != (D2 / D1) * (Cm.rho1 / Cm.rho0) ** (h_of(Cm, D2, L, Np, Dc) - h_of(Cm, D1, L, Np, Dc))
cf = BM.Consol('exc', kappa=0, u=1, bg=F(1, 4), theta_c=F(3, 4), tag=F(1, 8), rho0=F(3, 4), rho1=F(9, 10))
Dcf = F(1, 2)
D1f, D2f = F(1, 4), F(3, 5)
pair = (t_of(cf, D1f, 2, Dcf), t_of(cf, D2f, 2, Dcf), retain(cf, at(cf, {'a:0': D1f, 'a:1': D1f}), 2, 1), retain(cf, at(cf, {'a:0': D2f, 'a:1': D2f}), 2, 1))
~~~
